1: """Store command — fetch store data for inspection.
2: 
3: Pure data fetch, no rendering knowledge.
4: """
5: from __future__ import annotations
6: 
7: import sys
8: from functools import partial
9: from pathlib import Path
10: from typing import Any
11: 
12: 
13: def _bucket_timestamps(timestamps: list[float], width: int) -> list[float]:
14:     """Bucket timestamps into equal-width time bins, return counts per bin.
15: 
16:     *timestamps* should be sorted newest-first (as returned by
17:     ``StoreReader.tick_timestamps``).  Returns *width* floats — one
18:     count per bin, ordered oldest→newest so the visual reads left-to-right.
19:     """
20:     if not timestamps or width <= 0:
21:         return []
22:     lo, hi = min(timestamps), max(timestamps)
23:     if lo == hi:
24:         # All ticks at same instant — single spike in the middle
25:         buckets = [0.0] * width
26:         buckets[width // 2] = float(len(timestamps))
27:         return buckets
28:     span = hi - lo
29:     buckets = [0.0] * width
30:     for ts in timestamps:
31:         idx = int((ts - lo) / span * (width - 1))
32:         idx = max(0, min(idx, width - 1))
33:         buckets[idx] += 1.0
34:     return buckets
35: 
36: 
37: def _refuse_store(msg: str, *, label: str | None = None) -> int:
38:     """Render a refusal to stderr and return the refusal exit code (2).
39: 
40:     A refusal is a store saying "this operation is not available here",
41:     which is neither success nor a crash — every refusing path in this
42:     module renders it the same way so the exit code cannot drift between
43:     subcommands.
44:     """
45:     from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
46: 
47:     lines = msg.splitlines() or [""]
48:     head = f"✗ {label}: {lines[0]}" if label else f"✗ {lines[0]}"
49:     blocks = [Block.text(head, Style())]
50:     for line in lines[1:]:
51:         blocks.append(Block.text(line, Style()))
52:     paint(join_vertical(*blocks), file=sys.stderr)
53:     return 2
54: 
55: 
56: def resolve_store_path(file_path: Path) -> Path:
57:     """Resolve a .vertex or .db file to the actual store .db path.
58: 
59:     The read-side composition of the write-side resolution: the canonical
60:     artifact, then its index. A JSONL-canonical vertex (``store "….jsonl"``)
61:     resolves to its derived sqlite index — see ``engine.residence``.
62: 
63:     Resolution *materializes*: ``ensure_index`` builds the index a fresh
64:     clone (log tracked, derived ``.db`` untracked) does not have, so the read
65:     reports the store's contents rather than an unmaterialized store. With no
66:     log to build from, the index stays absent and the not-yet-materialized
67:     contract below still speaks.
68: 
69:     Deriving it from :func:`resolve_canonical_path` rather than resolving
70:     independently keeps one answer to "which artifact does this target name":
71:     two resolvers that can disagree is how a writer and a reader end up on
72:     different files.
73:     """
74:     from engine.jsonl_store import ensure_index
75: 
76:     return ensure_index(resolve_canonical_path(file_path))
77: 
78: 
79: def resolve_canonical_path(file_path: Path) -> Path:
80:     """Resolve a ``.vertex`` (or ``.db``) to its **canonical** store artifact.
81: 
82:     The write-side sibling of :func:`resolve_store_path`. A JSONL-canonical
83:     vertex resolves to its ``….jsonl`` log; everything else to the same
84:     sqlite path :func:`resolve_store_path` returns. Feed it to
85:     ``open_canonical_store`` — constructing ``SqliteStore`` on the resolved
86:     *index* of a JSONL-canonical vertex writes rows the log never sees, and
87:     the ``JsonlCanonicalUnsupported`` refusals on the history-mutating ops
88:     never fire because the subclass is never instantiated.
89: 
90:     A direct ``.db`` target stays itself: the documented direct-db bypass is
91:     detected on the next open, not prevented here.
92:     """
93:     if file_path.suffix == ".vertex":
94:         from engine.residence import canonical_store_path
95:         from lang import parse_vertex_file
96: 
97:         ast = parse_vertex_file(file_path)
98:         if ast.store is None:
99:             raise ValueError(f"No store configured in {file_path}")
100:         return canonical_store_path(ast.store, file_path)
101:     elif file_path.suffix == ".db":
102:         return file_path.resolve()
103:     else:
104:         raise ValueError(f"Expected .vertex or .db file, got {file_path.suffix}")
105: 
106: 
107: def _require_materialized_store(target_path: Path) -> Path:
108:     """Resolve *target_path* to its store ``.db`` and require it exists.
109: 
110:     State 2 of the store-verb existence contract (decision/design/
111:     store-verb-existence-exit-code-parity): a present ``.vertex`` whose store
112:     ``.db`` was never written is *not yet materialized* — a distinct condition
113:     from *written and empty* (write-receipt-vs-temporal-query at the exit-code
114:     layer). All three store verbs (ticks/stats/verify) surface it as a clean
115:     RC=1 with a named message rather than ticks' prior silent RC=0-empty.
116:     """
117:     db_path = resolve_store_path(target_path)
118:     if not db_path.exists():
119:         raise FileNotFoundError(
120:             f"store for '{target_path.stem}' not yet materialized — "
121:             f"no facts emitted (no database at {db_path})"
122:         )
123:     return db_path
124: 
125: 
126: def canonical_agreement(target_path: Path, *, deep: bool = False):
127:     """Judge a log-canonical target's index against its log — or ``None``.
128: 
129:     The gate every store read verb runs BEFORE it reads (design/store/
130:     verify-canonical-agreement). A ``.db``-canonical target has one artifact
131:     and nothing to disagree with, so it returns ``None`` and the caller's
132:     behavior is unchanged.
133: 
134:     Deliberately resolved through :func:`resolve_canonical_path` and
135:     ``index_path_for`` — both pure — rather than :func:`resolve_store_path`.
136:     That path runs ``ensure_index`` / ``ensure_arrival_index``, which
137:     constructs a store whenever the index is behind, and a store constructor
138:     *repairs*: it would catch the index up and then report agreement about a
139:     store whose disagreement it had just erased. Verification and open-time
140:     recovery are opposite contracts; this is the seam where they must not meet.
141: 
142:     Returns ``(index_path, report)``. A non-materialized log raises the same
143:     ``FileNotFoundError`` as :func:`_require_materialized_store`.
144:     """
145:     from engine.canonical_audit import audit_agreement, audit_deep
146:     from engine.residence import canonical_mode, index_path_for
147: 
148:     canonical = resolve_canonical_path(target_path)
149:     mode = canonical_mode(canonical)
150:     if mode not in ("jsonl", "arrival"):
151:         return None
152:     if not canonical.exists():
153:         raise FileNotFoundError(
154:             f"store for '{target_path.stem}' not yet materialized — "
155:             f"no facts emitted (no canonical log at {canonical})"
156:         )
157:     index = index_path_for(canonical)
158:     if not index.exists():
159:         # Fresh clone: the log is tracked, the derived index is not. BUILDING
160:         # an absent index from the log is not repair — there is no prior state
161:         # to destroy and no divergence it could hide, so the store-verb
162:         # existence contract keeps working. An index that DOES exist is
163:         # evidence and is never materialized through here.
164:         if mode == "arrival":
165:             from engine.arrival_store import ensure_arrival_index
166: 
167:             ensure_arrival_index(canonical)
168:         else:
169:             from engine.jsonl_store import ensure_index
170: 
171:             ensure_index(canonical)
172:     audit = audit_deep if deep else audit_agreement
173:     return index, audit(canonical)
174: 
175: 
176: def _gate_read(target_path: Path) -> None:
177:     """Refuse a read verb on a store whose index is known to be poisoned.
178: 
179:     ``stats``/``ticks`` render store-level claims — totals, chain counts —
180:     straight off the derived index. Serving those from an index that provably
181:     disagrees with the canonical log is the same false attestation ``verify``
182:     was rendering, one surface over. Raised as ``ValueError`` so each verb's
183:     existing fetch-error path renders it at RC=1.
184:     """
185:     gated = canonical_agreement(target_path)
186:     if gated is None or gated[1].ok:
187:         return
188:     report = gated[1]
189:     if report.index_behind:
190:         # Every divergence sits past the consumed prefix. That is the shape an
191:         # interrupted append leaves, and the product already holds its repair:
192:         # any read verb catches the index up. It is ALSO the shape a rewound
193:         # marker plus a doctored suffix leaves, which L1 cannot tell apart —
194:         # so name the state and both exits, and never call it innocent.
195:         raise ValueError(
196:             f"index behind the log in '{target_path.stem}' — "
197:             f"{report.summary()}. The totals here would undercount. Catch the "
198:             f"index up with 'loops read {target_path.stem}', or rule out "
199:             f"tampering with 'loops store verify --deep', then re-run"
200:         )
201:     raise ValueError(
202:         f"canonical disagreement in '{target_path.stem}' — "
203:         f"{report.summary()}. Refusing to serve derived data the log "
204:         f"does not support; run 'loops store verify' for the full report"
205:     )
206: 
207: 
208: def make_fetcher(path: Path, zoom: int, *, kind: str | None = None):
209:     """Create a zero-arg fetcher for store data.
210: 
211:     zoom controls enrichment depth:
212:       0:   summary only (counts + stats)
213:       1:   + sparkline + payload_keys per tick
214:       2:   + latest tick payloads
215:       3:   + recent fact payloads
216: 
217:     ``kind``, when given, is the explicit ``--kind`` escape hatch (SPEC
218:     §9.4): the reserved ``_decl.*`` namespace is excluded from
219:     ``facts.kinds`` by default, but an explicit ``--kind`` ask narrows the
220:     listing to that one kind and includes it regardless of namespace —
221:     an explicit request overrides the ambient default, same rule as every
222:     other read surface this task touches.
223:     """
224:     def fetch() -> dict:
225:         from engine.store_reader import StoreReader
226: 
227:         from ..lenses._statview import spark
228: 
229:         store_path = resolve_store_path(path)
230:         with StoreReader(store_path) as reader:
231:             data = reader.summary(include_internal=kind is not None)
232:             if kind is not None:
233:                 data["facts"]["kinds"] = {
234:                     k: v for k, v in data["facts"]["kinds"].items() if k == kind
235:                 }
236:             data["freshness"] = reader.freshness
237:             if zoom >= 1:
238:                 # Sparkline + payload keys per tick name
239:                 for name, info in data["ticks"]["names"].items():
240:                     ts_list = reader.tick_timestamps(name, 50)
241:                     buckets = _bucket_timestamps(ts_list, 8)
242:                     info["sparkline"] = spark(buckets)
243:                     # Extract payload key names from latest tick
244:                     recent = reader.recent_ticks(name, 1)
245:                     info["payload_keys"] = (
246:                         list(recent[0].payload.keys()) if recent else []
247:                     )
248:                 # Keep sample payload per fact kind for SUMMARY gist
249:                 for fkind, info in data["facts"]["kinds"].items():
250:                     recent = reader.recent_facts(fkind, 1)
251:                     if recent:
252:                         info["sample_payload"] = recent[0]["payload"]
253:             if zoom >= 2:
254:                 for name, info in data["ticks"]["names"].items():
255:                     recent = reader.recent_ticks(name, 3)
256:                     if recent:
257:                         info["latest_payload"] = recent[0].payload
258:                         info["latest_since"] = recent[0].since.timestamp() if recent[0].since else None
259:                         info["latest_ts"] = recent[0].ts.timestamp()
260:             if zoom >= 3:
261:                 for fkind, info in data["facts"]["kinds"].items():
262:                     recent = reader.recent_facts(fkind, 5)
263:                     info["recent"] = [f["payload"] for f in recent]
264:             return data
265:     return fetch
266: 
267: 
268: def _resolve_target(file_arg: str | None, vertex_path: Path | None) -> Path:
269:     """Resolve the store target: explicit vertex_path > file/name arg > local root.
270: 
271:     State 1 of the store-verb existence contract (decision/design/
272:     store-verb-existence-exit-code-parity): when a named/explicit target
273:     resolves to a path that does not exist, raise a clean ``FileNotFoundError``
274:     here — the single shared chokepoint all three store verbs (ticks/stats/
275:     verify) pass through — so an absent ``.vertex`` reads as "X does not exist"
276:     rather than leaking a raw ``[Errno 2]`` from a downstream parse.
277:     """
278:     from .resolve import loops_home
279: 
280:     if vertex_path is not None:
281:         return vertex_path
282:     if file_arg is not None:
283:         p = Path(file_arg)
284:         if p.suffix or file_arg.startswith("./") or file_arg.startswith("/"):
285:             target = p
286:         else:
287:             # Local-first — same resolution the verbs use
288:             # (thread:global-local-walk-broken).
289:             from .resolve import _resolve_vertex_for_dispatch
290: 
291:             resolved = _resolve_vertex_for_dispatch(file_arg)
292:             if resolved is not None:
293:                 target = resolved
294:             else:
295:                 from lang.population import resolve_vertex
296: 
297:                 target = resolve_vertex(file_arg, loops_home())
298:         # State 1 covers the .vertex/name target only: an absent .db is a
299:         # state-2 (materialization) concern handled downstream by
300:         # _require_materialized_store, and the verbs that forbid a .db target
301:         # (ticks/reanchor) reject it by suffix with a more useful message — so
302:         # an existence check here must not pre-empt those.
303:         if target.suffix != ".db" and not target.exists():
304:             raise FileNotFoundError(f"{file_arg} does not exist")
305:         return target
306:     home = loops_home()
307:     root = home / ".vertex"
308:     if root.exists():
309:         return root
310:     from .resolve import missing_root_message
311: 
312:     raise FileNotFoundError(missing_root_message(root))
313: 
314: 
315: def _run_verify(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
316:     """Verify the tick hash chain of a store. Exit 0 = intact, 1 = broken.
317: 
318:     Read-only — never migrates schema. Pre-chain stores report all ticks
319:     as legacy and pass (nothing to verify yet).
320:     """
321:     import argparse
322: 
323:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
324:         prog="loops store verify",
325:         description="Verify tick hash chain and fact-window commitments.",
326:     )
327:     if vertex_path is None:
328:         p.add_argument("file", nargs="?", help="Store .db or .vertex file, or vertex name")
329:     p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="JSON report")
330:     p.add_argument(
331:         "-v", "--verbose", action="store_true",
332:         help="Per-tick attestation rows for the chained era "
333:              "(signature status, window fact count, cursor target)",
334:     )
335:     p.add_argument(
336:         "--deep", action="store_true",
337:         help="JSONL-canonical stores: stream every log line, compare it "
338:              "field-for-field and in order against the index, and re-derive "
339:              "the tick chain from canonical content (O(log), not O(1))",
340:     )
341:     # -h/--help is owned by argparse (add_help=True): parse_args prints the
342:     # help built from this parser and exits 0 natively. No hand-rolled block.
343:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
344: 
345:     try:
346:         target_path = _resolve_target(getattr(args, "file", None), vertex_path).resolve()
347:         # The agreement gate resolves the artifacts itself, purely — see
348:         # canonical_agreement on why _require_materialized_store's resolve
349:         # cannot run first on a JSONL-canonical target.
350:         gated = canonical_agreement(target_path, deep=args.deep)
351:         if gated is None:
352:             if args.deep:
353:                 msg = (
354:                     "--deep judges a canonical log against its derived index; "
355:                     f"{target_path.name} is sqlite-canonical (one artifact, "
356:                     "nothing to disagree with)"
357:                 )
358:                 if args.json:
359:                     # Same F2 shape-parity rule as the error branch below: a
360:                     # machine consumer gets {"error": ...}, never plain text.
361:                     import json as _json
362:                     print(_json.dumps({"error": msg}))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
363:                     return 2
364:                 return _refuse_store(msg, label="store verify")
365:             db_path = _require_materialized_store(target_path)
366:             agreement = None
367:         else:
368:             db_path, agreement = gated
369:     except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
370:         # F2 — three-verb --json parity: verify is hand-rolled and raises
371:         # before its --json branch, so without this an absent target / absent
372:         # .db / aggregate would emit PLAIN text under --json (a JSONDecodeError
373:         # for a machine consumer) where store stats emits {"error": ...} via
374:         # run_cli's _export_json. Match that shape. Non-json errors re-raise to
375:         # the cli/views boundary for the normal plain rendering (unchanged).
376:         if args.json:
377:             import json as _json
378:             print(_json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
379:             return 1
380:         raise
381: 
382:     if agreement is not None and not agreement.ok:
383:         # Suppress the chain walk ENTIRELY. Walking a poisoned index and
384:         # printing "chain intact" alongside a warning would still be the false
385:         # attestation: the chain's verdict is only worth anything about an
386:         # index the log supports. Name the check that failed instead.
387:         if args.json:
388:             import json as _json
389:             print(_json.dumps(  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
390:                 {"ok": False, "canonical": agreement.as_dict()}, indent=2
391:             ))
392:             return 1
393:         from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
394:         from painted.views import Severity, callout
395: 
396:         if agreement.index_behind:
397:             from engine.residence import canonical_mode
398: 
399:             canonical = resolve_canonical_path(target_path)
400:             mode = canonical_mode(canonical)
401:             # Both modes say the same thing: every divergence sits past what
402:             # the index claims to have consumed — where an interrupted append
403:             # lands, and (jsonl) also where a rewound marker plus a doctored
404:             # suffix lands. So this reports WHERE, never "benign". Still rc=1
405:             # and still no chain walk (it would attest to a partial index).
406:             # The vocabulary is the only difference — arrival counts records
407:             # against a consumed ordinal (see engine.canonical_audit.
408:             # Check.behind_by / at_ordinal, whose rewound check rules out a
409:             # moved marker), jsonl counts bytes against a consumed prefix and
410:             # L1 corroborates only the first unindexed line.
411:             if mode == "arrival":
412:                 log_name = "canonical arrival log"
413:                 divergence = "records beyond the consumed ordinal"
414:                 provenance = (
415:                     "consistent with a crash between the arrival fsync and "
416:                     "the index commit, with record counts and the rewound "
417:                     "check confirming the consumed prefix"
418:                 )
419:             else:
420:                 log_name = "canonical log"
421:                 divergence = "bytes the index never claimed to have consumed"
422:                 provenance = (
423:                     "consistent with a crash between the log's fsync and the "
424:                     "index commit, which this check cannot tell apart from an "
425:                     "edited suffix"
426:                 )
427:             detail = (
428:                 f"the derived index is behind the {log_name}, and every "
429:                 f"disagreement is in {divergence} — {provenance}; the tick "
430:                 "chain was NOT walked (it would attest to a partial index). "
431:                 f"Catch the index up with 'loops read {target_path.stem}', or "
432:                 "run 'loops store verify --deep' to rule out tampering"
433:             )
434:             head = callout(
435:                 f"{db_path.name} — INDEX BEHIND THE LOG",
436:                 severity=Severity.WARNING,
437:                 detail=detail,
438:             )
439:         else:
440:             head = callout(
441:                 f"{db_path.name} — CANONICAL DISAGREEMENT",
442:                 severity=Severity.ERROR,
443:                 detail="the derived index does not agree with the canonical "
444:                        "log; the tick chain was NOT walked (its verdict would "
445:                        "attest to an index the log does not support)",
446:             )
447:         blocks = [head]
448:         for check in agreement.checks:
449:             mark = "✓" if check.ok else "✗"
450:             blocks.append(Block.text(
451:                 f"  {mark} {check.name:<10} {check.detail}", Style(dim=check.ok)
452:             ))
453:         paint(join_vertical(*blocks))
454:         return 1
455: 
456:     # Tick-signature verification composes here (injection, not import):
457:     # the observer-key registry lives in the .vertex, so a raw .db target
458:     # verifies the chain but cannot check signatures.
459:     from custody import fact_verifier_for, tick_verifier_for
460: 
461:     verifier, declared_keys = tick_verifier_for(target_path)
462:     fact_verifier, _fact_keys = fact_verifier_for(target_path)
463: 
464:     from atoms import Fact
465:     from engine.sqlite_store import SqliteStore
466: 
467:     store = SqliteStore(path=db_path, serialize=lambda f: f.to_dict(), deserialize=Fact.from_dict)
468:     try:
469:         report = store.verify_chain(verifier=verifier, include_ticks=args.verbose)
470:         fact_report = store.verify_facts(verifier=fact_verifier)
471:     finally:
472:         store.close()
473: 
474:     if args.json:
475:         import json as _json
476:         payload = {**report, "fact_signatures": fact_report}
477:         if agreement is not None:
478:             payload["canonical"] = agreement.as_dict()
479:         print(_json.dumps(payload, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
480:         return 0 if report["ok"] and fact_report["ok"] else 1
481: 
482:     from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
483:     from painted.views import Severity, callout
484: 
485:     ok = report["ok"] and fact_report["ok"]
486:     total_facts = report["covered_facts"] + report["uncovered_facts"]
487:     if not report["ok"]:
488:         verdict, verdict_sev = "CHAIN BROKEN", Severity.ERROR
489:     elif not fact_report["ok"]:
490:         verdict, verdict_sev = "FACT SIGNATURES BROKEN", Severity.ERROR
491:     elif agreement is None:
492:         verdict, verdict_sev = "chain intact", Severity.SUCCESS
493:     elif agreement.deep:
494:         # Say what was actually checked. "chain intact" on a two-artifact
495:         # store used to name the weaker claim with the stronger words.
496:         verdict, verdict_sev = (
497:             "canonical content verified line-by-line; chain re-derived from "
498:             "the log", Severity.SUCCESS,
499:         )
500:     else:
501:         verdict, verdict_sev = (
502:             "index chain intact; canonical parity checks pass (not deep)",
503:             Severity.SUCCESS,
504:         )
505: 
506:     # Composed, never Block.text("\n".join(...)): painted 0.4.0 neutralizes a
507:     # raw \n to a space at the cell level, so multi-line must be real rows
508:     # (friction:block-text-multiline-passthrough-broke-on-040).
509:     blocks: list[Block] = [
510:         callout(f"{db_path.name} — {verdict}", severity=verdict_sev),
511:     ]
512: 
513:     def row(text: str) -> None:
514:         blocks.append(Block.text(f"  {text}", Style(dim=False)))
515: 
516:     # Three orthogonal axes, labeled so they stop reading as competing
517:     # fractions of one total (disclosure-grammar item b):
518:     #   chain      — tick hash chain integrity
519:     #   coverage   — which facts are sealed under a tick window
520:     #   authorship — per-fact signatures (delta 3); independent of coverage
521:     chain_bits = [f"{report['ticks']} ticks"]
522:     if report["chained"]:
523:         chain_bits.append(f"{report['chained']} chained")
524:     if report["legacy"]:
525:         chain_bits.append(f"{report['legacy']} legacy")
526:     if report["signed"]:
527:         chain_bits.append(f"{report['signed']} signed")
528:     chain_line = "chain        " + " · ".join(chain_bits)
529:     if report["signed"] and report["sig_checked"]:
530:         plural = "s" if len(declared_keys) != 1 else ""
531:         chain_line += f" · verified against registry ({len(declared_keys)} key{plural})"
532:     elif report["signed"]:
533:         chain_line += " · unchecked (no keys in registry — verify via the .vertex)"
534:     row(chain_line)
535: 
536:     if report["uncovered_facts"]:
537:         row(
538:             f"coverage     {report['covered_facts']}/{total_facts} facts sealed "
539:             f"under a tick · {report['uncovered_facts']} on live edge"
540:         )
541:     else:
542:         row(f"coverage     {report['covered_facts']}/{total_facts} facts sealed under a tick")
543: 
544:     if fact_report["signed"]:
545:         checked = "checked against registry" if fact_report["sig_checked"] else (
546:             "unchecked (no keys in registry)"
547:         )
548:         row(f"authorship   {fact_report['signed']}/{total_facts} facts signed · {checked}")
549:     elif fact_report["facts"]:
550:         row(f"authorship   0/{total_facts} facts signed (pre-signature era)")
551: 
552:     if agreement is not None:
553:         # A fourth axis, and the one that says how much of the log was read.
554:         # The scope boundary is printed, not left in a docstring: a
555:         # coordinated edit of an unsealed fact in BOTH artifacts is uncaught
556:         # here by design, and the output must not imply otherwise.
557:         if agreement.deep:
558:             row("canonical    every log line matches the index in order · "
559:                 "chain re-derived from log content")
560:         else:
561:             row("canonical    offset · counts · last-line agree with the log "
562:                 "(interior rows unread — use --deep)")
563:         row("             live edge: an unsealed fact edited in BOTH log and "
564:             "index is out of scope (its witnesses are the fact signature and "
565:             "the next seal)")
566: 
567:     # The strip-attack tripwires stay WARNING: a benign pre-signing store and a
568:     # malicious live-edge strip currently look identical here, and under-alarming
569:     # a real strip is worse than the false-positive. Honest fix (per-observer
570:     # pre-signing-vs-live-edge detection) is deferred to
571:     # thread:verify-strip-vs-presigning-detection (disclosure-grammar item c).
572:     if declared_keys and report["chained"] and not report["signed"]:
573:         blocks.append(callout(
574:             "registry declares signing key(s) but no tick is signed",
575:             severity=Severity.WARNING,
576:             detail="pre-signing store, or signatures stripped — the registry is the external anchor",
577:         ))
578:     if _fact_keys:
579:         silent = [
580:             name for name in _fact_keys
581:             if fact_report["observers"].get(name, {}).get("signed", 0) == 0
582:             and fact_report["observers"].get(name, {}).get("unsigned", 0) > 0
583:         ]
584:         if silent and fact_report["signed"]:
585:             blocks.append(callout(
586:                 f"keyed observer(s) with no signed facts: {', '.join(silent)}",
587:                 severity=Severity.WARNING,
588:                 detail="pre-signing era, or signatures stripped on the live edge",
589:             ))
590: 
591:     for b in report["breaks"]:
592:         blocks.append(callout(
593:             f"{b['name']} ({b['tick']})", severity=Severity.ERROR, detail=b["reason"]
594:         ))
595:     for b in fact_report["breaks"]:
596:         blocks.append(callout(
597:             f"fact {b['fact']} ({b['observer']}/{b['kind']})",
598:             severity=Severity.ERROR, detail=b["reason"],
599:         ))
600:     if report["truncated"] or fact_report["truncated"]:
601:         row(f"… stopped after {len(report['breaks']) + len(fact_report['breaks'])} breaks")
602:     if args.verbose and report.get("tick_detail"):
603:         from datetime import datetime, timezone
604: 
605:         row("chained ticks (append order):")
606:         for t in report["tick_detail"]:
607:             ts = datetime.fromtimestamp(t["ts"], tz=timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
608:             if not t["signed"]:
609:                 sig = "unsigned"
610:             elif t["sig_ok"] is None:
611:                 sig = "signed (unchecked)"
612:             else:
613:                 sig = "sig ✓" if t["sig_ok"] else "sig ✗"
614:             cursor = t["cursor_kind"] or "?"
615:             if t["cursor_preview"]:
616:                 cursor += f': "{t["cursor_preview"]}"'
617:             row(
618:                 f"  {'✓' if t['ok'] else '✗'} {ts} {t['name']} · {sig} · "
619:                 f"{t['window_facts']} facts · cursor → {cursor}"
620:             )
621:     paint(join_vertical(*blocks))
622:     return 0 if ok else 1
623: 
624: 
625: def _run_rebirth(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
626:     """Rebirth a store: transform-replay into a new store, with receipt.
627: 
628:     The reborn store is a NEW custody context — facts replay in witness
629:     order through a deterministic transform, old ticks re-enter as facts
630:     (envelope verbatim), a receipt fact records the source's identity
631:     (content hash, file hash, chain head), and a genesis tick seals
632:     everything including the receipt. The source is never modified;
633:     swapping the reborn store in is a deliberate, separate act.
634: 
635:     Signing composes from the source vertex's key custody: the new
636:     incarnation is the same identity's next chapter, so the lineage's
637:     key signs its genesis. A rebirth is immediately verified (re-run
638:     the transform, diff); ``--check`` re-verifies an existing one.
639:     """
640:     import argparse
641:     import os
642: 
643:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
644:         prog="loops store rebirth",
645:         description="Replay a store through a transform into a new store, "
646:                     "with a verifiable receipt and a sealed genesis tick.",
647:     )
648:     if vertex_path is None:
649:         p.add_argument("source", help="Source store .db or .vertex file, or vertex name")
650:     p.add_argument("target", help="Path for the reborn .db (must not exist; with --check: must exist)")
651:     p.add_argument(
652:         "--rule", default="identity", choices=["identity", "ulid-migration"],
653:         help="Transform: identity (re-seal/cleanup) or ulid-migration "
654:              "(deterministically migrate uuid4-era ids to event-time ULIDs)",
655:     )
656:     p.add_argument(
657:         "--observer", default=None,
658:         help="Who performs the rebirth (default: $LOOPS_OBSERVER or 'rebirth')",
659:     )
660:     p.add_argument(
661:         "--check", action="store_true",
662:         help="Verify an existing rebirth instead of performing one "
663:              "(re-run the transform, diff the target)",
664:     )
665:     p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="JSON receipt/report")
666:     # -h/--help is owned by argparse (add_help=True): parse_args prints the
667:     # help built from this parser and exits 0 natively. No hand-rolled block.
668:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
669: 
670:     src_target = _resolve_target(getattr(args, "source", None), vertex_path).resolve()
671:     src_db = resolve_store_path(src_target)
672:     if not src_db.exists():
673:         raise FileNotFoundError(f"{src_db} does not exist")
674:     target = Path(args.target)
675: 
676:     from store import identity, rebirth_store, ulid_migration, verify_rebirth
677: 
678:     transform = {"identity": identity, "ulid-migration": ulid_migration}[args.rule]()
679:     observer = args.observer or os.environ.get("LOOPS_OBSERVER") or "rebirth"
680: 
681:     # Signing/verification compose from the SOURCE vertex's custody — the
682:     # reborn store is the same lineage's next incarnation (injection, not
683:     # import; raw .db source → unsigned genesis, honest pre-signature era).
684:     from custody import tick_signer_for, tick_verifier_for
685: 
686:     signer = tick_signer_for(src_target)
687:     verifier, _keys = tick_verifier_for(src_target)
688: 
689:     result = None
690:     if not args.check:
691:         result = rebirth_store(
692:             src_db, target,
693:             transform=transform,
694:             tick_signer=signer,
695:             observer=observer,
696:             source_name=src_db.stem,
697:         )
698:     verification = verify_rebirth(src_db, target, transform=transform, verifier=verifier)
699: 
700:     if args.json:
701:         import dataclasses
702:         import json as _json
703: 
704:         report = {"verification": dataclasses.asdict(verification)}
705:         if result is not None:
706:             report["rebirth"] = dataclasses.asdict(result)
707:         print(_json.dumps(report, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
708:         return 0 if verification.ok else 1
709: 
710:     from painted import Block, Style, paint
711: 
712:     mark = "✓" if verification.ok else "✗"
713:     lines = []
714:     if result is not None:
715:         lines.append(f"{mark} {src_db.name} → {target.name}: rebirth (rule={args.rule})")
716:         lines.append(
717:             f"  facts: {result.facts_in} in, {result.facts_out} out "
718:             f"({result.ids_migrated} ids migrated, {result.filtered} filtered)"
719:         )
720:         lines.append(f"  ticks: {result.ticks_in} re-entered as facts")
721:         lines.append(
722:             f"  receipt: {result.receipt_id} · genesis tick "
723:             f"{'signed' if result.tick_signed else 'unsigned (no key for source vertex)'}"
724:         )
725:     else:
726:         lines.append(f"{mark} {src_db.name} → {target.name}: rebirth check (rule={args.rule})")
727:     checks = [
728:         ("re-run diff clean" if not verification.mismatches
729:          else f"{len(verification.mismatches)} row mismatches"),
730:         ("receipt found" if verification.receipt_found else "RECEIPT MISSING"),
731:         ("counts match" if verification.counts_match else "COUNTS DISAGREE"),
732:         ("source content match" if verification.source_content_match
733:          else "SOURCE CHANGED since rebirth"),
734:         ("chain intact" if verification.chain_ok else "CHAIN BROKEN"),
735:     ]
736:     lines.append(f"  verified: {' · '.join(checks)} ({verification.facts_checked} facts checked)")
737:     for m in verification.mismatches:
738:         lines.append(f"  ✗ {m}")
739:     # Compose real rows — Block.text("\n".join(...)) flattens to one line
740:     # under painted 0.4.0 (friction:block-text-multiline-passthrough-broke-on-040).
741:     from painted import join_vertical
742:     paint(join_vertical(*(Block.text(ln, Style(dim=False)) for ln in lines)))
743:     return 0 if verification.ok else 1
744: 
745: 
746: def _run_migrate(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
747:     """Migrate a legacy store into a fresh arrival lineage (ratified
748:     ``design:arrival-break-slice4-migration-sidecar`` §F, §J.1 WP4).
749: 
750:     A thin wrapper over ``migrate.sidecar.run_migration`` — no privileged
751:     path of its own. Mints a signed genesis under the target ``.vertex``'s
752:     own self-observer (the migration custodian: the only identity whose
753:     private key this process holds), introduces every keyed declared
754:     observer, replays the legacy source deterministically, verifies the
755:     result (Full + equivalence re-run), and atomically publishes by
756:     surgically editing the ``.vertex`` store clause onto the minted
757:     lineage. ``--resume`` targets an interrupted run explicitly — resuming
758:     is a deliberate act, never inferred from an existing staged file.
759:     """
760:     import argparse
761: 
762:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
763:         prog="loops store migrate",
764:         description="Migrate a legacy .jsonl/.sqlite store into a fresh "
765:                     "arrival lineage, with a signed migration report and "
766:                     "an atomic .vertex store-clause publish.",
767:     )
768:     p.add_argument("source", help="Legacy store .jsonl or .sqlite file")
769:     p.add_argument(
770:         "--vertex", required=True,
771:         help="Target .vertex file or vertex name",
772:     )
773:     p.add_argument(
774:         "--rule", default="ulid-migration", choices=["identity", "ulid-migration"],
775:         help="Transform: identity (verbatim) or ulid-migration "
776:              "(deterministically migrate uuid4-era ids to event-time ULIDs)",
777:     )
778:     p.add_argument(
779:         "--resume", default=None, metavar="TARGET",
780:         help="Resume an interrupted migration at TARGET .arrival file "
781:              "(deliberate — never inferred)",
782:     )
783:     p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="JSON outcome")
784:     # -h/--help is owned by argparse (add_help=True): parse_args prints the
785:     # help built from this parser and exits 0 natively. No hand-rolled block.
786:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
787: 
788:     if vertex_path is not None and args.vertex is not None:
789:         pos_target = _resolve_target(None, vertex_path).resolve()
790:         flag_target = _resolve_target(args.vertex, None).resolve()
791:         if pos_target != flag_target:
792:             return _refuse_store(
793:                 f"positional vertex target '{vertex_path}' conflicts with --vertex '{args.vertex}' — "
794:                 f"targets resolve to different vertices ({pos_target.name} != {flag_target.name})",
795:                 label="store migrate",
796:             )
797:         vertex_target = flag_target
798:     else:
799:         vertex_target = _resolve_target(args.vertex, vertex_path).resolve()
800: 
801:     if vertex_target.suffix != ".vertex":
802:         raise ValueError(
803:             "migrate requires a .vertex target — the custodian signer and "
804:             "observer registry live in the custody context, not a raw .db"
805:         )
806: 
807:     from lang import ParseError, parse_vertex_file
808:     from engine.residence import canonical_mode
809: 
810:     try:
811:         pre_ast = parse_vertex_file(vertex_target)
812:     except ParseError as exc:
813:         return _refuse_store(
814:             f"the vertex file cannot be parsed: {exc}",
815:             label=vertex_target.stem,
816:         )
817: 
818:     if pre_ast.store is not None:
819:         if canonical_mode(pre_ast.store) == "arrival":
820:             return _refuse_store(
821:                 f"already on an arrival lineage ({pre_ast.store}) — "
822:                 "re-migration would orphan it; deliberate re-migration is a slice-6 ceremony",
823:                 label=vertex_target.stem,
824:             )
825: 
826:     source_path = Path(args.source).resolve()
827:     if not source_path.exists():
828:         raise FileNotFoundError(f"{source_path} does not exist")
829: 
830:     from custody import arrival_signer_for
831: 
832:     signer = arrival_signer_for(vertex_target)
833:     if signer is None or signer(vertex_target.stem, "0" * 64) is None:
834:         return _refuse_store(
835:             "no signing key for the vertex's self-observer — migration "
836:             "must sign the custodian genesis and key introductions "
837:             "(loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)",
838:             label=vertex_target.stem,
839:         )
840: 
841:     from migrate.legacy_ids import identity, ulid_migration
842:     from migrate.refusals import MigrationRefused
843:     from migrate.sidecar import run_migration
844: 
845:     transform_rule = {"identity": identity, "ulid-migration": ulid_migration}[args.rule]()
846: 
847:     try:
848:         outcome = run_migration(
849:             source_path, vertex_target,
850:             store_dir=vertex_target.parent,
851:             signer=signer,
852:             transform_rule=transform_rule,
853:             resume_target=args.resume,
854:         )
855:     except MigrationRefused as exc:
856:         return _refuse_store(str(exc), label=vertex_target.stem)
857: 
858:     if args.json:
859:         import dataclasses
860:         import json as _json
861: 
862:         report = {
863:             "target_path": str(outcome.target_path),
864:             "lineage": outcome.lineage,
865:             "head": dataclasses.asdict(outcome.head),
866:             "report_path": str(outcome.report_path),
867:             "exceptions": dataclasses.asdict(outcome.exceptions),
868:         }
869:         print(_json.dumps(report, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
870:         return 0
871: 
872:     from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
873: 
874:     exc_lines = []
875:     if outcome.exceptions.keyless_declared_observers:
876:         exc_lines.append(
877:             f"  keyless observers (skipped): "
878:             f"{', '.join(outcome.exceptions.keyless_declared_observers)}"
879:         )
880:     if outcome.exceptions.undeclared_row_observers:
881:         exc_lines.append(
882:             f"  undeclared observers in rows: "
883:             f"{', '.join(outcome.exceptions.undeclared_row_observers)}"
884:         )
885:     if outcome.exceptions.dropped_units:
886:         exc_lines.append(f"  dropped units: {len(outcome.exceptions.dropped_units)}")
887:     lines = [
888:         f"✓ {source_path.name} → {vertex_target.stem}: migrated (rule={args.rule})",
889:         f"  lineage: {outcome.lineage} · head {outcome.head.ordinal} "
890:         f"{outcome.head.record_hash[:16]}…",
891:         f"  target: {outcome.target_path.name}",
892:         f"  report: {outcome.report_path.name}",
893:     ] + exc_lines
894:     paint(join_vertical(*(Block.text(ln, Style(dim=False)) for ln in lines)))
895:     return 0
896: 
897: 
898: def _run_export(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
899:     """Refuse ``store export``: the sqlite-to-JSONL bridge it drove is gone.
900: 
901:     ``store.jsonl`` — the export/rebuild bridge to the JSONL-canonical shape —
902:     dissolved with the arrival cut (decision:design/arrival-sliceC-ordering).
903:     Migration targets an arrival log now, so there is no surviving thing for
904:     this verb to do.
905: 
906:     The ARM stays rather than the whole subcommand being deleted, because
907:     deleting it is not a retirement a user can read: ``_dispatch_store`` would
908:     fall through to the base-inspect pre-parse, which takes the first argv as a
909:     store path and reports that a store named ``export`` cannot be resolved.
910:     That reads as a broken command. A refusal that names what happened and
911:     where to go instead is the honest end of a removed feature.
912: 
913:     Argv is not parsed — every invocation refuses, so parsing it could only
914:     produce a usage error for a command that no longer exists.
915:     """
916:     return _refuse_store(
917:         "the sqlite-to-JSONL export bridge (store.jsonl) is gone — JSONL-"
918:         "canonical is a frozen legacy family and nothing produces its log "
919:         "any more. Canonical-log stores are arrival logs now "
920:         "(engine.arrival_store.ArrivalStore); a plain SqliteStore is read "
921:         "with `loops store` / `loops store stats`.",
922:         label="store export",
923:     )
924: 
925: 
926: def _run_reanchor(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
927:     # slice-5: reanchor dies outright — no ceremony successor. It rewrites
928:     # chain rows in place, which an append-only hash-chained ledger cannot
929:     # do; its purpose (re-deriving the attestation layer after a key
930:     # change) already has an arrival successor in the key-introduction
931:     # record, and migrated records are never re-signed (inner signatures
932:     # ride verbatim). Ruled disposition:
933:     # design:arrival-break-slice4-migration-sidecar §F.
934:     """Re-anchor a store's attestation layer under the current canonical
935:     encoding (SPEC §8.1: canon migrations re-anchor, never grandfather).
936: 
937:     Requires a .vertex target — the signing keys and observer registry
938:     live in the custody context, not the raw .db. Re-signs signed facts,
939:     re-links the chain, then verifies the result against the registry.
940:     Exit 0 = re-anchored and verified, 1 = post-reanchor verify failed.
941:     """
942:     import argparse
943: 
944:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
945:         prog="loops store reanchor",
946:         description="Recompute chain hashes and signatures under the "
947:                     "current canonical encoding, then verify.",
948:     )
949:     if vertex_path is None:
950:         p.add_argument("file", nargs="?", help="Vertex .vertex file or vertex name")
951:     p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="JSON receipt")
952:     # -h/--help is owned by argparse (add_help=True): parse_args prints the
953:     # help built from this parser and exits 0 natively. No hand-rolled block.
954:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
955: 
956:     target_path = _resolve_target(getattr(args, "file", None), vertex_path).resolve()
957:     if target_path.suffix != ".vertex":
958:         raise ValueError(
959:             "reanchor requires a .vertex target — re-signing needs the "
960:             "keys and registry co-located with the store"
961:         )
962:     db_path = resolve_store_path(target_path)
963:     if not db_path.exists():
964:         raise FileNotFoundError(f"{db_path} does not exist")
965: 
966:     from custody import (
967:         fact_signer_for, fact_verifier_for, tick_signer_for, tick_verifier_for,
968:     )
969: 
970:     from atoms import Fact
971:     from engine.jsonl_store import JsonlCanonicalUnsupported, open_canonical_store
972: 
973:     # open_canonical_store, not SqliteStore on db_path: reanchor rewrites
974:     # chain rows in place, which a JSONL-canonical store refuses — and the
975:     # refusal only fires if the JsonlStore subclass is the one constructed.
976:     store = open_canonical_store(
977:         resolve_canonical_path(target_path),
978:         serialize=lambda f: f.to_dict(),
979:         deserialize=Fact.from_dict,
980:         tick_signer=tick_signer_for(target_path),
981:         fact_signer=fact_signer_for(target_path),
982:     )
983:     try:
984:         try:
985:             receipt = store.reanchor()
986:         except JsonlCanonicalUnsupported as exc:
987:             return _refuse_store(str(exc), label=db_path.name)
988:         verifier, _keys = tick_verifier_for(target_path)
989:         fact_verifier, _fkeys = fact_verifier_for(target_path)
990:         report = store.verify_chain(verifier=verifier)
991:         fact_report = store.verify_facts(verifier=fact_verifier)
992:     finally:
993:         store.close()
994: 
995:     ok = report["ok"] and fact_report["ok"]
996:     if args.json:
997:         import json as _json
998:         print(_json.dumps({**receipt, "verified": ok}, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
999:         return 0 if ok else 1
1000: 
1001:     from painted import Block, Style, paint
1002: 
1003:     head = receipt["head"][:16] + "…" if receipt["head"] else "(no chain)"
1004:     paint(Block.text(
1005:         f"{'✓' if ok else '✗'} {db_path.name}: re-anchored — "
1006:         f"{receipt['facts_resigned']} facts re-signed, "
1007:         f"{receipt['ticks_rechained']} ticks re-chained "
1008:         f"({receipt['ticks_resigned']} re-signed) · head {head} · "
1009:         f"verify {'ok' if ok else 'FAILED'}",
1010:         Style(),
1011:     ))
1012:     return 0 if ok else 1
1013: 
1014: 
1015: def _read_absorption_state(
1016:     db_path: Path,
1017: ) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None, bool]:
1018:     """Read (has_genesis, chain_head, fact_cursor, has_marker) for absorb.
1019: 
1020:     SPEC §9.2 era rule: a genesis pins "everything before me predates
1021:     historization" — a verifiable claim, not an inference from row order.
1022: 
1023:     - ``has_genesis`` — a ``_decl.genesis`` fact already exists (this store
1024:       has opened its lineage; re-absorb / edit is S4, so absorb refuses).
1025:     - ``chain_head`` — the row-identity hash of the latest *chained* tick
1026:       (what a successor tick's ``prev_hash`` commits to), or None when the
1027:       store has no chained tick. A pre-chain-only store pins on the cursor.
1028:     - ``fact_cursor`` — the id of the newest fact by WITNESS order (rowid),
1029:       matching ``append_tick``'s cursor authority — or None on empty.
1030: 
1031:     ``has_marker`` — the ``store_meta.own_lineage`` identity marker exists.
1032:     Genesis rows WITHOUT a marker are unclaimable (pre-marker store, or a
1033:     merged foreign genesis) — absorb refuses with adopt guidance rather than
1034:     inferring identity from facts.
1035: 
1036:     A not-yet-materialized store (no ``.db``, or a schemaless file) returns
1037:     ``(False, None, None, False)``: genesis becomes its first fact.
1038:     """
1039:     import sqlite3
1040: 
1041:     if not db_path.exists():
1042:         return False, None, None, False
1043: 
1044:     from engine.sqlite_store import (
1045:         _TICK_ROW_SQL,
1046:         _TICK_ROW_SQL_V1,
1047:         tick_row_hash,
1048:     )
1049:     from lang.document import DECL_GENESIS
1050: 
1051:     conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
1052:     conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
1053:     try:
1054:         try:
1055:             has_genesis = (
1056:                 conn.execute(
1057:                     "SELECT 1 FROM facts WHERE kind = ? LIMIT 1", (DECL_GENESIS,)
1058:                 ).fetchone()
1059:                 is not None
1060:             )
1061:             def newest_first(table: str) -> str:
1062:                 """Newest-row ordering for `table`: the arrival coordinate
1063:                 when the store carries one, rowid on a pre-coordinate store."""
1064:                 cols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")}
1065:                 return (
1066:                     "ORDER BY arrival_ordinal DESC, arrival_seq DESC"
1067:                     if "arrival_ordinal" in cols
1068:                     else "ORDER BY rowid DESC"
1069:                 )
1070: 
1071:             frow = conn.execute(
1072:                 f"SELECT id FROM facts {newest_first('facts')} LIMIT 1"
1073:             ).fetchone()
1074:             fact_cursor = frow[0] if frow else None
1075:         except sqlite3.OperationalError:
1076:             # Schemaless / non-store file — nothing absorbed, nothing to pin.
1077:             return False, None, None, False
1078: 
1079:         chain_head: str | None = None
1080:         try:
1081:             tcols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(ticks)")}
1082:             if "window_hash" in tcols:
1083:                 row_sql = _TICK_ROW_SQL if "signature" in tcols else _TICK_ROW_SQL_V1
1084:                 row = conn.execute(
1085:                     f"SELECT {row_sql} FROM ticks "
1086:                     f"WHERE window_hash IS NOT NULL {newest_first('ticks')} LIMIT 1"
1087:                 ).fetchone()
1088:                 if row is not None:
1089:                     # tick_row_hash reads an 11-field row (signature at [10]);
1090:                     # a delta-1 schema yields 10, pad the signature slot NULL.
1091:                     chain_head = tick_row_hash(row if len(row) > 10 else (*row, None))
1092:         except sqlite3.OperationalError:
1093:             pass
1094: 
1095:         has_marker = False
1096:         try:
1097:             has_marker = (
1098:                 conn.execute(
1099:                     "SELECT 1 FROM store_meta WHERE key = 'own_lineage'"
1100:                 ).fetchone()
1101:                 is not None
1102:             )
1103:         except sqlite3.OperationalError:
1104:             pass  # pre-marker schema
1105: 
1106:         return has_genesis, chain_head, fact_cursor, has_marker
1107:     finally:
1108:         conn.close()
1109: 
1110: 
1111: def _run_absorb(
1112:     argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None, observer: str | None = None
1113: ) -> int:
1114:     """Reconcile a store with its ``.vertex`` declaration (SPEC §9.2). Bimodal.
1115: 
1116:     The verb detects whether the store's lineage is open and switches ceremony:
1117: 
1118:     - **No genesis → genesis mode** (S1, :func:`_absorb_genesis_mode`): parse the
1119:       ``.vertex`` once, decompose into subject-scoped documents, and append ONE
1120:       signed ``_decl.genesis`` fact carrying the whole document set, the protocol
1121:       version, and the era pins. The genesis fact's own id IS the lineage id.
1122: 
1123:     - **Genesis present → edit mode** (S4, :func:`_absorb_edit`): diff the
1124:       freshly-parsed file against the fold head (Latest per (kind, subject),
1125:       self-lineage) and re-emit ONLY the changed subjects as whole documents,
1126:       tombstoning removed subjects. Unchanged file writes nothing (idempotence).
1127: 
1128:     Both ceremonies MUST be signed — declaration events are the store's
1129:     attestation root/history; a missing signing key refuses (exit 2). ``-n`` is
1130:     read-only: genesis mode previews the absorb, edit mode surfaces which
1131:     subjects diverge from the store head (the sole divergence surface — nothing
1132:     auto-absorbs, and the store head stays authoritative until a real absorb).
1133:     ``--json`` emits a machine-readable receipt.
1134:     """
1135:     import argparse
1136: 
1137:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
1138:         prog="loops store absorb",
1139:         description="Reconcile a store with its .vertex declaration (SPEC §9.2). "
1140:                     "No genesis yet → open the lineage (absorb the declaration "
1141:                     "whole as a signed genesis event). Genesis present → edit "
1142:                     "mode: diff the file against the store's fold head and "
1143:                     "re-emit only the changed subjects. -n surfaces divergence "
1144:                     "without writing.",
1145:     )
1146:     if vertex_path is None:
1147:         p.add_argument("file", nargs="?", help="Vertex .vertex file or vertex name")
1148:     p.add_argument(
1149:         "--observer", default=None,
1150:         help="Observer recording the genesis (default: the resolved "
1151:              "self-observer, same resolution emit uses)",
1152:     )
1153:     p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="JSON receipt")
1154:     p.add_argument(
1155:         "-n", "--dry-run", action="store_true",
1156:         help="Show what would be absorbed (genesis) or which subjects diverge "
1157:              "from the store head (edit) without writing",
1158:     )
1159:     # -h/--help is owned by argparse (add_help=True): parse_args prints the
1160:     # help built from this parser and exits 0 natively. No hand-rolled block.
1161:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
1162: 
1163:     target_path = _resolve_target(getattr(args, "file", None), vertex_path).resolve()
1164:     if target_path.suffix != ".vertex":
1165:         raise ValueError(
1166:             "absorb requires a .vertex target — genesis records the "
1167:             "declaration and signs it with the vertex's co-located key"
1168:         )
1169:     # resolve_store_path raises ValueError('No store configured') for a pure
1170:     # aggregate (combine/discover) vertex — those own no store, so there is
1171:     # nothing to open a lineage over (build-plan aggregation wrinkle, deferred).
1172:     db_path = resolve_store_path(target_path)
1173: 
1174:     # Parse the declaration ONCE — and VALIDATE it. Absorb mints immutable
1175:     # signed events (genesis/edit rows are never rewritten), so a declaration
1176:     # that ordinary validation rejects (e.g. a loop named into the _decl.*
1177:     # reserved namespace) must never enter the lineage (closing review #6).
1178:     from lang import parse_vertex_file, validate_vertex
1179: 
1180:     ast = parse_vertex_file(target_path)
1181:     try:
1182:         validate_vertex(ast)
1183:     except Exception as exc:
1184:         from painted import Block, Style, paint
1185:         paint(Block.text(
1186:             f"✗ {target_path.stem}: declaration invalid — {exc}", Style()
1187:         ), file=sys.stderr)
1188:         return 2
1189: 
1190:     # Observer resolution. Precedence: explicit ``--observer`` on the absorb
1191:     # parser (direct/test calls) > invocation observer from the global peel >
1192:     # resolve_observer's env/declared chain — the same resolution emit uses.
1193:     from loops.commands.identity import resolve_observer
1194: 
1195:     explicit = args.observer if args.observer is not None else observer
1196:     observer = resolve_observer(explicit)
1197: 
1198:     # Detect whether the store has already opened its lineage — this selects
1199:     # the ceremony (SPEC §9.2 / §9.5). No genesis → genesis mode (open the
1200:     # lineage, S1). Genesis present → edit mode (S4): diff the file against the
1201:     # fold head and re-emit ONLY the changed subjects. Same verb, two ceremonies.
1202:     has_genesis, _chain_head, _fact_cursor, has_marker = _read_absorption_state(db_path)
1203:     if has_genesis and not has_marker:
1204:         # Genesis rows exist but none is CLAIMED as self — a pre-marker store
1205:         # or a merged foreign genesis. Facts alone cannot prove which; the
1206:         # explicit adopt ceremony must run first (closing re-review #1).
1207:         from painted import Block, Style, paint
1208:         paint(Block.text(
1209:             f"✗ {target_path.stem}: genesis row(s) present but no own_lineage "
1210:             "marker — run `loops store adopt` to claim the store's own "
1211:             "lineage before editing (identity is adopted, never inferred)",
1212:             Style(),
1213:         ), file=sys.stderr)
1214:         return 2
1215:     if has_genesis:
1216:         return _absorb_edit(
1217:             target_path, db_path, ast, observer,
1218:             dry_run=args.dry_run, as_json=args.json,
1219:         )
1220:     return _absorb_genesis_mode(
1221:         target_path, db_path, ast, observer,
1222:         dry_run=args.dry_run, as_json=args.json,
1223:     )
1224: 
1225: 
1226: def _absorb_genesis_mode(
1227:     target_path: Path,
1228:     db_path: Path,
1229:     ast: Any,
1230:     observer: str,
1231:     *,
1232:     dry_run: bool,
1233:     as_json: bool,
1234: ) -> int:
1235:     # slice-5: absorb-genesis dissolves into the sidecar's mint. It is not
1236:     # a ceremony the sidecar offers; it is a thing the sidecar's genesis
1237:     # makes unnecessary — under arrival there are no genesisless stores,
1238:     # so nothing calls this path. Ruled disposition:
1239:     # design:arrival-break-slice4-migration-sidecar §F.
1240:     """Genesis mode — open the store's lineage (SPEC §9.2 era opening, S1).
1241: 
1242:     Unchanged from the original ``absorb`` behavior; extracted so the bimodal
1243:     dispatcher can select it when the store has no genesis yet. Its output is
1244:     golden-locked, so the rendering is preserved verbatim.
1245:     """
1246:     from lang.document import genesis_payload
1247:     from custody import fact_signer_for
1248: 
1249:     declaration_payload = genesis_payload(ast)
1250:     protocol = declaration_payload["protocol"]
1251:     documents = declaration_payload["documents"]
1252:     doc_count = len(documents)
1253: 
1254:     def _render(receipt: dict, *, dry_run: bool) -> None:
1255:         chain_head, fact_cursor = receipt["chain_head"], receipt["fact_cursor"]
1256:         if as_json:
1257:             import json as _json
1258:             print(_json.dumps({**receipt, "dry_run": dry_run}, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
1259:             return
1260:         from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
1261:         verb = "would absorb" if dry_run else "genesis absorbed"
1262:         lineage_line = (
1263:             "  lineage: (dry-run — no genesis minted)"
1264:             if dry_run else f"  lineage: {receipt['lineage']}"
1265:         )
1266:         head_disp = (chain_head[:16] + "…") if chain_head else "(no chained tick)"
1267:         cursor_disp = fact_cursor if fact_cursor else "(empty store)"
1268:         lines = [
1269:             f"✓ {target_path.stem}: {verb} — protocol v{protocol}",
1270:             lineage_line,
1271:             f"  documents: {doc_count} subject{'s' if doc_count != 1 else ''}",
1272:             f"  pins: chain_head {head_disp} · fact_cursor {cursor_disp}",
1273:             f"  observer: {observer} · signed",
1274:         ]
1275:         paint(join_vertical(*(Block.text(ln, Style(dim=False)) for ln in lines)))
1276: 
1277:     _refuse = partial(_refuse_store, label=target_path.stem)
1278: 
1279:     if dry_run:
1280:         # Preview only — a read-only projection of what the real (atomic) path
1281:         # would do. Inherently racy (no write lock held); the atomicity
1282:         # guarantees belong to the real path's single transaction.
1283:         has_genesis, chain_head, fact_cursor, _has_marker = _read_absorption_state(db_path)
1284:         if has_genesis:
1285:             # Defensive: a concurrent absorb opened the lineage between the
1286:             # dispatcher's mode check and here — fall back to the edit refusal.
1287:             return _refuse(
1288:                 "already absorbed — a genesis event exists. Re-run absorb to "
1289:                 "reconcile an edited declaration (edit mode)."
1290:             )
1291:         signer = fact_signer_for(target_path)
1292:         signable = observer and signer is not None and signer(observer, "0" * 64) is not None
1293:         if not signable:
1294:             why = ("no observer resolved to sign as" if not observer
1295:                    else f"no signing key for observer '{observer}'")
1296:             return _refuse(
1297:                 f"cannot absorb — {why}. Genesis is the lineage's attestation "
1298:                 "root and must be signed; set up signing first "
1299:                 "(loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)."
1300:             )
1301:         _render(
1302:             {
1303:                 "vertex": target_path.stem, "lineage": None,
1304:                 "protocol": protocol, "documents": doc_count,
1305:                 "chain_head": chain_head, "fact_cursor": fact_cursor,
1306:                 "observer": observer, "signed": True,
1307:             },
1308:             dry_run=True,
1309:         )
1310:         return 0
1311: 
1312:     # Real path — the atomic engine primitive holds the identity check, era
1313:     # pins, sign-final-payload, and append in ONE transaction (no TOCTOU).
1314:     if not observer:
1315:         return _refuse(
1316:             "cannot absorb — no observer resolved to sign as. Genesis is the "
1317:             "lineage's attestation root and must be signed; set up signing "
1318:             "first (loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)."
1319:         )
1320: 
1321:     from atoms import Fact
1322:     from engine.jsonl_store import JsonlCanonicalUnsupported, open_canonical_store
1323:     from engine.sqlite_store import GenesisExists, UnsignableGenesis
1324: 
1325:     # open_canonical_store: absorb writes declaration facts, which under a
1326:     # JSONL-canonical vertex must either go through the log or refuse — a
1327:     # plain SqliteStore on the derived index does neither.
1328:     store = open_canonical_store(
1329:         resolve_canonical_path(target_path),
1330:         serialize=lambda f: f.to_dict(),
1331:         deserialize=Fact.from_dict,
1332:     )
1333:     try:
1334:         receipt = store.absorb_genesis(
1335:             documents,
1336:             observer=observer,
1337:             origin="",
1338:             fact_signer=fact_signer_for(target_path),
1339:         )
1340:     except JsonlCanonicalUnsupported as exc:
1341:         return _refuse(str(exc))
1342:     except GenesisExists:
1343:         # TOCTOU: a concurrent absorb opened the lineage after the dispatcher's
1344:         # mode check. The atomic primitive caught it — point at edit mode.
1345:         return _refuse(
1346:             "already absorbed — a genesis event exists. Re-run absorb to "
1347:             "reconcile an edited declaration (edit mode)."
1348:         )
1349:     except UnsignableGenesis:
1350:         return _refuse(
1351:             f"cannot absorb — no signing key for observer '{observer}'. Genesis "
1352:             "is the lineage's attestation root and must be signed; set up "
1353:             "signing first (loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)."
1354:         )
1355:     finally:
1356:         store.close()
1357: 
1358:     receipt["vertex"] = target_path.stem
1359:     _render(receipt, dry_run=False)
1360:     return 0
1361: 
1362: 
1363: def _decl_short(kind: str) -> str:
1364:     """A ``_decl.kind-defined`` → ``kind`` short label for edit-mode display."""
1365:     label = kind[len("_decl."):] if kind.startswith("_decl.") else kind
1366:     for suffix in ("-defined", "-retired", "-removed"):
1367:         if label.endswith(suffix):
1368:             return label[: -len(suffix)]
1369:     return label
1370: 
1371: 
1372: def _absorb_edit(
1373:     target_path: Path,
1374:     db_path: Path,
1375:     ast: Any,
1376:     observer: str,
1377:     *,
1378:     dry_run: bool,
1379:     as_json: bool,
1380: ) -> int:
1381:     """Edit mode — re-emit changed declaration subjects (SPEC §9.2, S4).
1382: 
1383:     The store's lineage is already open, so this is the edit ceremony: diff the
1384:     freshly-parsed file against the fold head (Latest per (kind, subject),
1385:     self-lineage) and re-emit ONLY the changed subjects as whole documents, plus
1386:     a tombstone per removed subject. Unchanged file → nothing written
1387:     (idempotence). File divergence is SURFACED via ``-n`` (the sole divergence
1388:     surface — no auto-absorb, no silent side-picking); the store head stays
1389:     authoritative for resolution until this runs.
1390:     """
1391:     from lang.document import EditRefused, diff_documents, vertex_to_documents
1392: 
1393:     from engine.declaration import (
1394:         AmbiguousLineage,
1395:         UnsupportedProtocol,
1396:         resolve_declaration_documents,
1397:     )
1398: 
1399:     _refuse = partial(_refuse_store, label=target_path.stem)
1400: 
1401:     # The edited file's document set.
1402:     new_docs = vertex_to_documents(ast)
1403: 
1404:     # CAS token FIRST, then the head read: if a concurrent edit lands between
1405:     # the two, the token is older than the head we diffed — absorb_edit then
1406:     # refuses (StaleDeclarationHead) instead of interleaving, and re-running
1407:     # picks up the moved head. Capturing in this order fails conservative.
1408:     from atoms import Fact
1409:     from engine.jsonl_store import JsonlCanonicalUnsupported, open_canonical_store
1410: 
1411:     # ONE handle for the whole ceremony. The CAS token still comes first —
1412:     # that ordering is what makes a concurrent edit refuse (StaleDeclarationHead)
1413:     # rather than interleave — but the token, not the handle's lifetime, is what
1414:     # carries it, so there is no reason to open the store twice.
1415:     store = open_canonical_store(
1416:         resolve_canonical_path(target_path),
1417:         serialize=lambda f: f.to_dict(),
1418:         deserialize=Fact.from_dict,
1419:     )
1420:     try:
1421:         expected_head = store.declaration_head()
1422: 
1423:         # The fold head from the store. has_genesis was true at dispatch, so this is
1424:         # normally a list; a resolution failure (ambiguous lineage, unsupported
1425:         # protocol) or a non-list refuses cleanly rather than diffing against noise.
1426:         try:
1427:             head = resolve_declaration_documents(db_path)
1428:         except (AmbiguousLineage, UnsupportedProtocol) as exc:
1429:             return _refuse(str(exc))
1430:         if not isinstance(head, list):
1431:             return _refuse(
1432:                 "store head unavailable — the lineage looks unopened or unhistorized; "
1433:                 "cannot diff (nothing to reconcile against)"
1434:             )
1435: 
1436:         # Diff. An inexpressible edit (singleton removal / identity rename) refuses.
1437:         try:
1438:             changes = diff_documents(head, new_docs)
1439:         except EditRefused as exc:
1440:             return _refuse(str(exc))
1441: 
1442:         n_def = sum(1 for c in changes if c.payload is not None)
1443:         n_ret = len(changes) - n_def
1444: 
1445:         def _emit_json(obj: dict) -> None:
1446:             import json as _json
1447:             print(_json.dumps(obj, indent=2))  # noqa: T201 — machine output path
1448: 
1449:         # Idempotence: an unchanged file writes nothing.
1450:         if not changes:
1451:             if as_json:
1452:                 _emit_json({
1453:                     "vertex": target_path.stem, "mode": "edit",
1454:                     "diverged": False, "defined": 0, "retired": 0, "changes": [],
1455:                 })
1456:             else:
1457:                 from painted import Block, Style, paint
1458:                 paint(Block.text(
1459:                     f"✓ {target_path.stem}: up to date — file matches store head",
1460:                     Style(dim=False),
1461:                 ))
1462:             return 0
1463: 
1464:         change_rows = [
1465:             {"kind": c.kind, "subject": c.subject, "change": c.annotation}
1466:             for c in changes
1467:         ]
1468: 
1469:         def _render_divergence(*, applied: bool) -> None:
1470:             if as_json:
1471:                 _emit_json({
1472:                     "vertex": target_path.stem, "mode": "edit",
1473:                     "diverged": True, "applied": applied,
1474:                     "defined": n_def, "retired": n_ret,
1475:                     "observer": observer, "signed": applied,
1476:                     "changes": change_rows,
1477:                 })
1478:                 return
1479:             from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
1480:             if applied:
1481:                 head_line = (
1482:                     f"✓ {target_path.stem}: reconciled — "
1483:                     f"{n_def} re-emitted, {n_ret} retired"
1484:                 )
1485:             else:
1486:                 head_line = f"✎ {target_path.stem}: file diverges from store head"
1487:             lines = [head_line]
1488:             for c in changes:
1489:                 mark = "−" if c.payload is None else ("+" if c.annotation == "added" else "~")
1490:                 lines.append(f"  {mark} {_decl_short(c.kind)}:{c.subject} ({c.annotation})")
1491:             if applied:
1492:                 lines.append(f"  observer: {observer} · signed")
1493:             else:
1494:                 lines.append("  run `loops store absorb` to reconcile")
1495:             paint(join_vertical(*(Block.text(ln, Style(dim=False)) for ln in lines)))
1496: 
1497:         # -n / --dry-run: the divergence surface. Read-only, exit 0.
1498:         if dry_run:
1499:             _render_divergence(applied=False)
1500:             return 0
1501: 
1502:         # Real path — atomic, signed re-emit of the changed subjects.
1503:         if not observer:
1504:             return _refuse(
1505:                 "cannot absorb — no observer resolved to sign as. A declaration "
1506:                 "edit must be signed (it enters the attestation tier); set up "
1507:                 "signing first (loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)."
1508:             )
1509: 
1510:         from custody import fact_signer_for
1511: 
1512:         from engine.sqlite_store import (
1513:             AmbiguousGenesis,
1514:             NoGenesis,
1515:             StaleDeclarationHead,
1516:             UnsignableEdit,
1517:         )
1518: 
1519:         try:
1520:             store.absorb_edit(
1521:                 changes,
1522:                 observer=observer,
1523:                 origin="",
1524:                 fact_signer=fact_signer_for(target_path),
1525:                 expected_head=expected_head,
1526:             )
1527:         except JsonlCanonicalUnsupported as exc:
1528:             return _refuse(str(exc))
1529:         except StaleDeclarationHead as exc:
1530:             return _refuse(f"{exc}")
1531:         except UnsignableEdit:
1532:             return _refuse(
1533:                 f"cannot absorb — no signing key for observer '{observer}'. A "
1534:                 "declaration edit must be signed; set up signing first "
1535:                 "(loops add <vertex> observer --keygen)."
1536:             )
1537:         except NoGenesis:
1538:             # TOCTOU: the lineage vanished between dispatch and here (not reachable
1539:             # in practice — stores are append-only).
1540:             return _refuse(
1541:                 "no genesis — the store's lineage is not open; run absorb to open it"
1542:             )
1543:         except AmbiguousGenesis as exc:
1544:             return _refuse(str(exc))
1545: 
1546:         _render_divergence(applied=True)
1547:         return 0
1548:     finally:
1549:         store.close()
1550: 
1551: 
1552: def _run_adopt(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
1553:     """Explicitly claim a genesis row as the store's own lineage (SPEC §9.2).
1554: 
1555:     The one legitimate path for an unmarked store (pre-marker era, or one
1556:     holding merged foreign genesis rows) to gain identity. Facts alone cannot
1557:     prove which genesis is self, so identity is ADOPTED under human intent —
1558:     never inferred (a singleton heuristic is the hijack vector this closes).
1559:     """
1560:     import argparse
1561: 
1562:     p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
1563:         prog="loops store adopt",
1564:         description="Claim a _decl.genesis row as this store's own lineage — "
1565:                     "stamps the store_meta.own_lineage identity marker. "
1566:                     "One-time ceremony for stores absorbed before the marker "
1567:                     "existed (or holding merged foreign genesis rows).",
1568:     )
1569:     if vertex_path is None:
1570:         p.add_argument("file", nargs="?", help="Vertex .vertex file or vertex name")
1571:     p.add_argument(
1572:         "--lineage", default=None,
1573:         help="Genesis id (or unique prefix) to adopt — required when several "
1574:              "genesis rows exist",
1575:     )
1576:     args = p.parse_args(argv)
1577: 
1578:     target_path = _resolve_target(getattr(args, "file", None), vertex_path).resolve()
1579:     db_path = _require_materialized_store(target_path)
1580: 
1581:     from atoms import Fact
1582:     from engine.jsonl_store import open_canonical_store
1583:     from engine.sqlite_store import AmbiguousGenesis, GenesisExists, NoGenesis
1584:     from painted import Block, Style, join_vertical, paint
1585: 
1586:     store = open_canonical_store(
1587:         resolve_canonical_path(target_path),
1588:         serialize=lambda f: f.to_dict(),
1589:         deserialize=Fact.from_dict,
1590:     )
1591:     try:
1592:         receipt = store.adopt_lineage(args.lineage)
1593:     except (GenesisExists, NoGenesis, AmbiguousGenesis) as exc:
1594:         paint(Block.text(f"✗ {target_path.stem}: {exc}", Style()), file=sys.stderr)
1595:         return 2
1596:     finally:
1597:         store.close()
1598: 
1599:     from datetime import datetime, timezone
1600:     when = datetime.fromtimestamp(receipt["ts"], tz=timezone.utc).isoformat()
1601:     lines = [
1602:         f"✓ {target_path.stem}: lineage adopted — {receipt['lineage']}",
1603:         f"  genesis by {receipt['observer']} at {when}",
1604:         f"  ({receipt['genesis_count']} genesis row(s) present; this one is now self)",
1605:     ]
1606:     paint(join_vertical(*(Block.text(ln, Style(dim=False)) for ln in lines)))
1607:     return 0
1608: 
1609: 
1610: def _run_store_ticks(argv: list[str], *, vertex_path: Path | None = None) -> int:
1611:     """Read a store's tick series — the attestation chain surface.
1612: 
1613:     Default projection is density (items/facts/delta per window).
1614:     ``--chain`` projects the stored attestation envelope per tick: chain
1615:     linkage, signature presence, and the window cursor. This is a READ of
1616:     the stored flags, not a re-verification — ``store verify`` walks the
1617:     chain in append order and checks integrity; ``store ticks`` lists what
1618:     each tick's envelope says. Requires a ``.vertex`` target: the tick
1619:     series resolves through the vertex name and store. ``--since`` narrows
1620:     the window; the default is the full chain (genesis and the legacy-era
