380: @dataclass(frozen=True)
381: class RuntimeCapture:
382:     """Closed-handle evidence and construction recipe for exact-H planning.
383: 
384:     Mutable runtime objects stay private and are copied before every planning
385:     operation. Public declaration, document, fact, tick, and source accessors
386:     likewise return copies, so caller mutation cannot alter a later plan.
387:     """
388: 
389:     basis: ReadBasis
390:     custodian: str
391:     max_atomic_records: int | None
392:     evaluated_at: float
393:     _effective_declaration: VertexFile = field(repr=False, compare=False)
394:     _locator: VertexFile = field(repr=False, compare=False)
395:     _declaration_anchor: Any = field(repr=False, compare=False)
396:     _declaration_documents: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
397:     _facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
398:     _ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
399:     _runtime_facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
400:     _runtime_ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
401:     _candidate_template: Vertex = field(repr=False, compare=False)
402:     _post_boundary_candidate_template: Vertex | None = field(
403:         default=None, repr=False, compare=False
404:     )
405:     _sources: tuple[Any, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False, compare=False)
406:     _pending_boundaries: tuple[Any, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False, compare=False)
407:     _signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = field(
408:         default=None, repr=False, compare=False
409:     )
410: 
411:     @property
412:     def effective_declaration(self) -> VertexFile:
413:         from .declaration import effective_declaration_from_documents
414: 
415:         return effective_declaration_from_documents(
416:             self._declaration_documents,
417:             self._locator,
418:             verify_pins=False,
419:         )
420: 
421:     @property
422:     def declaration_documents(self) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
423:         return copy.deepcopy(self._declaration_documents)
424: 
425:     @property
426:     def facts(self) -> tuple[SnapshotFact, ...]:
427:         return copy.deepcopy(self._facts)
428: 
429:     @property
430:     def ticks(self) -> tuple[SnapshotTick, ...]:
431:         return copy.deepcopy(self._ticks)
432: 
433:     @property
434:     def runtime_facts(self) -> tuple[SnapshotFact, ...]:
435:         """Facts eligible to establish the current runtime epoch's state."""
436:         return copy.deepcopy(self._runtime_facts)
437: 
438:     @property
439:     def sources(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
440:         return copy.deepcopy(self._sources)
441: 
442:     @property
443:     def pending_boundaries(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
444:         return copy.deepcopy(self._pending_boundaries)
445: 
446:     def _locator_copy(self) -> VertexFile:
447:         return _capture_locator_ingress(self._locator)
448: 
449:     def _candidate_copy(self, *, after_pending_boundaries: bool = False) -> Vertex:
450:         template = (
451:             self._post_boundary_candidate_template
452:             if after_pending_boundaries and self._post_boundary_candidate_template is not None
453:             else self._candidate_template
454:         )
455:         return template.detached_copy()
456: 
457: 
458: def _capture_locator_ingress(locator: VertexFile) -> VertexFile:
459:     """Detach the residence and ingress values capture may use after return."""
460:     from lang.ast import InlineSource, SourcesBlock, VertexFile
461: 
462:     def frozen_ast(cls: type, **fields: object) -> Any:
463:         # lang.ast deliberately implements its own frozen slots rather than
464:         # dataclasses. Constructing through object.__new__ is the narrow copy
465:         # primitive that neither invokes its custom setter nor shares a caller
466:         # object; all supplied values below are immutable scalars/tuples.
467:         instance = object.__new__(cls)
468:         for name, value in fields.items():
469:             object.__setattr__(instance, name, value)
470:         return instance
471: 
472:     blocks = tuple(
473:         frozen_ast(
474:             SourcesBlock,
475:             mode=block.mode,
476:             sources=tuple(
477:                 frozen_ast(
478:                     InlineSource,
479:                     command=source.command,
480:                     kind=source.kind,
481:                     observer=source.observer,
482:                     every=source.every,
483:                     on=source.on,
484:                     format=source.format,
485:                     timeout=source.timeout,
486:                     origin=source.origin,
487:                     env=tuple(source.env),
488:                     parse=tuple(source.parse),
489:                     path=source.path,
490:                 )
491:                 for source in block.sources
492:             ),
493:         )
494:         for block in (locator.sources_blocks or ())
495:     )
496:     return frozen_ast(
497:         VertexFile,
498:         name=locator.name,
499:         loops={},
500:         store=locator.store,
501:         store_backend=locator.store_backend,
502:         store_location=locator.store_location,
503:         sources_blocks=blocks or None,
504:         path=locator.path,
505:     )
506: 
507: 
508: def _json_default(value: object) -> object:
509:     from types import MappingProxyType
510: 
511:     if isinstance(value, MappingProxyType):
512:         return dict(value)
513:     raise TypeError(f"{type(value).__name__} is not JSON serializable")
514: 
515: 
516: def _payload_text(payload: object) -> str:
517:     """Match the live SQLite writer's fresh-row payload encoding."""
518:     return json.dumps(payload, default=_json_default)
519: 
520: 
521: class _BatchSnapshot:
522:     """One in-memory extension of a captured snapshot during planning only."""
523: 
524:     def __init__(
525:         self,
526:         base: QuerySnapshot | RuntimeCapture,
527:         facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
528:         ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
529:     ) -> None:
530:         if isinstance(base, RuntimeCapture):
531:             projected = base.basis.projected_through
532:             self.represented = (
533:                 None
534:                 if projected is None
535:                 else Watermark(lineage=projected.lineage, ordinal=projected.ordinal)
536:             )
537:             self.view_generation = base.basis.view_generation
538:             self.declaration_anchor = base._declaration_anchor
539:         else:
540:             self.represented = base.represented
541:             self.view_generation = base.view_generation
542:             self.declaration_anchor = base.declaration_anchor
543:         self._facts = facts
544:         self._ticks = ticks
545: 
546:     def facts(self, request: FactRequest):
547:         from .arrival_contract import FactPage
548: 
549:         rows = self._facts
550:         if request.fact_id is not None:
551:             rows = tuple(row for row in rows if row.id == request.fact_id)
552:         return FactPage(rows, None, False, request.order)
553: 
554:     def ticks(self, request: TickRequest) -> tuple[SnapshotTick, ...]:
555:         return self._ticks
556: 
557:     def summary(self, request: SummaryRequest) -> Any:
558:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused(
559:             "summary reads are unavailable on a pending batch planning snapshot"
560:         )
561: 
562:     def search(self, request: SearchRequest) -> SearchPage:
563:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("search is unavailable on a pending batch planning snapshot")
564: 
565:     def close(self) -> None:
566:         # The outer preparation scope owns the real snapshot. A synthetic
567:         # extension is an immutable view over tuples and holds no resource.
568:         return None
569: 
570: 
571: def _fact_from_draft(draft: RecordDraft, ordinal: int, seq: int) -> SnapshotFact:
572:     body = draft.body
573:     return SnapshotFact(
574:         id=body["id"],
575:         kind=body["kind"],
576:         ts=body["ts"],
577:         observer=body["observer"],
578:         origin=body["origin"],
579:         payload=json.loads(body["payload"]),
580:         arrival_ordinal=ordinal,
581:         arrival_seq=seq,
582:         payload_text=body["payload"],
583:         signature=body.get("signature"),
584:     )
585: 
586: 
587: def _tick_from_draft(draft: RecordDraft, ordinal: int, seq: int) -> SnapshotTick:
588:     body = draft.body
589:     return SnapshotTick(
590:         id=body["id"],
591:         name=body["name"],
592:         ts=body["ts"],
593:         since=body["since"],
594:         origin=body["origin"],
595:         payload=json.loads(body["payload"]),
596:         arrival_ordinal=ordinal,
597:         arrival_seq=seq,
598:         payload_text=body["payload"],
599:         prev_hash=body["prev_hash"],
600:         window_start=body["window_start"],
601:         fact_cursor=body["fact_cursor"],
602:         window_hash=body["window_hash"],
603:         signature=body.get("signature"),
604:     )
605: 
606: 
607: def _window_hash(
608:     facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
609:     *,
610:     start: str,
611:     appended: tuple[object, ...] | None = None,
612: ) -> str:
613:     """The live tick window hash over exact snapshot rows plus this fact."""
614:     import hashlib
615: 
616:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
617:     if appended is not None and start == appended[0]:
618:         return digest.hexdigest()
619:     start_at = -1
620:     if start:
621:         for index, fact in enumerate(facts):
622:             if fact.id == start:
623:                 start_at = index
624:                 break
625:         else:
626:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"tick window start {start!r} is absent from the snapshot")
627:     for fact in facts[start_at + 1 :]:
628:         if fact.payload_text is None:
629:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact fact payload text")
630:         digest.update(
631:             fact_row_hash(
632:                 (
633:                     fact.id,
634:                     fact.kind,
635:                     fact.ts,
636:                     fact.observer,
637:                     fact.origin,
638:                     fact.payload_text,
639:                     fact.signature,
640:                 )
641:             ).encode()
642:         )
643:     if appended is not None:
644:         digest.update(fact_row_hash(appended).encode())
645:     return digest.hexdigest()
646: 
647: 
648: def _current_tick_context(
649:     ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...], *, current_fact_id: str
650: ) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
651:     """Return the predecessor identity and next tick's window start."""
652:     if not ticks:
653:         return None, ""
654:     previous = max(ticks, key=lambda tick: (tick.arrival_ordinal, tick.arrival_seq))
655:     if previous.payload_text is None:
656:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact tick payload text")
657:     previous_hash = tick_row_hash(
658:         (
659:             previous.id,
660:             previous.name,
661:             previous.ts,
662:             previous.since,
663:             previous.origin,
664:             previous.payload_text,
665:             previous.prev_hash,
666:             previous.window_start,
667:             previous.fact_cursor,
668:             previous.window_hash,
669:             previous.signature,
670:         )
671:     )
672:     if previous.window_hash is None:
673:         # The live store starts a new chain epoch at its current fact edge,
674:         # which is this just-planned fact; its first window is empty.
675:         return previous_hash, current_fact_id
676:     if previous.fact_cursor is None:
677:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("chained tick has no fact cursor")
678:     return previous_hash, previous.fact_cursor
679: 
680: 
681: def _existing_fact_plan(
682:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
683:     basis: ReadBasis,
684:     fact: Fact,
685:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
686:     fact_id: str | None,
687:     *,
688:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
689:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
690: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan | None:
691:     """Recognize an exact retry before any new-fact admission decision."""
692:     if fact_id is None:
693:         return None
694:     chosen_id = fact_id
695:     existing = next(
696:         (
697:             row
698:             for row in snapshot.facts(
699:                 FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")
700:             ).items
701:             if row.id == chosen_id
702:         ),
703:         None,
704:     )
705:     if existing is None:
706:         return None
707:     payload_text = _payload_text(fact.payload)
708:     session = credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
709:     inner_signature = _author_signature(
710:         credentials,
711:         session,
712:         signing_context,
713:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
714:         observer=fact.observer,
715:         domain=SigningDomain.FACT,
716:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
717:         digest=fact_commitment_hash(
718:             fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text
719:         ),
720:         legacy_signer=credentials.fact_signer,
721:     )
722:     if existing.payload_text is None:
723:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact fact payload text")
724:     if (
725:         existing.kind,
726:         existing.ts,
727:         existing.observer,
728:         existing.origin,
729:         existing.payload_text,
730:         existing.signature,
731:     ) != (fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text, inner_signature):
732:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"fact id {chosen_id!r} already exists with different content")
733:     return OrdinaryWritePlan(
734:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
735:         fact_id=chosen_id,
736:         tick_id=None,
737:         drafts=(),
738:         already_present=True,
739:         credential_bindings=session.evidence,
740:     )
741: 
742: 
743: def _ensure_current_basis(snapshot: QuerySnapshot, basis: ReadBasis) -> None:
744:     if basis.projected_through != basis.captured_head:
745:         raise ProjectionBehind("ordinary writes require a current projection snapshot")
746:     if snapshot.represented is None:
747:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary writes require a represented projection")
748:     if snapshot.represented.lineage != basis.captured_head.lineage:
749:         raise NotAuthority("snapshot lineage differs from the captured ledger head")
750: 
751: 
752: def _current_write_basis(
753:     ledger: AttestedLedger, snapshot: QuerySnapshot, captured: Head
754: ) -> ReadBasis:
755:     """Complete a CURRENT query watermark without mistaking an advance for lag."""
756:     from .arrival_contract import ReadBasis
757: 
758:     watermark = snapshot.represented
759:     if watermark is None:
760:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a projection watermark")
761:     projected = complete_projection_custody(
762:         ledger,
763:         captured=captured,
764:         represented=watermark,
765:         lineage_refusal=NotAuthority,
766:         conflict_refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
767:     )
768:     if projected.ordinal < captured.ordinal:
769:         raise ProjectionBehind("CURRENT snapshot did not reach the captured head")
770:     return ReadBasis(
771:         lineage=captured.lineage,
772:         captured_head=captured,
773:         projected_through=projected,
774:         view_generation=snapshot.view_generation,
775:     )
776: 
777: 
778: def _require_signing_context(
779:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
780:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
781:     *,
782:     captured_head: Head,
783: ) -> CapturedSigningContext:
784:     if context is None or context.captured_head != captured_head:
785:         raise CredentialBindingRefused(
786:             "mapped signing requires authorization evidence from the captured head",
787:             captured_head=captured_head,
788:             use_position=captured_head.ordinal + 1,
789:         )
790:     return context
791: 
792: 
793: def _author_signature(
794:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
795:     session: CredentialResolutionSession,
796:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
797:     *,
798:     captured_head: Head,
799:     observer: str,
800:     domain: SigningDomain,
801:     purpose: CredentialPurpose,
802:     digest: str,
803:     legacy_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None,
804:     required: bool = False,
805: ) -> str | None:
806:     if not credentials.mapped:
807:         if legacy_signer is None:
808:             if required:
809:                 raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"missing {domain.value} signer")
810:             return None
811:         signature = legacy_signer(observer, digest)
812:         if required and not signature:
813:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"missing {domain.value} signature")
814:         return signature
815:     resolved_context = _require_signing_context(
816:         credentials, context, captured_head=captured_head
817:     )
818:     request = CredentialRequest(
819:         namespace=credentials.binding_namespace,
820:         observer=observer,
821:         domain=domain,
822:         purpose=purpose,
823:     )
824:     return session.sign(
825:         credentials,
826:         request,
827:         digest,
828:         context=resolved_context,
829:         authorized_keys=resolved_context.keys_for_author(observer),
830:         required=required,
831:     )
832: 
833: 
834: def _tick_signature(
835:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
836:     session: CredentialResolutionSession,
837:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
838:     *,
839:     captured_head: Head,
840:     digest: str,
841:     signed_era: bool,
842: ) -> str | None:
843:     if not credentials.mapped:
844:         signature = (
845:             credentials.tick_signer(digest)
846:             if credentials.tick_signer is not None
847:             else None
848:         )
849:         if signature is None and signed_era:
850:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("refusing an unsigned tick in the signed tick era")
851:         return signature
852:     resolved_context = _require_signing_context(
853:         credentials, context, captured_head=captured_head
854:     )
855:     if credentials.receipt_observer is None:
856:         if signed_era:
857:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
858:                 "missing-required",
859:                 captured_head=captured_head,
860:                 use_position=resolved_context.use_position,
861:             )
862:         return None
863:     request = CredentialRequest(
864:         namespace=credentials.binding_namespace,
865:         observer=credentials.receipt_observer,
866:         domain=SigningDomain.TICK,
867:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.RECEIPT,
868:     )
869:     return session.sign(
870:         credentials,
871:         request,
872:         digest,
873:         context=resolved_context,
874:         authorized_keys=resolved_context.receipt_keys,
875:         required=signed_era,
876:     )
877: 
878: 
879: def _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
880:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
881:     basis: ReadBasis,
882:     locator: VertexFile,
883:     *,
884:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
885:     wire_genesis_record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
886: ) -> tuple[
887:     Vertex,
888:     VertexFile,
889:     tuple[dict[str, Any], ...],
890:     tuple[Any, ...],
891:     tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
892:     tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
893:     tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
894:     tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
895: ]:
896:     """Build a fresh, storeless candidate from this exact bounded snapshot.
897: 
898:     The locator contributes only ingress/residence fields. Effective documents,
899:     facts, and ticks come from the one CURRENT query snapshot, so no mutable
900:     caller vertex or legacy store can decide the next boundary.
901:     """
902:     _ensure_current_basis(snapshot, basis)
903:     facts = snapshot.facts(FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")).items
904:     from .declaration import (
905:         Unhistorized,
906:         effective_declaration_from_documents,
907:         resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot,
908:         validate_arrival_declaration_anchor,
909:         validate_arrival_runtime_identity,
910:     )
911: 
912:     validate_arrival_declaration_anchor(snapshot.declaration_anchor, basis.captured_head)
913:     documents = resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot(snapshot.declaration_anchor, facts)
914:     if documents is None or isinstance(documents, Unhistorized):
915:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires historized effective declarations")
916:     effective = effective_declaration_from_documents(documents, locator)
917:     if effective.vertices or effective.discover or effective.combine is not None:
918:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("multi-store child execution is not in stage 3A")
919:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
920:         effective.name,
921:         effective.loops,
922:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
923:     )
924:     from .compiler import (
925:         CompiledVertex,
926:         compile_parse_pipelines,
927:         compile_sources,
928:         compile_sources_block,
929:         map_boundary,
930:         map_vertex_file,
931:         materialize_vertex,
932:     )
933: 
934:     base_dir = locator.path.parent if locator.path is not None else Path.cwd()
935:     sources, template_specs = compile_sources(effective, base_dir)
936:     for block in effective.sources_blocks or ():
937:         sources.append(compile_sources_block(block, effective.name))
938:     specs = map_vertex_file(effective)
939:     specs.update(template_specs)
940:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
941:         effective.name,
942:         specs,
943:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
944:     )
945:     if sources:
946:         from .executor import validate_dependency_graph
947: 
948:         validate_dependency_graph(sources)
949:     all_ticks = tuple(snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=float("-inf"))))
950:     continuity_ticks = all_ticks
951:     from .declaration import runtime_epoch_from_anchor
952: 
953:     anchor = snapshot.declaration_anchor
954:     runtime_epoch = runtime_epoch_from_anchor(anchor, wire_record=wire_genesis_record)
955:     runtime_facts = tuple(
956:         fact for fact in facts
957:         if runtime_epoch.start_ordinal is None
958:         or fact.arrival_ordinal > runtime_epoch.start_ordinal
959:     )
960:     # Strict hydration historically used TickRequest() (since=0); keep that
961:     # execution policy while full `all_ticks` remains custody-chain evidence.
962:     # Fresh epochs instead select by receipt ordinal, including negative time.
963:     runtime_ticks = tuple(
964:         tick for tick in all_ticks
965:         if (
966:             tick.ts >= 0.0
967:             if runtime_epoch.start_ordinal is None
968:             else tick.arrival_ordinal > runtime_epoch.start_ordinal
969:         )
970:     )
971:     from .arrival_boundary_continuity import (
972:         BoundaryContinuityConflict,
973:         analyze_boundary_continuity,
974:         collect_verified_parameter_rows,
975:     )
976: 
977:     verified_params = collect_verified_parameter_rows(
978:         snapshot.declaration_anchor,
979:         facts,
980:         target_documents=documents,
981:         base_dir=base_dir,
982:     )
983:     try:
984:         analyze_boundary_continuity(
985:             snapshot.declaration_anchor,
986:             facts,
987:             continuity_ticks,
988:             target_documents=documents,
989:             verified_params=verified_params,
990:             compiled_loop_names=set(specs) | {"cite"},
991:             runtime_epoch=runtime_epoch,
992:         )
993:     except BoundaryContinuityConflict as exc:
994:         raise BoundaryContinuityRefused(
995:             issue=exc.issue,
996:             basis=basis,
997:             effective_declaration=effective,
998:             cause=exc,
999:         ) from exc
1000:     compiled = CompiledVertex(
1001:         name=effective.name,
1002:         specs=specs,
1003:         children={},
1004:         routes=effective.routes,
1005:         store=effective.store,
1006:         path=effective.path,
1007:         boundary=tuple(map_boundary(boundary) for boundary in effective.boundary),
1008:         parse_pipelines=compile_parse_pipelines(effective),
1009:         strict=effective.strict,
1010:         sources=sources or None,
1011:         template_specs=template_specs or None,
1012:     )
1013:     candidate = materialize_vertex(compiled, fold_overrides=fold_overrides, attach_store=False)
1014:     candidate.hydrate_snapshot(runtime_facts, runtime_ticks)
1015:     return (
1016:         candidate,
1017:         effective,
1018:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(documents)),
1019:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(sources)),
1020:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(facts)),
1021:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(all_ticks)),
1022:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(runtime_facts)),
1023:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(runtime_ticks)),
1024:     )
1025: 
1026: 
1027: def _hydrate_effective_arrival_candidate(
1028:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
1029:     basis: ReadBasis,
1030:     locator: VertexFile,
1031:     *,
1032:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1033:     wire_genesis_record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
1034: ) -> tuple[Vertex, VertexFile]:
1035:     candidate, effective, _documents, _sources, _facts, _ticks, _runtime_facts, _runtime_ticks = (
1036:         _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
1037:             snapshot,
1038:             basis,
1039:             locator,
1040:             fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1041:             wire_genesis_record=wire_genesis_record,
1042:         )
1043:     )
1044:     return candidate, effective
1045: 
1046: 
1047: def hydrate_arrival_candidate(
1048:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
1049:     basis: ReadBasis,
1050:     locator: VertexFile,
1051:     *,
1052:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1053:     wire_genesis_record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
1054: ) -> Vertex:
1055:     """Build the supported storeless candidate from this bounded snapshot."""
1056:     candidate, _effective = _hydrate_effective_arrival_candidate(
1057:         snapshot,
1058:         basis,
1059:         locator,
1060:         fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1061:         wire_genesis_record=wire_genesis_record,
1062:     )
1063:     return candidate
1064: 
1065: 
1066: def plan_ordinary_write(
1067:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
1068:     basis: ReadBasis,
1069:     vertex: Vertex,
1070:     fact: Fact,
1071:     *,
1072:     grant: Grant | None,
1073:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1074:     custodian: str,
1075:     fact_id: str | None = None,
1076:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
1077:     _mutate_candidate: bool = False,
1078:     _signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
1079:     _credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
1080: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan:
1081:     """Internal, caller-responsible planner for one local fact and tick.
1082: 
1083:     ``vertex`` must be a detached reconstruction of this exact snapshot.
1084:     Supported consumers use :func:`prepare_ordinary_write`, which creates it
1085:     through :func:`hydrate_arrival_candidate`; this lower-level seam exists
1086:     for focused engine composition and must not be offered as a coherent
1087:     writer from a stale caller-owned vertex.
1088:     """
1089:     _ensure_current_basis(snapshot, basis)
1090:     if getattr(vertex, "_store", None) is not None:
1091:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary planning requires a detached vertex")
1092:     if getattr(vertex, "_has_children", False):
1093:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("multi-store child execution is not in stage 3A")
1094:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
1095: 
1096:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
1097:         vertex.name,
1098:         vertex.kinds,
1099:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
1100:     )
1101:     anchor = snapshot.declaration_anchor
1102:     if anchor.own_lineage is None or anchor.genesis is None:
1103:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a declaration anchor")
1104:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_declaration_anchor
1105: 
1106:     validate_arrival_declaration_anchor(anchor, basis.captured_head)
1107: 
1108:     credential_session = _credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
1109:     existing_plan = _existing_fact_plan(
1110:         snapshot,
1111:         basis,
1112:         fact,
1113:         credentials,
1114:         fact_id,
1115:         signing_context=_signing_context,
1116:         credential_session=credential_session,
1117:     )
1118:     if existing_plan is not None:
1119:         return existing_plan
1120:     facts = snapshot.facts(FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")).items
1121:     # The neutral request is receipt-ordered, but keep this safety boundary at
1122:     # the chain calculation: window membership is coordinates, never a
1123:     # backend's event-time or identifier presentation order.
1124:     facts = tuple(sorted(facts, key=lambda row: (row.arrival_ordinal, row.arrival_seq)))
1125:     ticks = snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=float("-inf")))
1126:     chosen_id = fact_id if fact_id is not None else str(ULID())
1127:     payload_text = _payload_text(fact.payload)
1128:     inner_signature = _author_signature(
1129:         credentials,
1130:         credential_session,
1131:         _signing_context,
1132:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1133:         observer=fact.observer,
1134:         domain=SigningDomain.FACT,
1135:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1136:         digest=fact_commitment_hash(
1137:             fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text
1138:         ),
1139:         legacy_signer=credentials.fact_signer,
1140:     )
1141:     fact_row = (
1142:         chosen_id,
1143:         fact.kind,
1144:         fact.ts,
1145:         fact.observer,
1146:         fact.origin,
1147:         payload_text,
1148:         inner_signature,
1149:     )
1150:     candidate = vertex if _mutate_candidate else vertex.detached_copy()
1151:     receipt = candidate.plan_receive_receipt(
1152:         fact,
1153:         grant,
1154:         id_override=chosen_id,
1155:         admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1156:     )
1157:     if not receipt.stored:
1158:         raise _AdmissionPlanRefused("admission rejected the fact before append")
1159:     fact_body = body_of_fact_row(fact_row)
1160:     outer_signature = _author_signature(
1161:         credentials,
1162:         credential_session,
1163:         _signing_context,
1164:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1165:         observer=fact.observer,
1166:         domain=SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
1167:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1168:         digest=content_commitment(
1169:             "fact", fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, fact_body
1170:         ),
1171:         legacy_signer=credentials.arrival_signer,
1172:     )
1173:     drafts = [
1174:         RecordDraft(
1175:             kind="fact",
1176:             authored_at=fact.ts,
1177:             observer=fact.observer,
1178:             origin=fact.origin,
1179:             body=fact_body,
1180:             signature=outer_signature,
1181:         )
1182:     ]
1183:     tick_id: str | None = None
1184:     if receipt.tick is not None:
1185:         previous_hash, window_start = _current_tick_context(ticks, current_fact_id=chosen_id)
1186:         tick = receipt.tick
1187:         tick_id = str(ULID())
1188:         if any(existing.id == tick_id for existing in ticks):
1189:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"generated tick id {tick_id!r} already exists")
1190:         tick_payload_text = _payload_text(tick.payload)
1191:         tick_row = (
1192:             tick_id,
1193:             tick.name,
1194:             tick.ts.timestamp(),
1195:             None if tick.since is None else tick.since.timestamp(),
1196:             tick.origin,
1197:             tick_payload_text,
1198:             previous_hash,
1199:             window_start,
1200:             chosen_id,
1201:             _window_hash(facts, start=window_start, appended=fact_row),
1202:         )
1203:         signed_era = any(
1204:             existing.signature is not None and existing.window_hash is not None
1205:             for existing in ticks
1206:         )
1207:         tick_signature = _tick_signature(
1208:             credentials,
1209:             credential_session,
1210:             _signing_context,
1211:             captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1212:             digest=tick_commitment_hash(tick_row),
1213:             signed_era=signed_era,
1214:         )
1215:         tick_body = body_of_tick_row((*tick_row, tick_signature))
1216:         drafts.append(
1217:             RecordDraft(
1218:                 kind="tick",
1219:                 authored_at=tick.ts.timestamp(),
1220:                 observer=custodian,
1221:                 origin=tick.origin,
1222:                 body=tick_body,
1223:                 signature=None,
1224:             )
1225:         )
1226:     return OrdinaryWritePlan(
1227:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1228:         fact_id=chosen_id,
1229:         tick_id=tick_id,
1230:         drafts=tuple(drafts),
1231:         tick_run=None if receipt.tick is None else receipt.tick.run,
1232:         credential_bindings=credential_session.evidence,
1233:     )
1234: 
1235: 
1236: def _custodian_from_verified_genesis(ledger: AttestedLedger) -> str:
1237:     """Return the physical lineage's tick custodian from its verified record 0."""
1238:     genesis = ledger.read(0)
1239:     if genesis.get("k") != "genesis":
1240:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ledger record 0 is not a physical genesis")
1241:     observer = genesis.get("observer")
1242:     if not isinstance(observer, str) or not observer:
1243:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("physical genesis has no custodian observer")
1244:     return observer
1245: 
1246: 
1247: def capture_runtime(
1248:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1249:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1250:     locator: VertexFile,
1251:     *,
1252:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1253:     source_mode: bool = False,
1254:     evaluated_at: float | None = None,
1255:     credentials: WriteCredentials | None = None,
1256: ) -> RuntimeCapture:
1257:     """Capture one immutable, closed-handle Authority/CURRENT runtime view."""
1258:     from .arrival_contract import Profile, ProjectionRequirement
1259: 
1260:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1261:         raise NotAuthority("runtime capture requires an Authority descriptor role")
1262:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1263:     snapshot = None
1264:     try:
1265:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1266:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1267:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1268:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1269:             if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis) and comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1270:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1271:             if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1272:                 raise comparison.refusal
1273:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("runtime capture requires a minted compared ledger")
1274:         captured = comparison.presented
1275:         snapshot = query.open_snapshot(
1276:             captured_head=captured, requirement=ProjectionRequirement.CURRENT
1277:         )
1278:         basis = _current_write_basis(ledger, snapshot, captured)
1279:         candidate, effective, documents, sources, facts, ticks, runtime_facts, runtime_ticks = (
1280:             _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
1281:                 snapshot, basis, locator, fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1282:                 wire_genesis_record=(
1283:                     None if snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis is None
1284:                     else ledger.read(snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis.arrival_ordinal)
1285:                 ),
1286:             )
1287:         )
1288:         at = time.time() if evaluated_at is None else evaluated_at
1289:         post_boundary_candidate = candidate.detached_copy() if source_mode else None
1290:         pending = (
1291:             post_boundary_candidate.plan_pending_boundaries(
1292:                 runtime_facts, runtime_ticks, evaluated_at=at
1293:             )
1294:             if post_boundary_candidate is not None
1295:             else ()
1296:         )
1297:         captured_locator = _capture_locator_ingress(locator)
1298:         signing_context = None
1299:         if credentials is not None and credentials.mapped:
1300:             from .arrival import key_registry_from_records
1301: 
1302:             verifier = credentials.signature_verifier
1303:             if verifier is None:  # guarded by WriteCredentials construction
1304:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1305:                     "mapped signing requires a signature verifier",
1306:                     captured_head=captured,
1307:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1308:                 )
1309:             try:
1310:                 key_registry, _key_rows = key_registry_from_records(
1311:                     ledger.scan(through=captured),
1312:                     lambda key, signature, digest: verifier(
1313:                         SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, key, signature, digest
1314:                     ),
1315:                 )
1316:             except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
1317:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1318:                     exc.reason,
1319:                     request=exc.request,
1320:                     captured_head=captured,
1321:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1322:                     evidence=exc.evidence,
1323:                 ) from exc
1324:             except Exception as exc:
1325:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1326:                     "key-history-unverified",
1327:                     captured_head=captured,
1328:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1329:                 ) from exc
1330:             author_keys = tuple(
1331:                 (
1332:                     observer,
1333:                     tuple(
1334:                         key
1335:                         for key, _introduced in key_registry.keys_valid_at(
1336:                             observer, captured.ordinal + 1
1337:                         )
1338:                     ),
1339:                 )
1340:                 for observer in sorted(key_registry.introductions)
1341:             )
1342:             receipt_keys = tuple(
1343:                 observer.key
1344:                 for observer in (effective.observers or ())
1345:                 if isinstance(observer.key, str) and observer.key
1346:             )
1347:             signing_context = CapturedSigningContext(
1348:                 captured_head=captured,
1349:                 author_keys=author_keys,
1350:                 receipt_keys=receipt_keys,
1351:             )
1352:         return RuntimeCapture(
1353:             basis=basis,
1354:             custodian=_custodian_from_verified_genesis(ledger),
1355:             max_atomic_records=ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records,
1356:             evaluated_at=at,
1357:             _effective_declaration=effective,
1358:             _locator=captured_locator,
1359:             _declaration_anchor=copy.deepcopy(snapshot.declaration_anchor),
1360:             _declaration_documents=copy.deepcopy(documents),
1361:             _facts=copy.deepcopy(facts),
1362:             _ticks=copy.deepcopy(ticks),
1363:             _runtime_facts=copy.deepcopy(runtime_facts),
1364:             _runtime_ticks=copy.deepcopy(runtime_ticks),
1365:             _candidate_template=candidate.detached_copy(),
1366:             _post_boundary_candidate_template=(
1367:                 None
1368:                 if post_boundary_candidate is None
1369:                 else post_boundary_candidate.detached_copy()
1370:             ),
1371:             _sources=copy.deepcopy(sources),
1372:             _pending_boundaries=copy.deepcopy(tuple(pending)),
1373:             _signing_context=signing_context,
1374:         )
1375:     finally:
1376:         if snapshot is not None:
1377:             _close_quietly(snapshot)
1378:         _close_quietly(query)
1379:         _close_quietly(ledger)
1380: 
1381: 
1382: def prepare_ordinary_write(
1383:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1384:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1385:     locator: VertexFile,
1386:     fact: Fact,
1387:     *,
1388:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1389:     fact_id: str | None = None,
1390:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1391:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
1392: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan:
1393:     """Capture a CURRENT basis and prepare one retryable ordinary write.
1394: 
1395:     The writer opens its own registry handles because a read consumer never
1396:     receives append capability.  It closes those handles before returning the
1397:     detached plan; execution later compares the same complete head under the
1398:     ledger fence and refuses any intervening append.
1399:     """
1400:     capture = capture_runtime(
1401:         registry,
1402:         descriptor,
1403:         locator,
1404:         fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1405:         credentials=credentials,
1406:     )
1407:     basis = capture.basis
1408:     effective = capture.effective_declaration
1409:     candidate = capture._candidate_copy()
1410:     snapshot = _BatchSnapshot(capture, capture._facts, capture._ticks)
1411:     credential_session = CredentialResolutionSession()
1412:     duplicate = _existing_fact_plan(
1413:         snapshot,
1414:         basis,
1415:         fact,
1416:         credentials,
1417:         fact_id,
1418:         signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1419:         credential_session=credential_session,
1420:     )
1421:     if duplicate is not None:
1422:         return replace(
1423:             duplicate,
1424:             effective_declaration=effective,
1425:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1426:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1427:         )
1428:     grant = None
1429:     try:
1430:         from .admission import AdmissionError, grant_for_observer
1431: 
1432:         grant = grant_for_observer(effective, fact.observer)
1433:         plan = plan_ordinary_write(
1434:             snapshot,
1435:             basis,
1436:             candidate,
1437:             fact,
1438:             grant=grant,
1439:             credentials=credentials,
1440:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1441:             fact_id=fact_id,
1442:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1443:             _signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1444:             _credential_session=credential_session,
1445:         )
1446:     except (AdmissionError, _AdmissionPlanRefused) as exc:
1447:         raise OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused(
1448:             effective_declaration=effective,
1449:             admission_grant=grant,
1450:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1451:             basis=basis,
1452:             fact=fact,
1453:             cause=exc,
1454:         ) from exc
1455:     return replace(
1456:         plan,
1457:         effective_declaration=effective,
1458:         admission_grant=grant,
1459:         custodian=capture.custodian,
1460:         admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1461:     )
1462: 
1463: 
1464: def execute_ordinary_write(
1465:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1466:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1467:     plan: OrdinaryWritePlan,
1468:     *,
1469:     after_commit: Callable[[Head], object] | None = None,
1470: ) -> CommittedWrite:
1471:     """Append a previously prepared plan once against its exact full head.
1472: 
1473:     Projection publication is deliberately not attempted in 3A.  A successful
1474:     result says only that custody committed; maintenance is an explicit later
1475:     operation.  ``after_commit`` is the optional injected maintenance seam:
1476:     it receives the durable ``Commit.after`` and cannot change the append
1477:     outcome.  ``NotWitnessed`` already carries the durable commit and is
1478:     intentionally propagated rather than relabeled as a refusal.
1479:     """
1480:     from .arrival_contract import Profile
1481: 
1482:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1483:         raise NotAuthority("ordinary writes require an Authority descriptor role")
1484:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1485:     try:
1486:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1487:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1488:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1489:         if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
1490:             if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1491:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1492:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a minted ledger")
1493:         if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1494:             raise comparison.refusal
1495:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1496:             raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
1497:         if plan.already_present:
1498:             return CommittedWrite(
1499:                 commit=None,
1500:                 fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1501:                 tick_id=None,
1502:                 projection=ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED,
1503:             )
1504:         if comparison.presented != plan.captured_head:
1505:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("captured head changed before append")
1506:         try:
1507:             commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, plan.drafts)
1508:         except NotWitnessed:
1509:             raise
1510:         except ContractRefusal:
1511:             raise
1512:         except Exception as exc:
1513:             raise WriteCommitUnknown(plan, exc) from exc
1514:         if after_commit is not None:
1515:             try:
1516:                 after_commit(commit.after)
1517:             except Exception as exc:
1518:                 raise PostCommitProjectionFailed(commit, plan, exc) from exc
1519:             projection = ProjectionOutcome.SYNCED
1520:         else:
1521:             projection = ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED
1522:         return CommittedWrite(
1523:             commit=commit,
1524:             fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1525:             tick_id=plan.tick_id,
1526:             projection=projection,
1527:         )
1528:     finally:
1529:         _close_quietly(query)
1530:         _close_quietly(ledger)
1531: 
1532: 
1533: def _pack_batch_drafts(
1534:     drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft],
1535:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1536:     *,
1537:     captured_head: Head | None = None,
1538:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
1539:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
1540: ) -> tuple[RecordDraft, ...]:
1541:     """Turn adjacent ordinary fact drafts into wire-valid same-author batches."""
1542:     packed: list[RecordDraft] = []
1543:     held: list[RecordDraft] = []
1544:     session = credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
1545: 
1546:     def flush() -> None:
1547:         if not held:
1548:             return
1549:         if len(held) == 1:
1550:             packed.append(held[0])
1551:         else:
1552:             first = held[0]
1553:             rows = [
1554:                 (
1555:                     row.body["id"],
1556:                     row.body["kind"],
1557:                     row.body["ts"],
1558:                     row.body["observer"],
1559:                     row.body["origin"],
1560:                     row.body["payload"],
1561:                     row.body.get("signature"),
1562:                 )
1563:                 for row in held
1564:             ]
1565:             body = body_of_batch(rows)
1566:             digest = content_commitment(
1567:                 "batch", first.authored_at, first.observer, first.origin, body
1568:             )
1569:             if credentials.mapped:
1570:                 if captured_head is None:
1571:                     raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1572:                         "mapped batch signing requires a captured head"
1573:                     )
1574:                 signature = _author_signature(
1575:                     credentials,
1576:                     session,
1577:                     signing_context,
1578:                     captured_head=captured_head,
1579:                     observer=first.observer,
1580:                     domain=SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
1581:                     purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1582:                     digest=digest,
1583:                     legacy_signer=None,
1584:                 )
1585:             else:
1586:                 signature = (
1587:                     credentials.arrival_signer(first.observer, digest)
1588:                     if credentials.arrival_signer is not None
1589:                     else None
1590:                 )
1591:             packed.append(
1592:                 RecordDraft(
1593:                     kind="batch",
1594:                     authored_at=first.authored_at,
1595:                     observer=first.observer,
1596:                     origin=first.origin,
1597:                     body=body,
1598:                     signature=signature,
1599:                 )
1600:             )
1601:         held.clear()
1602: 
1603:     for draft in drafts:
1604:         if draft.kind == "fact" and (not held or held[0].observer == draft.observer):
1605:             held.append(draft)
1606:             continue
1607:         flush()
1608:         if draft.kind == "fact":
1609:             held.append(draft)
1610:         else:
1611:             packed.append(draft)
1612:     flush()
1613:     return tuple(packed)
1614: 
1615: 
1616: def _pending_tick_draft(
1617:     tick: Any,
1618:     facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
1619:     ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
1620:     *,
1621:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1622:     custodian: str,
1623:     captured_head: Head,
1624:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
1625:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession,
1626: ) -> RecordDraft:
1627:     """Encode one captured pending boundary without re-evaluating it."""
1628:     if not facts:
1629:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("a pending boundary requires captured fact evidence")
1630:     current_fact = max(facts, key=lambda row: (row.arrival_ordinal, row.arrival_seq))
1631:     previous_hash, window_start = _current_tick_context(ticks, current_fact_id=current_fact.id)
1632:     tick_id = str(ULID())
1633:     payload_text = _payload_text(tick.payload)
1634:     row = (
1635:         tick_id,
1636:         tick.name,
1637:         tick.ts.timestamp(),
1638:         None if tick.since is None else tick.since.timestamp(),
1639:         tick.origin,
1640:         payload_text,
1641:         previous_hash,
1642:         window_start,
1643:         current_fact.id,
1644:         _window_hash(facts, start=window_start),
1645:     )
1646:     signed_era = any(
1647:         existing.signature is not None and existing.window_hash is not None for existing in ticks
1648:     )
1649:     signature = _tick_signature(
1650:         credentials,
1651:         credential_session,
1652:         signing_context,
1653:         captured_head=captured_head,
1654:         digest=tick_commitment_hash(row),
1655:         signed_era=signed_era,
1656:     )
1657:     return RecordDraft(
1658:         kind="tick",
1659:         authored_at=tick.ts.timestamp(),
1660:         observer=custodian,
1661:         origin=tick.origin,
1662:         body=body_of_tick_row((*row, signature)),
1663:         signature=None,
1664:     )
1665: 
1666: 
1667: def plan_batch_from_capture(
1668:     capture: RuntimeCapture,
1669:     items: Sequence[BatchFactInput],
1670:     *,
1671:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1672:     include_pending_boundaries: bool = False,
1673: ) -> BatchWritePlan:
1674:     """Plan one batch from frozen evidence without reopening the registry."""
1675:     from .admission import AdmissionError, grant_for_observer
1676: 
1677:     pending = capture._pending_boundaries if include_pending_boundaries else ()
1678:     if not items and not pending:
1679:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch requires at least one fact or pending boundary")
1680:     basis = capture.basis
1681:     effective = capture._effective_declaration
1682:     candidate = capture._candidate_copy(after_pending_boundaries=include_pending_boundaries)
1683:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
1684: 
1685:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
1686:         candidate.name,
1687:         candidate.kinds,
1688:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
1689:     )
1690:     facts = copy.deepcopy(capture._facts)
1691:     ticks = copy.deepcopy(capture._ticks)
1692:     logical: list[RecordDraft] = []
1693:     pending_ids: list[str] = []
1694:     pending_names: list[str] = []
1695:     pending_runs: list[str | None] = []
1696:     credential_session = CredentialResolutionSession()
1697:     for sequence, tick in enumerate(copy.deepcopy(pending)):
1698:         draft = _pending_tick_draft(
1699:             tick,
1700:             facts,
1701:             ticks,
1702:             credentials=credentials,
1703:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1704:             captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1705:             signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1706:             credential_session=credential_session,
1707:         )
1708:         logical.append(draft)
1709:         pending_ids.append(str(draft.body["id"]))
1710:         pending_names.append(str(draft.body["name"]))
1711:         pending_runs.append(tick.run)
1712:         ticks = (
1713:             *ticks,
1714:             _tick_from_draft(draft, basis.captured_head.ordinal + 1, sequence),
1715:         )
1716: 
1717:     results: list[BatchItemResult] = []
1718:     for index, item in enumerate(items):
1719:         synthetic = _BatchSnapshot(capture, facts, ticks)
1720:         try:
1721:             plan = _existing_fact_plan(
1722:                 synthetic,
1723:                 basis,
1724:                 item.fact,
1725:                 credentials,
1726:                 item.fact_id,
1727:                 signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1728:                 credential_session=credential_session,
1729:             )
1730:             if plan is None:
1731:                 grant = grant_for_observer(effective, item.fact.observer)
1732:                 plan = plan_ordinary_write(
1733:                     synthetic,
1734:                     basis,
1735:                     candidate,
1736:                     item.fact,
1737:                     grant=grant,
1738:                     credentials=credentials,
1739:                     custodian=capture.custodian,
1740:                     fact_id=item.fact_id,
1741:                     admit_undeclared=item.admit_undeclared,
1742:                     _mutate_candidate=True,
1743:                     _signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1744:                     _credential_session=credential_session,
1745:                 )
1746:         except (AdmissionError, _AdmissionPlanRefused) as exc:
1747:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1748:                 input_index=index,
1749:                 item=item,
1750:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1751:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1752:                 basis=basis,
1753:                 cause=exc,
1754:                 admission_refused=True,
1755:             ) from exc
1756:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
1757:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1758:                 input_index=index,
1759:                 item=item,
1760:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1761:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1762:                 basis=basis,
1763:                 cause=exc,
1764:                 admission_refused=False,
1765:             ) from exc
1766:         except RuntimeWriteRefused as exc:
1767:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1768:                 input_index=index,
1769:                 item=item,
1770:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1771:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1772:                 basis=basis,
1773:                 cause=exc,
1774:                 admission_refused=False,
1775:             ) from exc
1776:         tick_draft = next((draft for draft in plan.drafts if draft.kind == "tick"), None)
1777:         results.append(
1778:             BatchItemResult(
1779:                 fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1780:                 tick_id=plan.tick_id,
1781:                 tick_name=None if tick_draft is None else tick_draft.body["name"],
1782:                 already_present=plan.already_present,
1783:                 tick_run=plan.tick_run,
1784:             )
1785:         )
1786:         if plan.already_present:
1787:             continue
1788:         ordinal = basis.captured_head.ordinal + 1 + len(logical)
1789:         for sequence, draft in enumerate(plan.drafts):
1790:             if draft.kind == "fact":
1791:                 facts = (*facts, _fact_from_draft(draft, ordinal, sequence))
1792:             elif draft.kind == "tick":
1793:                 ticks = (*ticks, _tick_from_draft(draft, ordinal, sequence))
1794:         logical.extend(plan.drafts)
1795: 
1796:     packed = _pack_batch_drafts(
1797:         logical,
1798:         credentials,
1799:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1800:         signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1801:         credential_session=credential_session,
1802:     )
1803:     if capture.max_atomic_records is not None and len(packed) > capture.max_atomic_records:
1804:         raise AtomicLimitExceeded(
1805:             f"{len(packed)} packed records exceeds this backend's configured "
1806:             f"atomic limit of {capture.max_atomic_records}; the batch was refused before append"
1807:         )
1808:     return BatchWritePlan(
1809:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1810:         items=tuple(results),
1811:         drafts=packed,
1812:         effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1813:         custodian=capture.custodian,
1814:         pending_tick_ids=tuple(pending_ids),
1815:         pending_tick_names=tuple(pending_names),
1816:         pending_tick_runs=tuple(pending_runs),
1817:         credential_bindings=credential_session.evidence,
1818:     )
1819: 
1820: 
1821: def prepare_batch_write(
1822:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1823:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1824:     locator: VertexFile,
1825:     items: Sequence[BatchFactInput],
1826:     *,
1827:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1828:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1829: ) -> BatchWritePlan:
1830:     """Capture once, then prepare every item against that exact evidence."""
1831:     if not items:
1832:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch requires at least one fact")
1833:     capture = capture_runtime(
1834:         registry,
1835:         descriptor,
1836:         locator,
1837:         fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1838:         credentials=credentials,
1839:     )
1840:     return plan_batch_from_capture(capture, items, credentials=credentials)
1841: 
1842: 
1843: def execute_batch_write(
1844:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1845:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1846:     plan: BatchWritePlan,
1847:     *,
1848:     after_commit: Callable[[Head], object] | None = None,
1849: ) -> CommittedBatchWrite:
1850:     """Append a prepared batch exactly once; no item is retried separately."""
1851:     from .arrival_contract import Profile
1852: 
1853:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1854:         raise NotAuthority("batch writes require an Authority descriptor role")
1855:     if not plan.drafts:
1856:         return CommittedBatchWrite(
1857:             None,
1858:             plan.items,
1859:             ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED,
1860:             plan.pending_tick_ids,
1861:             plan.pending_tick_names,
1862:             plan.pending_tick_runs,
1863:         )
1864:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1865:     try:
1866:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1867:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1868:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1869:         if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
1870:             if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1871:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1872:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch emission requires a minted ledger")
1873:         if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1874:             raise comparison.refusal
1875:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1876:             raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
1877:         if comparison.presented != plan.captured_head:
1878:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("captured head changed before batch append")
1879:         try:
1880:             commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, plan.drafts)
1881:         except NotWitnessed:
1882:             raise
1883:         except ContractRefusal:
1884:             raise
1885:         except Exception as exc:
1886:             raise BatchWriteCommitUnknown(plan, exc) from exc
1887:         if after_commit is not None:
1888:             try:
1889:                 after_commit(commit.after)
1890:             except Exception as exc:
1891:                 raise BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed(commit, plan, exc) from exc
1892:         projection = ProjectionOutcome.SYNCED if after_commit else ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED
1893:         return CommittedBatchWrite(
1894:             commit,
1895:             plan.items,
1896:             projection,
1897:             plan.pending_tick_ids,
1898:             plan.pending_tick_names,
1899:             plan.pending_tick_runs,
1900:         )
1901:     finally:
1902:         _close_quietly(query)
1903:         _close_quietly(ledger)

=== libs/engine/tests/test_runtime_write.py ===

71: class Snapshot:
72:     def __init__(self, head: Head, *, facts=(), ticks=(), documents=(), anchor_ordinal=0):
73:         self.represented = Watermark(head.lineage, head.ordinal)
74:         self.view_generation = "test-view"
75:         self.declaration_anchor = DeclarationAnchor(
76:             own_lineage=head.lineage,
77:             genesis=Fact(
78:                 id=head.lineage,
79:                 kind="_decl.genesis",
80:                 ts=0.0,
81:                 observer="kyle",
82:                 origin="",
83:                 payload={"protocol": 1, "documents": list(documents)},
84:                 arrival_ordinal=anchor_ordinal,
85:                 arrival_seq=0,
86:                 payload_text=json.dumps({"protocol": 1, "documents": list(documents)}),
87:             ),
88:         )
89:         self._facts = tuple(facts)
90:         self._ticks = tuple(ticks)

244:     )

465:     head = _head()
466:     existing = Fact(

481:         _vertex(),
482:         AtomFact("note", 42.0, {"value": "one"}, observer="kyle"),
483:         # A retry remains idempotent even if its former grant is no longer
484:         # sufficient; it must not re-run admission or create another tick.
485:         grant=Grant(potential=frozenset()),
486:         credentials=WriteCredentials(),
487:         custodian="kyle",
488:         fact_id="fact-1",
489:     )
490:     assert equal.already_present
491:     assert not equal.drafts
492:     with pytest.raises(RuntimeWriteRefused, match="different content"):
493:         plan_ordinary_write(
494:             Snapshot(head, facts=(existing,)),
495:             ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view"),
496:             _vertex(),
497:             AtomFact("note", 42.0, {"value": "changed"}, observer="kyle"),
498:             grant=None,
499:             credentials=WriteCredentials(),
500:             custodian="kyle",
501:             fact_id="fact-1",
502:         )

505: def test_plan_hashes_the_tick_window_by_arrival_coordinate_not_input_order():
506:     head = _head()
507:     later = Fact(
508:         id="later",
509:         kind="note",
510:         ts=1.0,
511:         observer="kyle",
512:         origin="",
513:         payload={"value": "later"},
514:         arrival_ordinal=3,
515:         arrival_seq=0,
516:         payload_text='{"value": "later"}',
517:         signature=None,
518:     )
519:     earlier = Fact(
520:         id="earlier",
521:         kind="note",
522:         ts=99.0,
523:         observer="kyle",
524:         origin="",
525:         payload={"value": "earlier"},
526:         arrival_ordinal=1,
527:         arrival_seq=0,
528:         payload_text='{"value": "earlier"}',
529:         signature=None,
530:     )
531:     plan = plan_ordinary_write(
532:         Snapshot(head, facts=(later, earlier)),
533:         ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view"),
534:         _vertex(),
535:         AtomFact("note", 42.0, {"value": "new"}, observer="kyle"),
536:         grant=None,
537:         credentials=WriteCredentials(),
538:         custodian="kyle",
539:         fact_id="new",
540:     )
541:     fact_body, tick_body = (draft.body for draft in plan.drafts)
542:     expected = hashlib.sha256()
543:     for row in (earlier, later):
544:         expected.update(
545:             fact_row_hash(
546:                 (
547:                     row.id,
548:                     row.kind,
549:                     row.ts,
550:                     row.observer,
551:                     row.origin,
552:                     row.payload_text,
553:                     row.signature,
554:                 )
555:             ).encode()
556:         )
557:     expected.update(
558:         fact_row_hash(
559:             (
560:                 fact_body["id"],
561:                 fact_body["kind"],
562:                 fact_body["ts"],
563:                 fact_body["observer"],
564:                 fact_body["origin"],
565:                 fact_body["payload"],
566:                 fact_body.get("signature"),
567:             )
568:         ).encode()
569:     )
570:     assert tick_body["window_hash"] == expected.hexdigest()

573: def test_hydrated_candidate_ignores_stale_runtime_state_and_uses_prior_tick_context():
574:     head = _head()
575:     effective = parse_vertex(
576:         'name "effective"\n'
577:         "loops {\n"
578:         "  note {\n"
579:         '    fold { total "sum" "amount" }\n'
580:         "    boundary every=2\n"
581:         "  }\n"
582:         "}\n"
583:     )
584:     locator = parse_vertex('name "locator"\nloops {\n  stale {\n    fold { count "inc" }\n  }\n}\n')
585:     documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(effective)]
586:     prior = Tick(
587:         id="prior-tick",
588:         name="note",
589:         ts=2.0,
590:         since=1.0,
591:         origin="effective",
592:         payload={"total": 3},
593:         arrival_ordinal=3,
594:         arrival_seq=0,
595:         payload_text='{"total": 3}',
596:         prev_hash=None,
597:         window_start="",
598:         fact_cursor="second",
599:         window_hash="sealed",
600:         signature=None,
601:     )
602:     facts = (
603:         Fact("first", "note", 1.0, "kyle", "", {"amount": 1}, 1, 0, '{"amount": 1}'),
604:         Fact("second", "note", 2.0, "kyle", "", {"amount": 2}, 2, 0, '{"amount": 2}'),
605:         Fact("suffix", "note", 3.0, "kyle", "", {"amount": 3}, 4, 0, '{"amount": 3}'),
606:     )
607:     snapshot = Snapshot(head, facts=facts, ticks=(prior,), documents=documents)
608:     snapshot._facts = (snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis, *facts)
609:     basis = ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view")
610: 
611:     candidate = hydrate_arrival_candidate(snapshot, basis, locator)
612:

614:     assert candidate.kinds == ["note", "cite"]
615:     assert candidate.state("note") == {"total": 3}
616:     plan = plan_ordinary_write(
617:         snapshot,
618:         basis,
619:         candidate,
620:         AtomFact("note", 4.0, {"amount": 4}, observer="kyle"),
621:         grant=None,
622:         credentials=WriteCredentials(),
623:         custodian="kyle",
624:         fact_id="fourth",
625:     )
626:     assert plan.tick_id is not None
627:     assert plan.drafts[1].body["payload"] == '{"total": 7}'
628:     assert plan.drafts[1].body["since"] == 3.0

631: def test_strict_negative_tick_keeps_hydration_policy_and_global_predecessor():
632:     head = _head()
633:     effective = parse_vertex(
634:         'name "effective"\nloops {\n  note {\n'
635:         '    fold { total "sum" "amount" }\n'
636:         '    boundary every=1\n  }\n}\n'
637:     )
638:     documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(effective)]
639:     before = Fact(
640:         "before", "note", 1.0, "kyle", "", {"amount": 3}, 1, 0,
641:         '{"amount":3}',
642:     )
643:     negative_tick = Tick(
644:         id="negative-tick", name="note", ts=-1.0, since=None,
645:         origin="effective", payload={"total": 3}, arrival_ordinal=2,
646:         arrival_seq=0, payload_text='{"total":3}', prev_hash=None,
647:         window_start="", fact_cursor="before", window_hash="prior-window",
648:         signature=None,
649:     )
650:     snapshot = Snapshot(
651:         head, facts=(before,), ticks=(negative_tick,), documents=documents,
652:     )
653:     snapshot._facts = (snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis, before)
654:     basis = ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view")
655:     locator = parse_vertex('name "locator"\nloops { note { fold { count "inc" } } }\n')

661:         AtomFact("note", 2.0, {"amount": 4}, observer="kyle"),
662:         grant=None, credentials=WriteCredentials(), custodian="kyle",
663:         fact_id="after",
664:     )

668:         negative_tick.prev_hash, negative_tick.window_start,
669:         negative_tick.fact_cursor, negative_tick.window_hash,

675: def test_public_hydration_requires_physical_fresh_epoch_anchor():
676:     head = _head()
677:     effective = parse_vertex(
678:         'name "effective"\nloops { note { fold { count "inc" } } }\n'
679:     )
680:     documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(effective)]
681:     before = Fact("before", "note", 1.0, "kyle", "", {"inc": 9}, 2, 0, '{"inc":9}')
682:     after = Fact("after", "note", 2.0, "kyle", "", {"inc": 2}, 4, 0, '{"inc":2}')
683:     snapshot = Snapshot(head, documents=documents, anchor_ordinal=3)
684:     genesis = snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis
685:     assert genesis is not None
686:     payload = {
687:         "protocol": 2,
688:         "documents": documents,
689:         "runtime_epoch": "fresh-after-anchor-v1",
690:     }
691:     genesis = replace(
692:         genesis,
693:         payload=payload,
694:         payload_text=json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")),
695:         signature="fact-signature",
696:     )
697:     snapshot.declaration_anchor = replace(snapshot.declaration_anchor, genesis=genesis)
698:     snapshot._facts = (before, genesis, after)
699:     basis = ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view")
700:     locator = parse_vertex('name "locator"\nloops { note { fold { count "inc" } } }\n')
701: 
702:     with pytest.raises(DeclarationResolutionError, match="physical genesis row"):
703:         hydrate_arrival_candidate(snapshot, basis, locator)
704: 
705:     wire = _fake_declaration_record(snapshot)
706:     wire["sig"] = "arrival-signature"
707:     candidate = hydrate_arrival_candidate(
708:         snapshot, basis, locator, wire_genesis_record=wire
709:     )
710:     assert candidate.state("note") == {"count": 1}

786: @pytest.mark.parametrize("reset", (False, True))
787: def test_direct_planner_refuses_runtime_loop_identity_before_signing(reset) -> None:
788:     head = _head()
789:     candidate = Vertex("same")
790:     candidate.register_loop(
791:         Loop("same", {}, lambda state, _payload: state, reset=reset)
792:     )
793:     signing_attempted = False
794: 
795:     def signer(*_args):
796:         nonlocal signing_attempted
797:         signing_attempted = True
798:         return "signature"
799: 
800:     with pytest.raises(RuntimeWriteRefused, match="vertex name 'same'"):
801:         plan_ordinary_write(
802:             Snapshot(head),
803:             ReadBasis(head.lineage, head, head, "test-view"),
804:             candidate,
805:             AtomFact("note", 1.0, {}, observer="kyle"),
806:             grant=None,
807:             credentials=WriteCredentials(
808:                 fact_signer=signer,
809:                 arrival_signer=signer,
810:             ),
811:             custodian="kyle",
812:         )
813: 
814:     assert signing_attempted is False

818:     monkeypatch,
819: ):
820:     head = _head()

846: def test_hydration_restores_vertex_tick_period_like_legacy_replay():
847:     vertex = _vertex()
848:     vertex.register_vertex_boundary("close")
849:     facts = (
850:         Fact("one", "note", 1.0, "kyle", "", {"value": "one"}, 1, 0, '{"value": "one"}'),
851:         Fact("two", "note", 3.0, "kyle", "", {"value": "two"}, 3, 0, '{"value": "two"}'),
852:     )
853:     boundary = Tick("close-tick", "kyle", 2.0, 1.0, "kyle", {}, 2, 0, "{}")
854: 
855:     vertex.hydrate_snapshot(facts, (boundary,))
856: 
857:     # Vertex boundaries snapshot all loops but source live/replay semantics do
858:     # not reset their fold state. The persisted tick timestamp is the period
859:     # context for the next vertex boundary.
860:     assert vertex.state("note") == {"items": ["one", "two"]}
861:     assert vertex._vertex_period_start is not None
862:     assert vertex._vertex_period_start.timestamp() == 2.0

893: def test_supported_prepare_uses_snapshot_policy_and_genesis_custodian(monkeypatch):
894:     head = _head()
895:     effective = parse_vertex(
896:         'name "effective"\n'
897:         'observers { kyle { grant { potential "current" } } }\n'
898:         'loops { current { fold { count "inc" } } }\n'
899:     )
900:     locator = parse_vertex(
901:         'name "locator"\n'
902:         'observers { kyle { grant { potential "stale" } } }\n'
903:         'loops { stale { fold { count "inc" } } }\n'
904:     )
905:     documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(effective)]
906:     snapshot = Snapshot(head, documents=documents, anchor_ordinal=1)
907:     snapshot._facts = (snapshot.declaration_anchor.genesis,)
908: 
909:     class FakeLedger:
910:         def __init__(self):
911:             self.opened = SimpleNamespace(comparison=Compared(Outcome.FIRST_CONTACT, head, None))
912: 
913:         def head_at(self, watermark):
914:             assert watermark == snapshot.represented
915:             return head
916: 
917:         def read(self, coordinate):
918:             return (
919:                 {"k": "genesis", "observer": "physical-custodian"}
920:                 if coordinate == 0 else _fake_declaration_record(snapshot)
921:             )
922: 
923:         def capabilities(self):
924:             return SimpleNamespace(max_atomic_records=None)
925: 
926:         def close(self):
927:             return None
928: 
929:     class FakeRegistry:
930:         def open(self, descriptor):
931:             return FakeLedger(), SimpleNamespace(
932:                 open_snapshot=lambda **kwargs: snapshot,
933:                 close=lambda: None,
934:             )
935: 
936:     monkeypatch.setattr("engine.runtime_write.AttestedLedger", FakeLedger)
937:     plan = prepare_ordinary_write(
938:         FakeRegistry(),
939:         StoreDescriptor("file", "ignored", role=Profile.AUTHORITY),
940:         locator,
941:         AtomFact("current", 1.0, {}, observer="kyle"),
942:         credentials=WriteCredentials(),
943:         fact_id="prepared",
944:     )
945: 
946:     assert plan.effective_declaration is not None
947:     assert plan.effective_declaration.name == "effective"
948:     assert plan.admission_grant is not None
949:     assert plan.admission_grant.potential == frozenset({"current"})
950:     assert plan.custodian == "physical-custodian"
951:     assert plan.drafts[0].observer == "kyle"
952:

988:             return None

=== libs/lang/src/lang/document.py ===
1: """Declaration document form — AST ↔ per-subject JSON documents (SPEC §9.2).
2: 
3: A ``.vertex`` file's declaration surface is decomposed into **subject-scoped
4: documents**: each is the *complete current definition* of one subject (a kind,
5: an observer, a combine member, a source, a lens, or the vertex singleton). No
6: facet deltas — a mutation re-emits the whole subject document. This is the
7: document tier the internal-table build (SPEC §9) records as immutable
8: declaration events under a reserved kind namespace.
9: 
10: This module is pure grammar: it maps between the frozen AST types the parser
11: returns (``ast.py``) and plain JSON-safe dicts. No engine/atoms/store imports,
12: no third-party dependencies. Payloads are strictly JSON types (dict/list/str/
13: int/float/bool/None) so they can later be JCS-canonicalized (rfc8785).
14: 
15: Residence is stripped structurally (SPEC §9.5): the ``store`` locator and the
16: ``VertexFile.path`` NEVER enter documents — they are supplied from outside at
17: projection time. Observer *public* keys DO enter (meaning-critical, portable);
18: private-key custody is never in the AST. Host-bound source wiring enters as
19: ``_decl.source-defined`` provenance documents (SPEC §9.2 Ingress stratum).
20: 
21: Union members carry explicit stable type tags (never reflected class names),
22: so a class rename cannot silently break the wire form. Every union member in
23: ``ast.py`` is enumerated below — folds, boundaries, parse steps, transform ops,
24: source variants — not sampled.
25: 
26: Forward compatibility is normative: :func:`documents_to_vertex` tolerates
27: unknown fields inside a document and unknown ``_decl.*`` kinds (skips them),
28: so a newer protocol's documents project safely under an older reader.
29:
