30: Protocol rules this module enforces (subject identity, ordering, safety):
31: 
32: - **Subject uniqueness.** ``(kind, subject)`` must be unique — the internal
33:   table's runtime state is a ``Latest`` fold keyed by it (SPEC §9.2), so a
34:   collision silently drops a subject. Subjects are the human-meaningful base
35:   (kind name / observer name / member name / source path or command); when two
36:   documents of one kind would share a base, later occurrences get a
37:   deterministic ``base#2``, ``base#3`` suffix in encounter order. Suffixes are
38:   allocated against the *set* of subjects already issued for that kind, not a
39:   per-base counter — so a suffix never lands on a naturally-occurring subject
40:   (bases ``["base", "base", "base#2"]`` → ``["base", "base#2", "base#3"]``). The
41:   suffix is identity only — projection reconstructs every value from the
42:   *payload*, never by parsing the subject — so a reorder of distinct subjects
43:   stays stable while identical duplicates disambiguate deterministically.
44: 
45: - **Order is meaning.** Declaration order is load-bearing in this AST
46:   (sequential source blocks, boundary match "first match fires", kind order
47:   driving fold/salience rendering) but per-subject ``Latest`` folding erases
48:   encounter order. So every multi-instance document (kind/observer/member/
49:   source/lens) carries an integer ``order`` = its encounter position at
50:   absorption; :func:`documents_to_vertex` restores structure by sorting on
51:   ``(order, subject)``. A reorder edit re-emits the affected subjects — honest,
52:   since order *is* meaning where declared. (The vertex-level boundary tuple
53:   rides ordered inside the vertex-defined singleton and needs no per-doc order.)
54: 
55: - **Source block structure** is recorded on the vertex-defined singleton as a
56:   ``source_blocks`` list of ``{"index", "mode"}`` (present iff
57:   ``sources_blocks`` is not None). Inline source documents keep a ``block`` tag;
58:   projection rebuilds blocks from the singleton list and drops each inline
59:   source into its block, so block mode and identity are authoritative rather
60:   than inferred from surviving members.
61: 
62: - **Round-trip guarantee is over loader-reachable ASTs.** The parser structurally
63:   cannot produce an empty-but-present ``observers=()`` or ``combine=()`` (the
64:   loader rejects empty ``observers {}`` / ``combine {}`` blocks) or an empty
65:   ``SourcesBlock`` (empty ``sources sequential {}`` is rejected). These
66:   empty-present tuples are therefore normalized to ``None`` on projection.
67:   ``VertexFile.sources`` *is* reachable as an empty tuple (empty ``sources {}``),
68:   and that distinction is preserved via ``sources_present`` on the singleton.
69: 
70: - **JSON safety is enforced, not assumed.** Every payload is walked at
71:   serialization (:func:`_ensure_json_safe`): dict keys must be ``str``, values
72:   must be JSON scalars/lists/dicts, and every float must be finite. A ``NaN`` or
73:   ``Infinity`` (AST-constructible even though the parser rejects it) or a
74:   copied-through non-JSON value raises ``ValueError`` with the field path here —
75:   never later, when JCS canonicalization would be the first to notice.
76: """
77: 
78: from __future__ import annotations
79: 
80: import math
81: from pathlib import Path
82: from typing import Any, NamedTuple
83: 
84: from .ast import (
85:     BackendDecl,
86:     BoundaryAfter,
87:     BoundaryCondition,
88:     BoundaryEvery,
89:     BoundaryWhen,
90:     Coerce,
91:     CombineEntry,
92:     EdgeDecl,
93:     Explode,
94:     Flatten,
95:     FoldAvg,
96:     FoldBy,
97:     FoldCollect,
98:     FoldCount,
99:     FoldDecl,
100:     FoldLatest,
101:     FoldMax,
102:     FoldMin,
103:     FoldSum,
104:     FoldWindow,
105:     FromFile,
106:     GrantDecl,
107:     InlineSource,
108:     LensDecl,
109:     LifecycleDecl,
110:     LoopDef,
111:     LStrip,
112:     ObserverDecl,
113:     Pick,
114:     Project,
115:     Replace,
116:     RStrip,
117:     Select,
118:     Skip,
119:     SourceParams,
120:     SourcesBlock,
121:     Split,
122:     Strip,
123:     TemplateSource,
124:     Transform,
125:     Trigger,
126:     VertexFile,
127:     Where,
128: )
129: 
130: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
131: # Reserved-kind vocabulary (SPEC §9.2, code-frozen)
132: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
133: 
134: DECL_PREFIX = "_decl."
135: 
136: DECL_GENESIS = "_decl.genesis"
137: DECL_KIND_DEFINED = "_decl.kind-defined"
138: DECL_KIND_RETIRED = "_decl.kind-retired"
139: DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED = "_decl.observer-defined"
140: DECL_OBSERVER_RETIRED = "_decl.observer-retired"
141: DECL_MEMBER_DEFINED = "_decl.member-defined"
142: DECL_MEMBER_REMOVED = "_decl.member-removed"
143: DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED = "_decl.vertex-defined"
144: DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED = "_decl.source-defined"
145: DECL_SOURCE_RETIRED = "_decl.source-retired"
146: DECL_LENS_DEFINED = "_decl.lens-defined"
147: DECL_TRANSIT = "_decl.transit"
148: DECL_MERGED = "_decl.merged"
149: 
150: #: Maximum declaration-protocol version this reader understands. Ordinary
151: #: initialization still emits v1; v2 is reserved for explicit fresh-epoch
152: #: adoption. A foreign lineage carrying an unsupported version is inert
153: #: (never partially interpreted) — see SPEC §9.2 / build plan.
154: DECLARATION_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 2
155: 
156: #: The frozen tombstone vocabulary (SPEC §9.2): which ``*-defined`` kind each
157: #: subject removal is expressed as. A ``*-defined`` kind ABSENT from this map
158: #: is a **singleton with no tombstone** — ``_decl.lens-defined`` (spec:
159: #: "replaced by re-definition") and ``_decl.vertex-defined`` (it IS identity).
160: #: Removing such a subject is inexpressible in the code-frozen vocabulary; the
161: #: edit ceremony (S4) refuses it rather than mint a new kind unilaterally
162: #: (thread:decl-lens-tombstone-vocab-gap — a later coordinated vocab change
163: #: with the loops-go oracle). This is the single home for the mapping; the
164: #: engine resolver imports its inverse.
165: DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE: dict[str, str] = {
166:     DECL_KIND_DEFINED: DECL_KIND_RETIRED,
167:     DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED: DECL_OBSERVER_RETIRED,
168:     DECL_MEMBER_DEFINED: DECL_MEMBER_REMOVED,
169:     DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED: DECL_SOURCE_RETIRED,
170: }
171: 
172: 
173: def is_internal_kind(kind: str) -> bool:
174:     """True if ``kind`` is in the reserved declaration namespace (``_decl.*``).
175: 
176:     The single predicate every read/emit/filter site should route through
177:     (SPEC §9.4 costs; build-plan "SQL wildcard trap"). Dependency-free — a
178:     plain prefix test — because engine and apps import it directly.
179:     """
180:     return kind.startswith(DECL_PREFIX)
181: 
182: 
183: class Document(NamedTuple):
184:     """One subject-scoped declaration document: (kind, subject, payload).
185: 
186:     ``kind`` is a reserved ``_decl.*`` vocabulary constant; ``subject`` is the
187:     stable identifier of the thing described (kind name, observer name, source
188:     name, or the vertex name for singletons); ``payload`` is the complete
189:     current definition as a JSON-safe dict.
190:     """
191: 
192:     kind: str
193:     subject: str
194:     payload: dict[str, Any]
195: 
196:     def as_json(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
197:         """Flat JSON-safe form: ``{"kind", "subject", "payload"}``."""
198:         return {"kind": self.kind, "subject": self.subject, "payload": self.payload}
199: 
200: 
201: def _ensure_json_safe(obj: Any, path: str) -> None:
202:     """Assert ``obj`` is strictly JSON-safe, or raise ``ValueError`` with path.
203: 
204:     Enforced at serialization so a bad value (a non-finite float, a non-str
205:     dict key, a copied-through tuple/set/Path) is caught here — with a field
206:     path pointing at it — rather than surfacing later at JCS canonicalization.
207:     ``bool`` is allowed (a JSON scalar); every ``float`` must be finite.
208:     """
209:     # bool is an int subclass, so (str, int) already covers True/False.
210:     if obj is None or isinstance(obj, (str, int)):
211:         return
212:     if isinstance(obj, float):
213:         if not math.isfinite(obj):
214:             raise ValueError(f"non-finite float at {path}: {obj!r}")
215:         return
216:     if isinstance(obj, dict):
217:         for k, v in obj.items():
218:             if not isinstance(k, str):
219:                 raise ValueError(f"non-str dict key at {path}: {k!r}")
220:             _ensure_json_safe(v, f"{path}.{k}")
221:         return
222:     if isinstance(obj, list):
223:         for i, v in enumerate(obj):
224:             _ensure_json_safe(v, f"{path}[{i}]")
225:         return
226:     raise ValueError(f"non-JSON type {type(obj).__name__} at {path}")
227: 
228: 
229: # ===========================================================================
230: # Union serializers — AST → JSON.  Every member carries an explicit type tag.
231: # ===========================================================================
232: 
233: 
234: def _fold_op_to_json(op: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
235:     """FoldOp → ``{"op": <tag>, ...}``. Enumerates all nine members."""
236:     if isinstance(op, FoldBy):
237:         return {"op": "by", "key_field": op.key_field}
238:     if isinstance(op, FoldCount):
239:         return {"op": "count"}
240:     if isinstance(op, FoldSum):
241:         return {"op": "sum", "field": op.field}
242:     if isinstance(op, FoldLatest):
243:         return {"op": "latest"}
244:     if isinstance(op, FoldCollect):
245:         return {"op": "collect", "max_items": op.max_items}
246:     if isinstance(op, FoldMax):
247:         return {"op": "max", "field": op.field}
248:     if isinstance(op, FoldMin):
249:         return {"op": "min", "field": op.field}
250:     if isinstance(op, FoldAvg):
251:         return {"op": "avg", "field": op.field}
252:     if isinstance(op, FoldWindow):
253:         return {"op": "window", "field": op.field, "size": op.size}
254:     raise TypeError(f"unknown FoldOp member: {type(op).__name__}")
255: 
256: 
257: def _fold_op_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
258:     tag = d["op"]
259:     if tag == "by":
260:         return FoldBy(key_field=d["key_field"])
261:     if tag == "count":
262:         return FoldCount()
263:     if tag == "sum":
264:         return FoldSum(field=d["field"])
265:     if tag == "latest":
266:         return FoldLatest()
267:     if tag == "collect":
268:         return FoldCollect(max_items=d["max_items"])
269:     if tag == "max":
270:         return FoldMax(field=d["field"])
271:     if tag == "min":
272:         return FoldMin(field=d["field"])
273:     if tag == "avg":
274:         return FoldAvg(field=d["field"])
275:     if tag == "window":
276:         return FoldWindow(field=d["field"], size=d["size"])
277:     raise ValueError(f"unknown fold op tag: {tag!r}")
278: 
279: 
280: def _fold_decl_to_json(decl: FoldDecl) -> dict[str, Any]:
281:     return {"target": decl.target, "op": _fold_op_to_json(decl.op)}
282: 
283: 
284: def _fold_decl_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> FoldDecl:
285:     return FoldDecl(target=d["target"], op=_fold_op_from_json(d["op"]))
286: 
287: 
288: def _transform_op_to_json(op: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
289:     """TransformOp → ``{"t": <tag>, ...}``. Enumerates all five members."""
290:     if isinstance(op, Strip):
291:         return {"t": "strip", "chars": op.chars}
292:     if isinstance(op, LStrip):
293:         return {"t": "lstrip", "chars": op.chars}
294:     if isinstance(op, RStrip):
295:         return {"t": "rstrip", "chars": op.chars}
296:     if isinstance(op, Replace):
297:         return {"t": "replace", "old": op.old, "new": op.new}
298:     if isinstance(op, Coerce):
299:         return {"t": "coerce", "type": op.type}
300:     raise TypeError(f"unknown TransformOp member: {type(op).__name__}")
301: 
302: 
303: def _transform_op_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
304:     tag = d["t"]
305:     if tag == "strip":
306:         return Strip(chars=d["chars"])
307:     if tag == "lstrip":
308:         return LStrip(chars=d["chars"])
309:     if tag == "rstrip":
310:         return RStrip(chars=d["chars"])
311:     if tag == "replace":
312:         return Replace(old=d["old"], new=d["new"])
313:     if tag == "coerce":
314:         return Coerce(type=d["type"])
315:     raise ValueError(f"unknown transform op tag: {tag!r}")
316: 
317: 
318: def _parse_step_to_json(step: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
319:     """ParseStep → ``{"step": <tag>, ...}``. Enumerates all nine members."""
320:     if isinstance(step, Skip):
321:         return {"step": "skip", "pattern": step.pattern}
322:     if isinstance(step, Split):
323:         return {"step": "split", "delimiter": step.delimiter}
324:     if isinstance(step, Pick):
325:         return {
326:             "step": "pick",
327:             "indices": list(step.indices),
328:             "names": list(step.names) if step.names is not None else None,
329:         }
330:     if isinstance(step, Select):
331:         return {"step": "select", "fields": list(step.fields)}
332:     if isinstance(step, Transform):
333:         return {
334:             "step": "transform",
335:             "field": step.field,
336:             "operations": [_transform_op_to_json(o) for o in step.operations],
337:         }
338:     if isinstance(step, Explode):
339:         return {
340:             "step": "explode",
341:             "path": step.path,
342:             "carry": dict(step.carry) if step.carry is not None else None,
343:         }
344:     if isinstance(step, Project):
345:         return {"step": "project", "fields": dict(step.fields)}
346:     if isinstance(step, Where):
347:         return {
348:             "step": "where",
349:             "path": step.path,
350:             "op": step.op,
351:             "value": step.value,
352:             "values": list(step.values),
353:         }
354:     if isinstance(step, Flatten):
355:         return {
356:             "step": "flatten",
357:             "field": step.field,
358:             "into": step.into,
359:             "extract": list(step.extract),
360:         }
361:     raise TypeError(f"unknown ParseStep member: {type(step).__name__}")
362: 
363: 
364: def _parse_step_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
365:     tag = d["step"]
366:     if tag == "skip":
367:         return Skip(pattern=d["pattern"])
368:     if tag == "split":
369:         return Split(delimiter=d["delimiter"])
370:     if tag == "pick":
371:         names = d["names"]
372:         return Pick(
373:             indices=tuple(d["indices"]),
374:             names=tuple(names) if names is not None else None,
375:         )
376:     if tag == "select":
377:         return Select(fields=tuple(d["fields"]))
378:     if tag == "transform":
379:         return Transform(
380:             field=d["field"],
381:             operations=tuple(_transform_op_from_json(o) for o in d["operations"]),
382:         )
383:     if tag == "explode":
384:         carry = d["carry"]
385:         return Explode(path=d["path"], carry=dict(carry) if carry is not None else None)
386:     if tag == "project":
387:         return Project(fields=dict(d["fields"]))
388:     if tag == "where":
389:         return Where(
390:             path=d["path"],
391:             op=d.get("op", "equals"),
392:             value=d.get("value"),
393:             values=tuple(d.get("values", ())),
394:         )
395:     if tag == "flatten":
396:         return Flatten(field=d["field"], into=d["into"], extract=tuple(d["extract"]))
397:     raise ValueError(f"unknown parse step tag: {tag!r}")
398: 
399: 
400: def _parse_steps_to_json(steps: tuple[Any, ...]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
401:     return [_parse_step_to_json(s) for s in steps]
402: 
403: 
404: def _parse_steps_from_json(items: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
405:     return tuple(_parse_step_from_json(d) for d in items)
406: 
407: 
408: def _boundary_condition_to_json(c: BoundaryCondition) -> dict[str, Any]:
409:     return {"target": c.target, "op": c.op, "value": c.value}
410: 
411: 
412: def _boundary_condition_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> BoundaryCondition:
413:     return BoundaryCondition(target=d["target"], op=d["op"], value=d["value"])
414: 
415: 
416: def _boundary_to_json(b: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
417:     """Boundary → ``{"type": <tag>, ...}``. Enumerates all three members."""
418:     if isinstance(b, BoundaryWhen):
419:         return {
420:             "type": "when",
421:             "kind": b.kind,
422:             "match": [[k, v] for (k, v) in b.match],
423:             "conditions": [_boundary_condition_to_json(c) for c in b.conditions],
424:             "run": b.run,
425:         }
426:     if isinstance(b, BoundaryAfter):
427:         return {"type": "after", "count": b.count, "run": b.run}
428:     if isinstance(b, BoundaryEvery):
429:         return {"type": "every", "count": b.count, "run": b.run}
430:     raise TypeError(f"unknown Boundary member: {type(b).__name__}")
431: 
432: 
433: def _boundary_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
434:     tag = d["type"]
435:     if tag == "when":
436:         return BoundaryWhen(
437:             kind=d["kind"],
438:             match=tuple((k, v) for k, v in d.get("match", ())),
439:             conditions=tuple(
440:                 _boundary_condition_from_json(c) for c in d.get("conditions", ())
441:             ),
442:             run=d.get("run"),
443:         )
444:     if tag == "after":
445:         return BoundaryAfter(count=d["count"], run=d.get("run"))
446:     if tag == "every":
447:         return BoundaryEvery(count=d["count"], run=d.get("run"))
448:     raise ValueError(f"unknown boundary type tag: {tag!r}")
449: 
450: 
451: def _trigger_to_json(t: Trigger | None) -> list[str] | None:
452:     return list(t.kinds) if t is not None else None
453: 
454: 
455: def _trigger_from_json(v: list[str] | None) -> Trigger | None:
456:     return Trigger(kinds=tuple(v)) if v is not None else None
457: 
458: 
459: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
460: # LoopDef  ↔  _decl.kind-defined payload
461: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
462: 
463: 
464: def _loop_def_to_payload(loop: LoopDef) -> dict[str, Any]:
465:     return {
466:         "folds": [_fold_decl_to_json(f) for f in loop.folds],
467:         "boundary": _boundary_to_json(loop.boundary)
468:         if loop.boundary is not None
469:         else None,
470:         "search": list(loop.search),
471:         "parse": _parse_steps_to_json(loop.parse),
472:         "preview": list(loop.preview_fields),
473:         "edges": [{"field": e.field, "target": e.target} for e in loop.edges],
474:         "lifecycle": (
475:             {"field": loop.lifecycle.field, "active": list(loop.lifecycle.active)}
476:             if loop.lifecycle is not None
477:             else None
478:         ),
479:     }
480: 
481: 
482: def _loop_def_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> LoopDef:
483:     boundary = p.get("boundary")
484:     # `.get()` default keeps §9.2 forward/backward compat: a kind-defined
485:     # document minted before the lifecycle facet existed simply lacks the key
486:     # and projects to `lifecycle=None`. This field did not bump the declaration
487:     # protocol when added (additive field — arbiter S5-F3).
488:     lc = p.get("lifecycle")
489:     return LoopDef(
490:         folds=tuple(_fold_decl_from_json(f) for f in p.get("folds", ())),
491:         boundary=_boundary_from_json(boundary) if boundary is not None else None,
492:         search=tuple(p.get("search", ())),
493:         parse=_parse_steps_from_json(p.get("parse", [])),
494:         preview_fields=tuple(p.get("preview", ())),
495:         edges=tuple(
496:             EdgeDecl(field=e["field"], target=e["target"]) for e in p.get("edges", ())
497:         ),
498:         lifecycle=(
499:             LifecycleDecl(field=lc["field"], active=tuple(lc["active"]))
500:             if lc is not None
501:             else None
502:         ),
503:     )
504: 
505: 
506: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
507: # ObserverDecl  ↔  _decl.observer-defined payload
508: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
509: 
510: 
511: def _observer_to_payload(o: ObserverDecl) -> dict[str, Any]:
512:     grant = (
513:         {"potential": sorted(o.grant.potential)} if o.grant is not None else None
514:     )
515:     return {
516:         "name": o.name,
517:         "identity": o.identity,
518:         "grant": grant,
519:         "key": o.key,
520:     }
521: 
522: 
523: def _observer_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> ObserverDecl:
524:     grant_d = p.get("grant")
525:     grant = (
526:         GrantDecl(potential=frozenset(grant_d["potential"]))
527:         if grant_d is not None
528:         else None
529:     )
530:     return ObserverDecl(
531:         name=p["name"],
532:         identity=p.get("identity"),
533:         grant=grant,
534:         key=p.get("key"),
535:     )
536: 
537: 
538: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
539: # CombineEntry  ↔  _decl.member-defined payload
540: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
541: 
542: 
543: def _combine_to_payload(c: CombineEntry) -> dict[str, Any]:
544:     return {"name": c.name, "alias": c.alias}
545: 
546: 
547: def _combine_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> CombineEntry:
548:     return CombineEntry(name=p["name"], alias=p.get("alias"))
549: 
550: 
551: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
552: # LensDecl  ↔  _decl.lens-defined payload (whole-vertex singleton)
553: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
554: 
555: 
556: def _lens_to_payload(lens: LensDecl) -> dict[str, Any]:
557:     return {"fold": lens.fold, "stream": lens.stream}
558: 
559: 
560: def _lens_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> LensDecl:
561:     return LensDecl(fold=p.get("fold"), stream=p.get("stream"))
562: 
563: 
564: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
565: # Sources  ↔  _decl.source-defined payloads
566: #
567: # Three variants share the source-defined kind, discriminated by "form":
568: #   - "path":     a bare .loop path entry in VertexFile.sources
569: #   - "template": a TemplateSource entry in VertexFile.sources
570: #   - "inline":   an InlineSource inside a VertexFile.sources_blocks group;
571: #                 carries "block" (group index) + "mode" so the SourcesBlock
572: #                 grouping and execution mode reconstruct exactly.
573: #
574: # Source Paths (host-bound wiring) stay inside these payloads by design —
575: # this is the Ingress / provenance stratum (SPEC §9.2), not portable config.
576: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
577: 
578: 
579: def _source_params_to_json(sp: SourceParams) -> dict[str, Any]:
580:     return {"values": dict(sp.values)}
581: 
582: 
583: def _source_params_from_json(d: dict[str, Any]) -> SourceParams:
584:     return SourceParams(values=dict(d["values"]))
585: 
586: 
587: def _content_sha256(path: Path, base: Path | None) -> str | None:
588:     """SHA-256 of a referenced file's bytes, or None if unreadable.
589: 
590:     Pins WHAT the referenced ``.loop``/template said at absorb time so later
591:     drift is detectable (surfaced at ``absorb -n``, never auto-absorbed).
592:     Content capture as a portable document form is deferred — embedding raw
593:     text would re-leak env values the indirection rule just excluded.
594:     """
595:     import hashlib
596: 
597:     candidate = path if path.is_absolute() else ((base / path) if base else path)
598:     try:
599:         return hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
600:     except OSError:
601:         return None
602: 
603: 
604: def _template_source_to_payload(
605:     t: TemplateSource, *, base: Path | None = None
606: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
607:     return {
608:         "form": "template",
609:         "template": str(t.template),
610:         "content_sha256": _content_sha256(t.template, base),
611:         "params": [_source_params_to_json(sp) for sp in t.params],
612:         "from": {
613:             "strategy": "file",
614:             "path": str(t.from_.path),
615:             # Pin the params file too — its rows parameterize the template,
616:             # so silent drift is the same auto-enactment hazard as the
617:             # template itself (closing re-review #3).
618:             "params_sha256": _content_sha256(Path(t.from_.path), base),
619:         }
620:         if t.from_ is not None
621:         else None,
622:         "loop": _loop_def_to_payload(t.loop) if t.loop is not None else None,
623:     }
624: 
625: 
626: def _template_source_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> TemplateSource:
627:     # "content_sha256" is provenance (drift pin), not AST state — ignored here.
628:     from_d = p.get("from")
629:     loop_d = p.get("loop")
630:     return TemplateSource(
631:         template=Path(p["template"]),
632:         params=tuple(_source_params_from_json(sp) for sp in p.get("params", ())),
633:         from_=FromFile(path=Path(from_d["path"])) if from_d is not None else None,
634:         loop=_loop_def_from_payload(loop_d) if loop_d is not None else None,
635:     )
636: 
637: 
638: def _inline_source_to_payload(
639:     s: InlineSource, *, block: int, mode: str
640: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
641:     return {
642:         "form": "inline",
643:         "block": block,
644:         "mode": mode,
645:         "command": s.command,
646:         "kind": s.kind,
647:         "observer": s.observer,
648:         "every": s.every,
649:         "on": _trigger_to_json(s.on),
650:         "format": s.format,
651:         "timeout": s.timeout,
652:         "origin": s.origin,
653:         # Env VALUES are ingress (SPEC §9.5 secrets-indirection): the
654:         # declaration records the key SET (shape — adding/removing a key is a
655:         # real edit); values live in the file and are re-attached at
656:         # resolution (load_declaration), never absorbed into payloads.
657:         "env": [k for (k, _v) in s.env],
658:         "parse": _parse_steps_to_json(s.parse),
659:         "path": s.path,
660:     }
661: 
662: 
663: def _inline_source_from_payload(p: dict[str, Any]) -> InlineSource:
664:     return InlineSource(
665:         command=p["command"],
666:         kind=p["kind"],
667:         observer=p.get("observer", ""),
668:         every=p.get("every", ""),
669:         on=_trigger_from_json(p.get("on")),
670:         format=p.get("format", "lines"),
671:         timeout=p.get("timeout", "60s"),
672:         origin=p.get("origin", ""),
673:         # Tolerant reader: new form is a key list (values are ingress,
674:         # re-attached from the file at resolution); legacy pre-indirection
675:         # rows carried [key, value] pairs — honor their recorded values.
676:         env=tuple(
677:             (e, "") if isinstance(e, str) else (e[0], e[1])
678:             for e in p.get("env", ())
679:         ),
680:         parse=_parse_steps_from_json(p.get("parse", [])),
681:         path=p.get("path", ""),
682:     )
683: 
684: 
685: def _source_subject(entry: Any) -> str:
686:     """Stable subject for a source-defined document."""
687:     if isinstance(entry, TemplateSource):
688:         return str(entry.template)
689:     if isinstance(entry, InlineSource):
690:         return entry.command
691:     # bare path entry
692:     return str(entry)
693: 
694: 
695: # ===========================================================================
696: # Public API — decompose and project
697: # ===========================================================================
698: 
699: 
700: def vertex_to_documents(ast: VertexFile) -> list[Document]:
701:     """Decompose a ``VertexFile`` AST into per-subject declaration documents.
702: 
703:     Emission order (deterministic): the ``_decl.vertex-defined`` singleton,
704:     then ``_decl.kind-defined`` per loop, ``_decl.observer-defined`` per
705:     observer, ``_decl.member-defined`` per combine entry, ``_decl.source-defined``
706:     per source (bare/template entries first, then inline sources per block),
707:     and ``_decl.lens-defined`` if a lens is declared.
708: 
709:     Each multi-instance document carries an ``order`` field (encounter
710:     position) and a subject deduplicated per kind (see module docstring:
711:     "Order is meaning", "Subject uniqueness"). Residence (``store`` locator,
712:     ``path``) is excluded. Every payload is validated JSON-safe before return.
713:     """
714:     docs: list[Document] = []
715:     order = 0  # global encounter position across all multi-instance documents
716: 
717:     # Per-kind subject deduplication. Allocate against the SET of subjects
718:     # already issued for this kind (not a per-base counter) so a suffix never
719:     # lands on a naturally-occurring subject: bases ["base", "base", "base#2"]
720:     # → ["base", "base#2", "base#3"], all distinct. Identity only — projection
721:     # reconstructs every value from the payload, never from the subject.
722:     issued: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
723: 
724:     def _subject(kind: str, base: str) -> str:
725:         seen = issued.setdefault(kind, set())
726:         candidate = base
727:         n = 1
728:         while candidate in seen:
729:             n += 1
730:             candidate = f"{base}#{n}"
731:         seen.add(candidate)
732:         return candidate
733: 
734:     def _emit(kind: str, base: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
735:         nonlocal order
736:         payload["order"] = order
737:         order += 1
738:         docs.append(Document(kind=kind, subject=_subject(kind, base), payload=payload))
739: 
740:     # vertex-defined singleton — subject is the vertex name (self); no order.
741:     docs.append(
742:         Document(
743:             kind=DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED,
744:             subject=ast.name,
745:             payload={
746:                 "name": ast.name,
747:                 "strict": ast.strict,
748:                 "observer_scoped": ast.observer_scoped,
749:                 "discover": ast.discover,
750:                 "emit": ast.emit,
751:                 "routes": dict(ast.routes) if ast.routes is not None else None,
752:                 "vertices": [str(p) for p in ast.vertices]
753:                 if ast.vertices is not None
754:                 else None,
755:                 "boundary": [_boundary_to_json(b) for b in ast.boundary],
756:                 # sources live in their own documents; this flag preserves the
757:                 # None-vs-empty-tuple distinction of VertexFile.sources.
758:                 "sources_present": ast.sources is not None,
759:                 # authoritative block structure (index + mode); None when there
760:                 # are no sources_blocks. Members are the inline source docs.
761:                 "source_blocks": [
762:                     {"index": i, "mode": b.mode}
763:                     for i, b in enumerate(ast.sources_blocks)
764:                 ]
765:                 if ast.sources_blocks is not None
766:                 else None,
767:             },
768:         )
769:     )
770: 
771:     # kind-defined per loop (dict preserves insertion order).
772:     for kind_name, loop in ast.loops.items():
773:         _emit(DECL_KIND_DEFINED, kind_name, _loop_def_to_payload(loop))
774: 
775:     # observer-defined per observer.
776:     for obs in ast.observers or ():
777:         _emit(DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED, obs.name, _observer_to_payload(obs))
778: 
779:     # member-defined per combine entry.
780:     for entry in ast.combine or ():
781:         _emit(DECL_MEMBER_DEFINED, entry.name, _combine_to_payload(entry))
782: 
783:     # source-defined — bare/template entries from VertexFile.sources ...
784:     base = ast.path.parent if ast.path is not None else None
785:     for entry in ast.sources or ():
786:         if isinstance(entry, TemplateSource):
787:             payload = _template_source_to_payload(entry, base=base)
788:         else:
789:             payload = {
790:                 "form": "path",
791:                 "path": str(entry),
792:                 "content_sha256": _content_sha256(Path(entry), base),
793:             }
794:         _emit(DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED, _source_subject(entry), payload)
795: 
796:     # ... then inline sources from VertexFile.sources_blocks, tagged with
797:     # their block index so projection can rebuild block grouping.
798:     for block_idx, block in enumerate(ast.sources_blocks or ()):
799:         # ast.py's custom frozen decorator rebuilds classes dynamically, so
800:         # Pyright can't resolve SourcesBlock.sources' type (infers Never).
801:         for src in block.sources:  # pyright: ignore[reportGeneralTypeIssues]
802:             _emit(
803:                 DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED,
804:                 _source_subject(src),
805:                 _inline_source_to_payload(src, block=block_idx, mode=block.mode),
806:             )
807: 
808:     # lens-defined singleton (whole-vertex fold/stream selection).
809:     if ast.lens is not None:
810:         _emit(DECL_LENS_DEFINED, ast.name, _lens_to_payload(ast.lens))
811: 
812:     # Enforce JSON safety (finite floats, str keys, JSON scalars) at the source.
813:     for d in docs:
814:         _ensure_json_safe(d.payload, f"{d.kind}:{d.subject}")
815: 
816:     return docs
817: 
818: 
819: def genesis_payload(ast: VertexFile) -> dict[str, Any]:
820:     """The genesis event payload: the whole document set + protocol version.
821: 
822:     ``genesis`` opens a store's lineage by absorbing the initial declaration
823:     whole (SPEC §9.2). JSON-safe; residence excluded like every document.
824:     """
825:     return {
826:         # Ordinary initialization remains v1. Protocol 2 is reserved for an
827:         # explicitly selected fresh runtime epoch at migration adoption.
828:         "protocol": 1,
829:         "documents": [d.as_json() for d in vertex_to_documents(ast)],
830:     }
831: 
832: 
833: def _as_triple(item: Any) -> tuple[str, str, dict[str, Any]]:
834:     """Normalize a document to (kind, subject, payload).
835: 
836:     Accepts :class:`Document` namedtuples and plain mappings with
837:     ``kind``/``subject``/``payload`` keys (e.g. from a genesis payload).
838:     """
839:     if isinstance(item, Document):
840:         return item.kind, item.subject, item.payload
841:     return item["kind"], item["subject"], item.get("payload", {})
842: 
843: 
844: def _rebuild_sources_blocks(
845:     source_blocks_decl: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
846:     inline_items: list[tuple[int, int, str, str, InlineSource]],
847: ) -> tuple[SourcesBlock, ...] | None:
848:     """Reassemble ``sources_blocks`` from the singleton's block structure.
849: 
850:     ``source_blocks_decl`` is the authoritative ``[{"index", "mode"}, ...]``
851:     recorded on the vertex-defined singleton; ``inline_items`` are the inline
852:     source docs as ``(order, block_index, mode, subject, InlineSource)``. Each
853:     inline source drops into its block by index, block members ordered by
854:     ``(order, subject)`` — subject breaks ties deterministically when duplicate
855:     ``order`` values appear (possible in edit-era document sets). An empty block
856:     survives as a member-less entry.
857: 
858:     Falls back to inferring blocks from inline tags (mode carried on each
859:     inline doc) when the singleton has no ``source_blocks`` — tolerating
860:     documents from an emitter predating the structural record (forward compat).
861:     """
862:     ordered = sorted(inline_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[3]))  # (order, subject)
863:     by_block: dict[int, list[InlineSource]] = {}
864:     for _order, block_idx, _mode, _subject, src in ordered:
865:         by_block.setdefault(block_idx, []).append(src)
866: 
867:     if source_blocks_decl is not None:
868:         return tuple(
869:             SourcesBlock(
870:                 mode=entry["mode"],
871:                 sources=tuple(by_block.get(entry["index"], ())),
872:             )
873:             for entry in source_blocks_decl
874:         )
875: 
876:     if not inline_items:
877:         return None
878: 
879:     # Fallback: no structural record — group by block index in first-seen
880:     # order, taking each block's mode from its first inline source's doc.
881:     mode_by_block: dict[int, str] = {}
882:     order_seen: list[int] = []
883:     for _order, block_idx, mode, _subject, _src in ordered:
884:         if block_idx not in mode_by_block:
885:             order_seen.append(block_idx)
886:             mode_by_block[block_idx] = mode
887:     return tuple(
888:         SourcesBlock(mode=mode_by_block[i], sources=tuple(by_block[i]))
889:         for i in order_seen
890:     )
891: 
892: 
893: def documents_to_vertex(
894:     documents: Any,
895:     *,
896:     path: Path | None = None,
897:     store: Path | None = None,
898:     store_backend: BackendDecl | None = None,
899:     store_location: str | None = None,
900: ) -> VertexFile:
901:     """Project declaration documents back to a ``VertexFile`` AST.
902: 
903:     ``path``, ``store``, ``store_backend`` and ``store_location`` are residence, supplied by the
904:     caller — they are not present in the documents. Pure inverse of
905:     :func:`vertex_to_documents` modulo those four fields.
906: 
907:     ``store_backend`` is residence for the same reason the locator is: which
908:     adapter opens a store is an operational choice about where the vertex
909:     lives, and putting an adapter name into signed declaration history would
910:     make it part of the vertex's absorbed identity. It is threaded rather
911:     than defaulted because a caller that drops it does not get an error — it
912:     gets a vertex whose backend arm has silently reverted to inference.
913: 
914:     Forward-compatible (normative, SPEC §9.2): unknown ``_decl.*`` kinds are
915:     skipped, and unknown fields inside a known document are ignored, so a
916:     newer protocol's documents project safely under this reader.
917:     """
918:     name: str | None = None
919:     strict = False
920:     observer_scoped = False
921:     discover: str | None = None
922:     emit: str | None = None
923:     routes: dict[str, str] | None = None
924:     vertices: tuple[Path, ...] | None = None
925:     boundary: tuple[Any, ...] = ()
926:     sources_present = False
927:     # authoritative block structure from the vertex-defined singleton.
928:     source_blocks_decl: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None
929: 
930:     # Collect multi-instance docs as (order, subject, value) so structure is
931:     # restored by sorting on (order, subject) — encounter order is meaning.
932:     kind_items: list[tuple[int, str, LoopDef]] = []
933:     observer_items: list[tuple[int, str, ObserverDecl]] = []
934:     member_items: list[tuple[int, str, CombineEntry]] = []
935:     lens_items: list[tuple[int, str, LensDecl]] = []
936:     bare_items: list[tuple[int, str, Any]] = []  # path / template entries
937:     # (order, block_index, mode, subject, InlineSource)
938:     inline_items: list[tuple[int, int, str, str, InlineSource]] = []
939: 
940:     def _order(p: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
941:         return p.get("order", 0)
942: 
943:     for item in documents:
944:         kind, subject, payload = _as_triple(item)
945: 
946:         if kind == DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED:
947:             name = payload["name"]
948:             strict = payload.get("strict", False)
949:             observer_scoped = payload.get("observer_scoped", False)
950:             discover = payload.get("discover")
951:             emit = payload.get("emit")
952:             r = payload.get("routes")
953:             routes = dict(r) if r is not None else None
954:             v = payload.get("vertices")
955:             vertices = tuple(Path(p) for p in v) if v is not None else None
956:             boundary = tuple(_boundary_from_json(b) for b in payload.get("boundary", ()))
957:             sources_present = payload.get("sources_present", False)
958:             source_blocks_decl = payload.get("source_blocks")
959: 
960:         elif kind == DECL_KIND_DEFINED:
961:             kind_items.append((_order(payload), subject, _loop_def_from_payload(payload)))
962: 
963:         elif kind == DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED:
964:             observer_items.append((_order(payload), subject, _observer_from_payload(payload)))
965: 
966:         elif kind == DECL_MEMBER_DEFINED:
967:             member_items.append((_order(payload), subject, _combine_from_payload(payload)))
968: 
969:         elif kind == DECL_LENS_DEFINED:
970:             lens_items.append((_order(payload), subject, _lens_from_payload(payload)))
971: 
972:         elif kind == DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED:
973:             form = payload.get("form")
974:             if form == "path":
975:                 bare_items.append((_order(payload), subject, Path(payload["path"])))
976:             elif form == "template":
977:                 bare_items.append(
978:                     (_order(payload), subject, _template_source_from_payload(payload))
979:                 )
980:             elif form == "inline":
981:                 inline_items.append(
982:                     (
983:                         _order(payload),
984:                         payload["block"],
985:                         payload.get("mode", "sequential"),
986:                         subject,
987:                         _inline_source_from_payload(payload),
988:                     )
989:                 )
990:             # unknown form → skip (forward compat)
991: 
992:         # unknown _decl.* kind → skip (forward compat)
993: 
994:     if name is None:
995:         raise ValueError("documents missing a _decl.vertex-defined singleton")
996: 
997:     # kind-defined: dict rebuilt in (order, subject) order.
998:     loops: dict[str, LoopDef] = {}
999:     for _o, subject, loop in sorted(kind_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1])):
1000:         loops[subject] = loop
1001: 
1002:     observers = [o for _o, _s, o in sorted(observer_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))]
1003:     combine = [c for _o, _s, c in sorted(member_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))]
1004:     lens = (
1005:         sorted(lens_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))[0][2] if lens_items else None
1006:     )
1007: 
1008:     # Reassemble VertexFile.sources (bare/template entries) in (order, subject).
1009:     bare_sorted = [e for _o, _s, e in sorted(bare_items, key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))]
1010:     sources: tuple[Any, ...] | None
1011:     if bare_sorted:
1012:         sources = tuple(bare_sorted)
1013:     elif sources_present:
1014:         sources = ()  # empty `sources {}` block — the one reachable empty tuple
1015:     else:
1016:         sources = None
1017: 
1018:     sources_blocks = _rebuild_sources_blocks(source_blocks_decl, inline_items)
1019: 
1020:     return VertexFile(
1021:         name=name,
1022:         loops=loops,
1023:         store=store,
1024:         store_backend=store_backend,
1025:         store_location=store_location,
1026:         discover=discover,
1027:         sources=sources,
1028:         vertices=vertices,
1029:         routes=routes,
1030:         emit=emit,
1031:         combine=tuple(combine) if combine else None,
1032:         sources_blocks=sources_blocks,
1033:         observers=tuple(observers) if observers else None,
1034:         lens=lens,
1035:         boundary=boundary,
1036:         observer_scoped=observer_scoped,
1037:         strict=strict,
1038:         path=path,
1039:     )
1040: 
1041: 
1042: # ===========================================================================
1043: # Subject-granular diff — the edit ceremony's grammar half (SPEC §9.2, S4)
1044: # ===========================================================================
1045: #
1046: # Re-absorbing an edited ``.vertex`` diffs its freshly-parsed document set
1047: # against the store's fold head (Latest per (kind, subject), self-lineage) and
1048: # re-emits ONLY the changed subjects as whole documents. This module supplies
1049: # the pure-grammar half: comparing two document sets. The store half (stamping
1050: # lineage, signing, appending atomically) lives in ``engine.sqlite_store``.
1051: #
1052: # Whole-document only (SPEC §9.2): a subject is "changed" iff its complete
1053: # payload — INCLUDING the ``order`` field — differs by value. There are no
1054: # facet deltas. This makes the round-trip exact (the resolver's overlay fold,
1055: # applied over the head, reproduces the edited file's document set) and
1056: # idempotence exact (an unchanged file re-parses to identical payloads, so the
1057: # diff is empty). ``order`` is a dense global counter, so a structural insert
1058: # shifts downstream subjects' ``order`` and honestly re-emits them — "order is
1059: # meaning" (see module docstring).
1060: 
1061: 
1062: #: Inverse of :data:`DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE`: which ``*-defined`` subject a
1063: #: tombstone kind removes. Used by :func:`apply_changes` to fold a removal.
1064: _TOMBSTONE_TO_DEFINED: dict[str, str] = {v: k for k, v in DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE.items()}
1065: 
1066: 
1067: class EditRefused(ValueError):
1068:     """A re-absorb edit is inexpressible in the frozen declaration vocabulary.
1069: 
1070:     Two cases, both structural refusals rather than silent lies:
1071: 
1072:     - **Singleton removal.** Dropping a ``_decl.lens-defined`` has no tombstone
1073:       in the code-frozen vocabulary (SPEC §9.2 — a lens is "replaced by
1074:       re-definition"), so removal cannot be recorded. Rather than mint a new
1075:       kind unilaterally (thread:decl-lens-tombstone-vocab-gap, a later
1076:       coordinated change with the loops-go oracle), the ceremony refuses.
1077: 
1078:     - **Identity change.** The ``_decl.vertex-defined`` singleton's subject is
1079:       the vertex name; a rename would surface as remove-old + add-new. The name
1080:       is meaning-critical self-description, so a post-genesis rename is a
1081:       deliberate identity ceremony, never a routine edit — refused.
1082:     """
1083: 
1084: 
1085: class Change(NamedTuple):
1086:     """One subject-granular declaration change (SPEC §9.2, S4).
1087: 
1088:     ``kind`` is the FINAL event kind: a ``_decl.*-defined`` kind for an
1089:     add/modify, or the paired ``_decl.*-retired``/``-removed`` tombstone for a
1090:     removal. ``payload`` is the whole document payload for a definition, or
1091:     ``None`` for a removal. ``annotation`` is the ``change=`` legibility label —
1092:     ``"added"``, ``"modified"``, or ``"removed"`` (SPEC §9.2: "MAY carry a
1093:     ``change`` annotation"). Emitters ride ``annotation`` on the row as
1094:     ``change=``; the resolver ignores it.
1095:     """
1096: 
1097:     kind: str
1098:     subject: str
1099:     payload: dict[str, Any] | None
1100:     annotation: str
1101: 
1102: 
1103: def diff_documents(head: Any, new: Any) -> list[Change]:
1104:     """Diff a fold head against a freshly-parsed document set → changes.
1105: 
1106:     ``head`` and ``new`` are each an iterable of documents — :class:`Document`
1107:     namedtuples or plain ``{"kind", "subject", "payload"}`` mappings (the
1108:     resolver returns the latter). Both are keyed by ``(kind, subject)``.
1109: 
1110:     Yields (in a deterministic order — additions/modifications in ``new``'s
1111:     iteration order, then removals in ``head``'s iteration order):
1112: 
1113:     - ``"added"`` — a ``(kind, subject)`` in ``new`` but not ``head``.
1114:     - ``"modified"`` — present in both, payloads differ by value.
1115:     - ``"removed"`` — in ``head`` but not ``new``; emitted as the paired
1116:       tombstone kind.
1117: 
1118:     Raises :class:`EditRefused` if a removal targets a singleton with no
1119:     tombstone (``_decl.lens-defined`` → vocabulary gap; ``_decl.vertex-defined``
1120:     → identity/rename). Unchanged subjects yield nothing.
1121:     """
1122:     head_map: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
1123:     for item in head:
1124:         k, s, p = _as_triple(item)
1125:         head_map[(k, s)] = p
1126: 
1127:     new_map: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
1128:     changes: list[Change] = []
1129:     for item in new:
1130:         k, s, p = _as_triple(item)
1131:         new_map[(k, s)] = p
1132:         prev = head_map.get((k, s))
1133:         if prev is None:
1134:             changes.append(Change(kind=k, subject=s, payload=p, annotation="added"))
1135:         elif prev != p:
1136:             changes.append(Change(kind=k, subject=s, payload=p, annotation="modified"))
1137:         # else unchanged → nothing
1138: 
1139:     for (k, s), _p in head_map.items():
1140:         if (k, s) in new_map:
1141:             continue
1142:         tombstone = DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE.get(k)
1143:         if tombstone is None:
1144:             if k == DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED:
1145:                 raise EditRefused(
1146:                     f"vertex identity change: the vertex-defined subject "
1147:                     f"{s!r} was removed (a rename). The vertex name is "
1148:                     "meaning-critical identity — a post-genesis rename is a "
1149:                     "deliberate ceremony, not an edit; not supported by "
1150:                     "re-absorb."
1151:                 )
1152:             raise EditRefused(
1153:                 f"cannot remove singleton {k!r} (subject {s!r}): the frozen "
1154:                 "declaration vocabulary has no tombstone for it "
1155:                 "(SPEC §9.2 — a lens is replaced by re-definition, never "
1156:                 "removed). Re-add the lens, or edit it in place; removal is a "
1157:                 "pending coordinated vocabulary change "
1158:                 "(thread:decl-lens-tombstone-vocab-gap)."
1159:             )
1160:         changes.append(Change(kind=tombstone, subject=s, payload=None, annotation="removed"))
1161: 
1162:     return changes
1163: 
1164: 
1165: def apply_changes(head: Any, changes: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
1166:     """Fold ``changes`` over ``head`` → the resulting document set (pure).
1167: 
1168:     Mirrors the store resolver's overlay/tombstone fold
1169:     (``engine.declaration.resolve_declaration_documents``) at the grammar tier,
1170:     with NO store: a ``*-defined`` change replaces its ``(kind, subject)``
1171:     document; a tombstone removes the paired ``*-defined`` subject. Returned
1172:     documents are ``{"kind", "subject", "payload"}`` dicts in insertion order
1173:     (definitions keep position across replacement).
1174: 
1175:     This is the reconstruction oracle for the diff property test:
1176:     ``apply_changes(head, diff_documents(head, new))`` equals ``new`` as a
1177:     document set. It intentionally re-implements the fold independently of the
1178:     engine resolver so the two agree by construction, not by shared code.
1179:     """
1180:     docs: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
1181:     for item in head:
1182:         k, s, p = _as_triple(item)
1183:         docs[(k, s)] = {"kind": k, "subject": s, "payload": p}
1184: 
1185:     for ch in changes:
1186:         kind, subject, payload, _annotation = (
1187:             (ch.kind, ch.subject, ch.payload, ch.annotation)
1188:             if isinstance(ch, Change)
1189:             else (ch["kind"], ch["subject"], ch.get("payload"), ch.get("change"))
1190:         )
1191:         defined = _TOMBSTONE_TO_DEFINED.get(kind)
1192:         if defined is not None:
1193:             docs.pop((defined, subject), None)
1194:         else:
1195:             docs[(kind, subject)] = {
1196:                 "kind": kind,
1197:                 "subject": subject,
1198:                 "payload": payload if payload is not None else {},
1199:             }
1200: 
1201:     return list(docs.values())

=== scripts/arrival_rehearsal.py ===
1: """Run a one-shot Arrival migration rehearsal on already-copied inputs.
2: 
3: All input, state, credentials, store, export and evidence paths must resolve
4: inside an explicit disposable sandbox. This script never opens the original
5: legacy store or its keys and never repairs rejected legacy rows.
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: import argparse
11: import hashlib
12: import json
13: import os
14: import sys
15: import tempfile
16: from contextlib import suppress
17: from pathlib import Path
18: from typing import Any
19: 
20: 
21: class RehearsalRefused(Exception):
22:     """An input or sandbox boundary is not suitable for an offline rehearsal."""
23: 
24: 
25: def _progress(stage: str) -> None:
26:     print(f"[arrival-rehearsal] {stage}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
27: 
28: 
29: def _inside(root: Path, value: Path, label: str, *, must_exist: bool) -> Path:
30:     try:
31:         resolved = value.expanduser().resolve(strict=must_exist)
32:     except OSError as exc:
33:         raise RehearsalRefused(f"{label} is unavailable") from exc
34:     if resolved == root or root not in resolved.parents:
35:         raise RehearsalRefused(f"{label} must be strictly inside the sandbox")
36:     return resolved
37: 
38: 
39: def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
40:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
41:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
42:         for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
43:             digest.update(chunk)
44:     return digest.hexdigest()
45: 
46: 
47: def _sha256_prefix(path: Path, size: int) -> str:
48:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
49:     remaining = size
50:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
51:         while remaining:
52:             chunk = stream.read(min(1024 * 1024, remaining))
53:             if not chunk:
54:                 raise RehearsalRefused("migrated custody prefix was truncated")
55:             digest.update(chunk)
56:             remaining -= len(chunk)
57:     return digest.hexdigest()
58: 
59: 
60: def _close(handle: object) -> None:
61:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
62:     if callable(close):
63:         with suppress(Exception):
64:             close()
65: 
66: 
67: def _head(head: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
68:     return {
69:         "lineage": head.lineage,
70:         "ordinal": head.ordinal,
71:         "record_hash": head.record_hash,
72:     }
73: 
74: 
75: def _refusal_evidence(exc: Exception) -> dict[str, Any]:
76:     """Retain SDK proof coordinates, never exception messages or row bodies."""
77:     result: dict[str, Any] = {"type": type(exc).__name__}
78:     source_type = getattr(exc, "source_type", None)
79:     if isinstance(source_type, str):
80:         result["source_type"] = source_type
81:     details = getattr(exc, "details", None)
82:     if isinstance(details, dict):
83:         head = details.get("captured_head")
84:         if isinstance(head, dict) and set(head) == {"lineage", "ordinal", "record_hash"}:
85:             result["captured_head"] = head
86:         proof = details.get("evidence")
87:         if isinstance(proof, dict):
88:             if isinstance(proof.get("phase"), str):
89:                 result["phase"] = proof["phase"]
90:             effects = proof.get("effects")
91:             if isinstance(effects, dict):
92:                 result["effects"] = {
93:                     resource: {"attempt": effect.get("attempt"), "state": effect.get("state")}
94:                     for resource, effect in effects.items()
95:                     if resource in {"custody", "witness", "derived", "cache", "artifact", "dispatch"}
96:                     and isinstance(effect, dict)
97:                     and effect.get("attempt") in {"not-entered", "entered"}
98:                     and effect.get("state") in {
99:                         "not-attempted", "known-none", "committed", "unknown", "completed", "incomplete"
100:                     }
101:                 }
102:     current: BaseException | None = exc
103:     for _ in range(3):
104:         current = current.__cause__ if current is not None else None
105:         if current is None:
106:             break
107:         issue = getattr(current, "issue", None)
108:         reason = getattr(issue, "reason", None)
109:         if reason in {
110:             "prior-incarnation", "ambiguous-tick-role", "unproven-loop-role",
111:             "unproven-generated-incarnation", "invalid-projection-shape",
112:             "runtime-namespace-mismatch", "reserved-runtime-name",
113:         }:
114:             result["boundary_reason"] = reason
115:             tick_id = getattr(issue, "tick_id", None)
116:             if isinstance(tick_id, str):
117:                 result["tick_id"] = tick_id
118:             tick_ordinal = getattr(issue, "tick_ordinal", None)
119:             if isinstance(tick_ordinal, int):
120:                 result["tick_ordinal"] = tick_ordinal
121:             break
122:     return result
123: 
124: 
125: def _save(path: Path, evidence: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
126:     encoded = json.dumps(evidence, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
127:     fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
128:     temporary = Path(name)
129:     try:
130:         os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
131:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
132:             stream.write(encoded + "\n")
133:             stream.flush()
134:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
135:         temporary.replace(path)
136:         directory_fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
137:         try:
138:             os.fsync(directory_fd)
139:         finally:
140:             os.close(directory_fd)
141:     finally:
142:         temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
143: 
144: 
145: def _check_paths(args: argparse.Namespace) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path, Path, Path]:
146:     root = Path(args.sandbox).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
147:     if not root.is_dir():
148:         raise RehearsalRefused("sandbox must be an existing directory")
149:     if any((parent / ".git").exists() for parent in (root, *root.parents)):
150:         raise RehearsalRefused("sandbox must be outside every repository checkout")
151:     vertex = _inside(root, Path(args.vertex), "copied vertex", must_exist=True)
152:     source = _inside(root, Path(args.source), "copied source", must_exist=True)
153:     keys = _inside(root, Path(args.legacy_key_dir), "copied legacy key directory", must_exist=True)
154:     output = _inside(root, Path(args.output), "output directory", must_exist=False)
155:     if not vertex.is_file() or not source.is_file() or not keys.is_dir():
156:         raise RehearsalRefused("vertex/source must be files and legacy key path a directory")
157:     if keys != (vertex.parent / "keys").resolve():
158:         raise RehearsalRefused("copied legacy key directory must be beside the copied vertex")
159:     private_key = keys / "ed25519.key"
160:     if private_key.is_symlink():
161:         raise RehearsalRefused("copied legacy key file must not be a symlink")
162:     private_key = _inside(root, private_key, "copied legacy key", must_exist=True)
163:     if not private_key.is_file():
164:         raise RehearsalRefused("copied legacy key must be a file")
165:     if output.exists():
166:         raise RehearsalRefused("output directory already exists; each run needs a fresh output")
167:     if any(output in path.parents or path in output.parents for path in (vertex, source, keys)):
168:         raise RehearsalRefused("output directory must be separate from copied inputs")
169:     return root, vertex, source, keys, output
170: 
171: 
172: def _check_vertex(vertex: Path, source: Path, observer: str) -> None:
173:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
174: 
175:     ast = parse_vertex_file(vertex)
176:     if ast.name != observer:
177:         raise RehearsalRefused("observer must equal the copied vertex self name")
178:     if ast.store_backend is not None or ast.store is None:
179:         raise RehearsalRefused("copied vertex must still declare a legacy store")
180:     named_source = (vertex.parent / ast.store).resolve()
181:     if named_source != source:
182:         raise RehearsalRefused("copied vertex store path must name the copied source")
183: 
184: 
185: def _isolated_environment(output: Path) -> None:
186:     runtime = output / "runtime"
187:     for name, leaf in (
188:         ("XDG_STATE_HOME", "state"),
189:         ("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", "config"),
190:         ("XDG_DATA_HOME", "data"),
191:         ("XDG_CACHE_HOME", "cache"),
192:         ("LOOPS_HOME", "loops"),
193:     ):
194:         path = runtime / leaf
195:         path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
196:         os.environ[name] = str(path)
197: 
198: 
199: def _verify_signature(key: str, signature: str, digest: str, domain: str) -> bool:
200:     from sign import ed25519
201: 
202:     try:
203:         public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
204:     except ValueError:
205:         return False
206:     return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=domain)
207: 
208: 
209: def _verify_rehearsal_emit(
210:     vertex: Path, fact_id: str, commit: Any, public_key: str
211: ) -> None:
212:     from custody.signing import ARRIVAL_DOMAIN, FACT_DOMAIN
213:     from engine.admission import fact_commitment_hash
214:     from engine.arrival import content_commitment
215:     from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry, descriptor_for
216:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
217: 
218:     matching = [
219:         record
220:         for record in commit.records
221:         if record.get("k") == "fact"
222:         and isinstance(record.get("body"), dict)
223:         and record["body"].get("id") == fact_id
224:     ]
225:     if len(matching) != 1:
226:         raise RehearsalRefused("exact ordinary commit lacks one emitted fact")
227:     committed = matching[0]
228:     ordinal = committed.get("ord")
229:     if not isinstance(ordinal, int):
230:         raise RehearsalRefused("emitted fact has no physical Arrival ordinal")
231:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
232:     if descriptor is None:
233:         raise RehearsalRefused("emitted target lost its Arrival descriptor")
234:     ledger, query = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends().open(descriptor)
235:     try:
236:         record = ledger.read(ordinal)
237:     finally:
238:         _close(query)
239:         _close(ledger)
240:     if record != committed:
241:         raise RehearsalRefused("committed emitted fact differs from its physical Arrival row")
242:     body = record["body"]
243:     assert isinstance(body, dict)
244:     inner = body.get("signature")
245:     outer = record.get("sig")
246:     if not isinstance(inner, str) or not isinstance(outer, str):
247:         raise RehearsalRefused("rehearsal emit is missing a signature")
248:     fact_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
249:         body["kind"], body["ts"], body["observer"], body["origin"], body["payload"]
250:     )
251:     arrival_digest = content_commitment(
252:         record["k"], record["at"], record["observer"], record["origin"], body
253:     )
254:     if not _verify_signature(public_key, inner, fact_digest, FACT_DOMAIN):
255:         raise RehearsalRefused("rehearsal FACT signature did not verify")
256:     if not _verify_signature(public_key, outer, arrival_digest, ARRIVAL_DOMAIN):
257:         raise RehearsalRefused("rehearsal ARRIVAL signature did not verify")
258: 
259: 
260: def _exact_export(vertex: Path, output: Path, head: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
261:     from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry, descriptor_for
262:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
263:     from sdk import export_target
264: 
265:     published = export_target(vertex, output, through=head)
266:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
267:     if descriptor is None:
268:         raise RehearsalRefused("export target has no Arrival descriptor")
269:     ledger, query = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends().open(descriptor)
270:     try:
271:         exact = ledger.export(through=head, codec=published.codec)
272:         digest = hashlib.sha256()
273:         size = 0
274:         for chunk in exact.records:
275:             digest.update(chunk)
276:             size += len(chunk)
277:     finally:
278:         _close(query)
279:         _close(ledger)
280:     artifact_hash = _sha256(output)
281:     if exact.head != head or size != published.byte_count or digest.hexdigest() != artifact_hash:
282:         raise RehearsalRefused("published export differs from exact bounded ledger export")
283:     return {
284:         "head": _head(head), "byte_count": size,
285:         "sha256": artifact_hash, "exact_stream_match": True,
286:     }
287: 
288: 
289: def run_rehearsal(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, Any]:
290:     root, vertex, source, keys, output = _check_paths(args)
291:     _check_vertex(vertex, source, args.observer)
292:     try:
293:         payload = json.loads(args.emit_payload_json)
294:     except ValueError as exc:
295:         raise RehearsalRefused("emit payload must be valid JSON") from exc
296:     if not isinstance(payload, dict):
297:         raise RehearsalRefused("emit payload must be a JSON object")
298:     if not args.emit_kind or args.emit_kind.startswith("_decl."):
299:         raise RehearsalRefused("emit kind must be an explicit ordinary kind")
300: 
301:     output.mkdir(mode=0o700)
302:     _isolated_environment(output)
303:     evidence_path = output / "evidence.json"
304:     evidence: dict[str, Any] = {
305:         "schema": "loops.rehearsal/arrival/v1",
306:         "status": "running", "stage": "source-hash",
307:         "sandbox": str(root), "copied_source": str(source),
308:         "copied_vertex": str(vertex), "output": str(output),
309:         "rehearsal_only": True,
310:     }
311:     migration_target: Path | None = None
312:     emit_target_before_hash: str | None = None
313:     try:
314:         _progress("hashing copied source")
315:         before = _sha256(source)
316:         evidence["source_sha256_before"] = before
317:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
318: 
319:         from custody.signing import ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
320:         from engine.arrival_contract import Full
321:         from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry, descriptor_for
322:         from engine.compiler import compile_vertex
323:         from lang import parse_vertex_file
324:         from migrate.inventory import inventory
325:         from migrate.sidecar import run_migration, verify_migration_report
326:         from sdk import (
327:             MappedCredentialProvider,
328:             adopt_arrival,
329:             emit_fact,
330:             inspect_declaration,
331:             read_facts,
332:             read_state,
333:             read_summary,
334:             sync_target,
335:             verify_target,
336:         )
337:         from sdk.read import _serialize_fold_section
338:         from sign import ed25519
339: 
340:         evidence["stage"] = "inventory"
341:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
342:         _progress("inventory of copied source")
343:         inv = inventory(source)
344:         evidence["inventory"] = {
345:             "format": inv.source_format, "total_rows": inv.total_rows,
346:             "total_lines": inv.total_lines, "tick_count": inv.tick_count,
347:             "batch_line_count": inv.batch_line_count,
348:             "per_kind_counts": inv.per_kind_counts,
349:             "observer_census": inv.observer_census,
350:             "id_era_census": inv.id_era_census,
351:             "content_sha256": inv.content_hash, "file_sha256": inv.file_hash,
352:         }
353:         if inv.file_hash != before:
354:             raise RehearsalRefused("copied source changed during inventory")
355: 
356:         pair = ed25519.load(keys)
357:         declared = parse_vertex_file(vertex)
358:         selected_key = None
359:         for document in declared.observers or ():
360:             if document.name == args.observer:
361:                 selected_key = document.key
362:                 break
363:         if selected_key != pair.public_b64:
364:             raise RehearsalRefused("copied self key differs from reviewed declaration")
365: 
366:         def signer(named: str, digest: str) -> str | None:
367:             if named != args.observer:
368:                 return None
369:             return ed25519.sign(pair, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN)
370: 
371:         evidence["stage"] = "migration"
372:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
373:         _progress("migrating copied source")
374:         reviewed_text = vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
375:         reviewed_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode()).hexdigest()
376:         reviewed_path = output / "reviewed.vertex"
377:         reviewed_path.write_text(reviewed_text, encoding="utf-8")
378:         reviewed_path.chmod(0o600)
379:         evidence["reviewed_snapshot"] = {
380:             "path": str(reviewed_path), "sha256": reviewed_sha256,
381:         }
382:         staging = output / "stores"
383:         staging.mkdir()
384:         migration = run_migration(source, vertex, store_dir=staging, signer=signer)
385:         migration_target = migration.target_path
386:         prefix_size = migration.target_path.stat().st_size
387:         prefix_hash = _sha256_prefix(migration.target_path, prefix_size)
388:         report_hash = _sha256(migration.report_path)
389:         evidence["migration"] = {
390:             "selected_head": _head(migration.head),
391:             "lineage": migration.lineage,
392:             "target_path": str(migration.target_path),
393:             "prefix_byte_count": prefix_size,
394:             "prefix_sha256": prefix_hash,
395:             "report_sha256": report_hash,
396:             "keyless_declared_observer_count": len(
397:                 migration.exceptions.keyless_declared_observers
398:             ),
399:             "undeclared_row_observer_count": len(
400:                 migration.exceptions.undeclared_row_observers
401:             ),
402:             "dropped_unit_count": len(migration.exceptions.dropped_units),
403:         }
404: 
405:         evidence["stage"] = "report-verify-at-S"
406:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
407:         _progress("verifying migration report at selected S")
408:         verified_report = verify_migration_report(
409:             migration.report_path, pair.public_b64,
410:             verify=lambda key, signature, digest: _verify_signature(
411:                 key, signature, digest, ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
412:             ),
413:             target_path=migration.target_path,
414:         )
415:         if not verified_report:
416:             raise RehearsalRefused("migration report did not verify at S")
417:         descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
418:         if descriptor is None or descriptor.lineage != migration.lineage:
419:             raise RehearsalRefused("published descriptor does not name migrated lineage")
420:         ledger, query = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends().open(descriptor)
421:         try:
422:             if ledger.verify(Full(through=migration.head)) != migration.head:
423:                 raise RehearsalRefused("migration S did not Full-verify")
424:         finally:
425:             _close(query)
426:             _close(ledger)
427:         evidence["report_verified_at_S"] = True
428: 
429:         evidence["stage"] = "mapped-import-and-adoption"
430:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
431:         _progress("importing copied key and adopting reviewed declaration")
432:         provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
433:             output / "credentials",
434:             namespace="arrival-rehearsal",
435:             receipt_observer=args.observer,
436:         )
437:         imported = provider.import_legacy(
438:             vertex, args.observer, token=f"rehearsal-{before[:24]}"
439:         )
440:         if imported.public_key != pair.public_b64:
441:             raise RehearsalRefused("mapped imported key differs from copied self key")
442:         adopted = adopt_arrival(
443:             vertex, selected_head=migration.head,
444:             reviewed_text=reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256=reviewed_sha256,
445:             declaration_text=vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
446:             observer=args.observer, credentials=provider,
447:             runtime_epoch=args.runtime_epoch,
448:         )
449:         if adopted.commit is None or adopted.commit.before != migration.head:
450:             raise RehearsalRefused("adoption did not report Commit(S,A)")
451:         if adopted.runtime_epoch != args.runtime_epoch:
452:             raise RehearsalRefused("adoption result runtime epoch differs from request")
453:         a = adopted.commit.after
454:         evidence["adoption"] = {
455:             "head": _head(a), "fact_id_equals_lineage": adopted.fact_id == migration.lineage,
456:             "phase": adopted.phase,
457:             "runtime_epoch": adopted.runtime_epoch,
458:             "projected_after": None if adopted.projection is None else adopted.projection.get(
459:                 "projected_after"
460:             ),
461:         }
462: 
463:         evidence["stage"] = "ordinary-reads"
464:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
465:         _progress("verifying adopted reads")
466:         verified_a = verify_target(vertex, through=a)
467:         inspected = inspect_declaration(vertex)
468:         summary = read_summary(vertex)
469:         page = read_facts(vertex, limit=1)
470:         state = read_state(vertex)
471:         state_epoch = state.generation.get("runtime_epoch")
472:         expected_epoch = (
473:             {"mode": "fresh", "anchor_ordinal": a.ordinal}
474:             if args.runtime_epoch == "fresh"
475:             else {"mode": "strict", "anchor_ordinal": None}
476:         )
477:         if state_epoch != expected_epoch:
478:             raise RehearsalRefused("adopted state runtime epoch does not match adoption")
479:         expected_kinds = dict(inv.per_kind_counts)
480:         for dropped in migration.exceptions.dropped_units:
481:             for kind in dropped.fact_kinds:
482:                 remaining = expected_kinds.get(kind)
483:                 if remaining is None:
484:                     raise RehearsalRefused("dropped migration unit has no inventoried kind")
485:                 if remaining == 1:
486:                     del expected_kinds[kind]
487:                 else:
488:                     expected_kinds[kind] = remaining - 1
489:         observed_kinds = {
490:             kind: int(values["count"])
491:             for kind, values in summary.kinds.items()
492:         }
493:         if (
494:             observed_kinds != expected_kinds
495:             or summary.fact_total != sum(expected_kinds.values())
496:             or summary.tick_total != inv.tick_count
497:         ):
498:             raise RehearsalRefused("adopted history differs from the inventoried migration")
499:         initial_state_matches_declared: bool | None = None
500:         if args.runtime_epoch == "fresh":
501:             declared_initial = {
502:                 name: _serialize_fold_section(spec.replay([]))
503:                 for name, spec in compile_vertex(declared).items()
504:             }
505:             initial_state_matches_declared = state.sections == declared_initial
506:             if not initial_state_matches_declared:
507:                 raise RehearsalRefused("fresh state at adoption differs from declared initial values")
508:         if not isinstance(state_epoch, dict):
509:             raise RehearsalRefused("adopted state lacks runtime-epoch evidence")
510:         state_sections = json.dumps(
511:             state.sections,
512:             sort_keys=True,
513:             separators=(",", ":"),
514:             ensure_ascii=False,
515:             allow_nan=False,
516:         ).encode("utf-8")
517:         if (
518:             verified_a.verified_through != a
519:             or summary.basis is None
520:             or summary.basis.captured_head != a
521:             or summary.basis.projected_through != a
522:             or inspected.basis is None
523:             or inspected.basis.captured_head != a
524:         ):
525:             raise RehearsalRefused("adopted verification/read basis failed")
526:         evidence["reads_at_A"] = {
527:             "head": _head(a), "declaration_status": inspected.status,
528:             "fact_total": summary.fact_total, "tick_total": summary.tick_total,
529:             "sample_fact_count": len(page.items),
530:             "history_matches_inventory": True,
531:             "summary_basis": None if summary.basis is None else _head(
532:                 summary.basis.captured_head
533:             ),
534:             "runtime_state": {
535:                 "runtime_epoch": state_epoch,
536:                 "sections_sha256": hashlib.sha256(state_sections).hexdigest(),
537:                 "declared_initial": initial_state_matches_declared,
538:             },
539:         }
540: 
541:         evidence["stage"] = "rehearsal-only-emit"
542:         emit_target_before_hash = _sha256(migration.target_path)
543:         evidence["rehearsal_emit_precondition"] = {
544:             "head": _head(a), "target_sha256": emit_target_before_hash,
545:         }
546:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
547:         _progress("writing one rehearsal-only signed fact")
548:         emitted = emit_fact(
549:             vertex, args.emit_kind, payload, observer=args.observer,
550:             origin="arrival-rehearsal-only", credentials=provider,
551:         )
552:         if not emitted.stored or not emitted.signed or emitted.commit is None:
553:             raise RehearsalRefused("rehearsal emit lacks a signed custody commit")
554:         _verify_rehearsal_emit(vertex, emitted.id, emitted.commit, pair.public_b64)
555:         synced = sync_target(vertex)
556:         final_head = emitted.commit.after
557:         if synced.projected_after != final_head:
558:             raise RehearsalRefused("projection did not reach rehearsal emit head")
559:         if verify_target(vertex, through=final_head).verified_through != final_head:
560:             raise RehearsalRefused("final rehearsal head did not Full-verify")
561:         evidence["rehearsal_emit"] = {
562:             "rehearsal_only": True, "origin": "arrival-rehearsal-only",
563:             "fact_id": emitted.id, "commit_before": _head(emitted.commit.before),
564:             "head": _head(final_head), "signed_fact_and_arrival": True,
565:             "physical_fact_matches_commit": True,
566:             "projected_after": _head(synced.projected_after),
567:             "full_verified": True,
568:         }
569: 
570:         evidence["stage"] = "exact-export"
571:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
572:         _progress("publishing and comparing exact export")
573:         evidence["export"] = _exact_export(
