574:             vertex, output / "final.arrival-jsonl", final_head
575:         )
576:         evidence["migration"]["prefix_unchanged_after_emit"] = (
577:             _sha256_prefix(migration.target_path, prefix_size) == prefix_hash
578:         )
579:         evidence["migration"]["report_unchanged_after_emit"] = (
580:             _sha256(migration.report_path) == report_hash
581:         )
582:         if not all((
583:             evidence["migration"]["prefix_unchanged_after_emit"],
584:             evidence["migration"]["report_unchanged_after_emit"],
585:         )):
586:             raise RehearsalRefused("migration evidence changed after adoption or emit")
587:         evidence["source_sha256_after"] = _sha256(source)
588:         evidence["source_unchanged"] = evidence["source_sha256_after"] == before
589:         if not evidence["source_unchanged"]:
590:             raise RehearsalRefused("copied source changed during rehearsal")
591:         evidence["status"] = "complete"
592:         evidence["stage"] = "complete"
593:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
594:         _progress("complete")
595:         return evidence
596:     except Exception as exc:
597:         evidence["status"] = "refused"
598:         evidence["refusal"] = _refusal_evidence(exc)
599:         evidence["error_type"] = type(exc).__name__
600:         if migration_target is not None and emit_target_before_hash is not None:
601:             try:
602:                 after_hash = _sha256(migration_target)
603:                 evidence["target_sha256_after_refusal"] = after_hash
604:                 evidence["target_unchanged_after_refusal"] = (
605:                     after_hash == emit_target_before_hash
606:                 )
607:             except OSError:
608:                 evidence["target_unchanged_after_refusal"] = None
609:         if "source_sha256_before" in evidence:
610:             try:
611:                 evidence["source_sha256_after"] = _sha256(source)
612:                 evidence["source_unchanged"] = (
613:                     evidence["source_sha256_after"] == evidence["source_sha256_before"]
614:                 )
615:             except OSError:
616:                 evidence["source_unchanged"] = None
617:         _save(evidence_path, evidence)
618:         raise
619: 
620: 
621: def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
622:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
623:     parser.add_argument("--sandbox", required=True, type=Path)
624:     parser.add_argument("--vertex", required=True, type=Path)
625:     parser.add_argument("--source", required=True, type=Path)
626:     parser.add_argument("--legacy-key-dir", required=True, type=Path)
627:     parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
628:     parser.add_argument("--observer", required=True)
629:     parser.add_argument("--runtime-epoch", choices=("strict", "fresh"), default="strict")
630:     parser.add_argument("--emit-kind", required=True)
631:     parser.add_argument("--emit-payload-json", required=True)
632:     args = parser.parse_args(argv)
633:     try:
634:         run_rehearsal(args)
635:     except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - show only type; private detail stays off stdout
636:         # The private evidence file retains a bounded stage/type. Never print
637:         # legacy row contents or key material from a third-party exception.
638:         _progress(f"refused: {type(exc).__name__}")
639:         return 2
640:     return 0
641: 
642: 
643: if __name__ == "__main__":
644:     raise SystemExit(main())

=== tests/test_arrival_migration_boundary.py ===
1: """Public SDK boundary checks for the offline migration sidecar.
2: 
3: These tests intentionally live in the repository integration suite: the SDK
4: must remain installable without making the quarantined ``migrate`` package a
5: runtime or test dependency.
6: """

46: @pytest.mark.parametrize("runtime_epoch", ["strict", "fresh"])
47: def test_migrated_owned_ticks_have_an_explicit_execution_epoch(
48:     tmp_path: Path, runtime_epoch: str,
49: ) -> None:
50:     """Fresh execution starts empty without severing historical custody."""
51:     from engine.canonical_audit import audit_deep
52:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival, export_target, read_state
53: 
54:     data = tmp_path / "data"
55:     data.mkdir()
56:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
57:         tmp_path / "credentials", namespace="epoch-rehearsal", receipt_observer="alice",
58:     )
59:     binding = provider.create_binding("alice", token="epoch-key")
60:     source = data / "legacy.jsonl"
61:     _write_jsonl(source)
62:     rows = [json.loads(line) for line in source.read_text().splitlines()]
63:     rows[1].update(name="alice", origin="alice")
64:     source.write_text("".join(json.dumps(row) + "\n" for row in rows))
65:     original = source.read_bytes()
66:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
67:     _write_vertex(vertex, binding.public_key, source.name)
68:     vertex.write_text(vertex.read_text().replace(
69:         "loops {", 'loops {\n  boundary when="seal"\n  seal { fold { items "collect" 10 } }',
70:     ))
71:     reviewed = vertex.read_text()
72:     signer = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
73:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
74:         CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
75:     ))
76:     assert signer is not None
77:     migration = run_migration(
78:         source, vertex, store_dir=data,
79:         signer=lambda _observer, digest: signer.sign_digest(digest),
80:     )
81:     assert verify_migration_report(
82:         migration.report_path, binding.public_key, verify=_verifier,
83:         target_path=migration.target_path,
84:     )
85:     prefix = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
86:     adopted = adopt_arrival(
87:         vertex, selected_head=migration.head, reviewed_text=reviewed,
88:         reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
89:         declaration_text=vertex.read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=provider,
90:         runtime_epoch=runtime_epoch,
91:     )
92:     assert adopted.head.ordinal == migration.head.ordinal + 1
93:     assert read_summary(vertex).fact_total == 1
94:     assert read_summary(vertex).tick_total == 1
95:     assert read_fact_by_id(vertex, rows[0]["id"]).fact is not None
96:     at_adoption = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
97:     if runtime_epoch == "strict":
98:         with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="predates declaration genesis"):
99:             emit_fact(vertex, "concept", {"text": "new"}, observer="alice", credentials=provider)
100:         assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == at_adoption
101:         return
102: 
103:     assert read_state(vertex).sections["concept"]["items"] == []
104:     # An old ID remains occupied even though it is excluded from runtime replay.
105:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
106:         emit_fact(
107:             vertex, "concept", {"text": "different"}, observer="alice",
108:             id_override=rows[0]["id"], credentials=provider,
109:         )
110:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == at_adoption
111:     emitted = emit_fact(
112:         vertex, "concept", {"text": "new"}, observer="alice", ts=10.0,
113:         credentials=provider,
114:     )
115:     assert emitted.stored and emitted.signed and emitted.witnessed
116:     state = read_state(vertex).sections["concept"]["items"]
117:     assert [item["text"] for item in state] == ["new"]
118:     # A new vertex boundary must keep the physical predecessor, despite fresh state.
119:     sealed = emit_fact(
120:         vertex, "seal", {"message": "epoch checkpoint"}, observer="alice", ts=-1.0,
121:         credentials=provider,
122:     )
123:     assert sealed.stored and sealed.signed
124:     assert read_summary(vertex).tick_total == 2
125:     # Even a negative event timestamp remains the latest physical predecessor.
126:     emit_fact(
127:         vertex, "seal", {"message": "second checkpoint"}, observer="alice", ts=12.0,
128:         credentials=provider,
129:     )
130:     assert read_summary(vertex).tick_total == 3
131:     chain = audit_deep(migration.target_path)
132:     assert chain.ok, chain.summary()
133:     exported = tmp_path / "fresh-export.arrival-jsonl"
134:     export_target(vertex, exported)
135:     assert exported.read_bytes() == migration.target_path.read_bytes()
136:     assert exported.read_bytes().startswith(prefix)
137:     assert source.read_bytes() == original

140: @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
141: def _isolate_runtime(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
142:     """Keep custody and projection state out of the user's environment."""
143:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "state"))
144:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(tmp_path / "config"))
145:     monkeypatch.setenv("LOOPS_HOME", str(tmp_path / "loops"))

147: 
148: class _StaticCredentials:
149:     def __init__(self, pair) -> None:
150:         self.pair = pair
151: 
152:     def for_write(self, _vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials:
153:         return WriteCredentials(
154:             fact_signer=lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
155:                 self.pair, digest.encode(), domain=FACT_DOMAIN
156:             ),
157:             arrival_signer=lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
158:                 self.pair, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
159:             ),
160:         )
161: 
162: 
163: def _signer(pair):
164:     return lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
165:         pair, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
166:     )
167: 
168: 
169: def _verifier(key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
170:     try:
171:         public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
172:     except ValueError:
173:         return False
174:     return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN)
175: 
176: 
177: def _write_vertex(path: Path, public_key: str, source_name: str) -> None:
178:     path.write_text(
179:         f'''name "alice"
180: store "./data/{source_name}"
181: 
182: observers {{
183:   alice {{
184:     key "{public_key}"
185:   }}
186: }}
187: 
188: loops {{
189:   concept {{ fold {{ items "collect" 100 }} }}
190: }}
191: ''',
192:         encoding="utf-8",
193:     )
194: 
195: 
196: def _write_jsonl(path: Path) -> None:
197:     rows = [
198:         {
199:             "t": "fact",
200:             "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0",
201:             "kind": "concept",
202:             "ts": 1000.0,
203:             "observer": "alice",
204:             "origin": "legacy",
205:             "payload": '{"text":"migrated"}',
206:         },
207:         {
208:             "t": "tick",
209:             "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
210:             "name": "heartbeat",
211:             "ts": 1001.0,
212:             "since": 1000.0,
213:             "origin": "system",
214:             "payload": '{"seq":1}',
215:             "prev_hash": None,
216:             "window_start": None,
217:             "fact_cursor": None,
218:             "window_hash": None,
219:         },
220:     ]
221:     path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(row, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n" for row in rows))
222: 
223: 
224: def _write_sqlite(path: Path) -> None:
225:     with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
226:         conn.executescript(
227:             """
228:             CREATE TABLE facts (
229:                 id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, kind TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL,
230:                 observer TEXT NOT NULL, origin TEXT NOT NULL,
231:                 payload TEXT NOT NULL, signature TEXT
232:             );
233:             CREATE TABLE ticks (
234:                 id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL,
235:                 since REAL, origin TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL,
236:                 prev_hash TEXT, window_start TEXT, fact_cursor TEXT,
237:                 window_hash TEXT, signature TEXT
238:             );
239:             INSERT INTO facts VALUES
240:               ('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0', 'concept', 1000.0,
241:                'alice', 'legacy', '{"text":"migrated"}', NULL);
242:             INSERT INTO ticks VALUES
243:               ('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1', 'heartbeat', 1001.0, 1000.0,
244:                'system', '{"seq":1}', NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
245:             """
246:         )
247: 
248: 
249: @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_format", ["jsonl", "sqlite"])
250: def test_migrated_descriptor_reaches_public_sdk_boundary(
251:     tmp_path: Path, source_format: str
252: ) -> None:
253:     """Migration output is structurally readable, but lacks adopted declaration history."""
254:     data = tmp_path / "data"
255:     data.mkdir()
256:     source = data / ("legacy.jsonl" if source_format == "jsonl" else "legacy.sqlite")
257:     if source_format == "jsonl":
258:         _write_jsonl(source)
259:     else:
260:         _write_sqlite(source)
261:     source_before = source.read_bytes()
262: 
263:     pair = ed25519.load_or_generate(tmp_path / "keys" / "alice")
264:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
265:     _write_vertex(vertex, pair.public_b64, source.name)
266:     outcome = run_migration(source, vertex, store_dir=data, signer=_signer(pair))
267: 
268:     # The migration's low-level records and report remain independently verifiable.
269:     assert verify_migration_report(
270:         outcome.report_path,
271:         pair.public_b64,
272:         verify=_verifier,
273:         target_path=outcome.target_path,
274:     )
275:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
276:     assert descriptor is not None
277:     assert descriptor.backend == "file"
278:     assert descriptor.lineage == outcome.lineage
279:     assert descriptor.role is not None and descriptor.role.value == "authority"
280:     target_bytes_before_sync = outcome.target_path.read_bytes()
281:     registry = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
282:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
283:     try:
284:         head = ledger.verify(Full(through=outcome.head))
285:         records = list(ledger.scan(through=head))
286:         assert head == outcome.head
287:         assert [record["k"] for record in records] == ["genesis", "fact", "tick"]
288:         genesis, fact, tick = records
289:         assert genesis["v"] == 1
290:         assert genesis["lin"] == outcome.lineage
291:         assert genesis["ord"] == 0
292:         assert genesis["prev"] is None
293:         assert genesis["at"] == 0.0
294:         assert genesis["observer"] == "alice"
295:         assert genesis["origin"] == ""
296:         assert genesis["body"] == {
297:             "protocol": 1,
298:             "lineage": outcome.lineage,
299:             "key": pair.public_b64,
300:         }
301:         assert isinstance(genesis["sig"], str) and genesis["sig"]
302:         assert isinstance(genesis["rh"], str) and len(genesis["rh"]) == 64
303:         assert fact == {
304:             "v": 1,
305:             "lin": outcome.lineage,
306:             "ord": 1,
307:             "prev": genesis["rh"],
308:             "at": 1000.0,
309:             "k": "fact",
310:             "observer": "alice",
311:             "origin": "legacy",
312:             "body": {
313:                 "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0",
314:                 "kind": "concept",
315:                 "ts": 1000.0,
316:                 "observer": "alice",
317:                 "origin": "legacy",
318:                 "payload": '{"text":"migrated"}',
319:             },
320:             "rh": fact["rh"],
321:         }
322:         assert tick == {
323:             "v": 1,
324:             "lin": outcome.lineage,
325:             "ord": 2,
326:             "prev": fact["rh"],
327:             "at": 1001.0,
328:             "k": "tick",
329:             "observer": "alice",
330:             "origin": "system",
331:             "body": {
332:                 "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
333:                 "name": "heartbeat",
334:                 "ts": 1001.0,
335:                 "since": 1000.0,
336:                 "origin": "system",
337:                 "payload": '{"seq":1}',
338:                 "prev_hash": None,
339:                 "window_start": None,
340:                 "fact_cursor": None,
341:                 "window_hash": None,
342:             },
343:             "rh": tick["rh"],
344:         }
345:         assert "sig" not in fact
346:         assert "sig" not in tick
347:     finally:
348:         query.close()
349:         ledger.close()
350: 
351:     resolved = resolve_arrival_target(vertex)
352:     assert resolved.store.lineage == outcome.lineage
353:     assert resolved.store.role == "authority"
354:     verified = verify_target(vertex)
355:     assert verified.store == resolved.store
356:     assert verified.captured_head == outcome.head
357:     assert verified.verified_through == outcome.head
358:     synced = sync_target(vertex)
359:     assert synced.store == resolved.store
360:     assert synced.captured_head == outcome.head
361:     assert synced.target == outcome.head
362:     assert synced.projected_before is None
363:     assert synced.projected_after == outcome.head
364:     assert synced.agreement is True
365:     assert outcome.target_path.read_bytes() == target_bytes_before_sync
366: 
367:     # Reads and writes must not substitute the current locator for an absent
368:     # adopted declaration. These operations may create/update a derived index,
369:     # so compare the authority log and descriptor around all refusal checks.
370:     ledger_before_refusals = outcome.target_path.read_bytes()
371:     descriptor_before_refusals = vertex.read_bytes()
372:     operations = [
373:         lambda: read_summary(vertex),
374:         lambda: read_facts(vertex),
375:         lambda: read_fact_by_id(vertex, "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0"),
376:         lambda: read_ticks(vertex),
377:         lambda: inspect_declaration(vertex),
378:         lambda: edit_declaration(
379:             vertex,
380:             vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
381:             observer="alice",
382:             credentials=_StaticCredentials(pair),
383:         ),
384:         lambda: emit_fact(
385:             vertex,
386:             "concept",
387:             {"text": "must refuse"},
388:             observer="alice",
389:             credentials=_StaticCredentials(pair),
390:         ),
391:     ]
392:     for operation in operations:
393:         with pytest.raises(
394:             SdkError, match="adopted declaration|historized(?: effective)? declarations?"
395:         ):
396:             operation()
397:     assert outcome.target_path.read_bytes() == ledger_before_refusals
398:     assert vertex.read_bytes() == descriptor_before_refusals
399:     assert source.read_bytes() == source_before
400: 
401: 
402: @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_format", ["jsonl", "sqlite"])
403: def test_copied_migration_adoption_and_mapped_write(
404:     tmp_path: Path, source_format: str
405: ) -> None:
406:     """Rehearse the explicit source-copy -> migration -> adoption boundary.
407: 
408:     All originals here are synthetic. SQLite is copied through its backup API;
409:     the quiescent JSONL fixture is copied byte-for-byte. Report verification is
410:     deliberately performed at S, before adoption advances the ledger.
411:     """
412:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival, export_target, read_state
413: 
414:     original = tmp_path / "original"
415:     (original / "data").mkdir(parents=True)
416:     source = original / "data" / f"legacy.{source_format}"
417:     (_write_jsonl if source_format == "jsonl" else _write_sqlite)(source)
418:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
419:         tmp_path / "rehearsal-credentials",
420:         namespace="adoption-rehearsal",
421:         receipt_observer="alice",
422:     )
423:     binding = provider.create_binding("alice", token="rehearsal-alice")
424:     original_vertex = original / "alice.vertex"
425:     _write_vertex(original_vertex, binding.public_key, source.name)
426:     original_bytes = source.read_bytes()
427:     reviewed_bytes = original_vertex.read_bytes()
428:     reviewed_hash = hashlib.sha256(reviewed_bytes).hexdigest()
429: 
430:     rehearsal = tmp_path / "rehearsal"
431:     (rehearsal / "data").mkdir(parents=True)
432:     copied_source = rehearsal / "data" / source.name
433:     if source_format == "sqlite":
434:         with sqlite3.connect(source) as src, sqlite3.connect(copied_source) as dst:
435:             src.backup(dst)
436:     else:
437:         shutil.copyfile(source, copied_source)
438:     vertex = rehearsal / "alice.vertex"
439:     vertex.write_bytes(reviewed_bytes)
440:     copied_bytes = copied_source.read_bytes()
441:     registry_binding = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
442:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
443:         CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
444:     ))
445:     assert registry_binding is not None
446: 
447:     def migration_signer(observer: str, digest: str) -> str:
448:         assert observer == "alice"
449:         return registry_binding.sign_digest(digest)
450: 
451:     migrated = run_migration(
452:         copied_source, vertex, store_dir=rehearsal / "data", signer=migration_signer,
453:     )
454:     assert verify_migration_report(
455:         migrated.report_path, binding.public_key, verify=_verifier,
456:         target_path=migrated.target_path,
457:     )
458:     report_bytes = migrated.report_path.read_bytes()
459:     # External provenance stays outside the declaration's protocol payload.
460:     evidence = {
461:         "reviewed_sha256": reviewed_hash,
462:         "report_sha256": hashlib.sha256(report_bytes).hexdigest(),
463:         "report_verified_at": migrated.head.record_hash,
464:     }
465:     prefix = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
466:     descriptor = resolve_arrival_target(vertex).store
467:     published_text = vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
468:     sync_target(vertex)
469:     with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="adopted declaration|historized"):
470:         read_summary(vertex)
471: 
472:     # A caller cannot silently pass revised bytes under the reviewed hash.
473:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
474:         adopt_arrival(
475:             vertex, selected_head=migrated.head,
476:             reviewed_text=reviewed_bytes.decode() + "\n// unreviewed\n",
477:             reviewed_sha256=reviewed_hash, declaration_text=published_text,
478:             observer="alice", credentials=provider,
479:         )
480:     assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == prefix
481: 
482:     adopted = adopt_arrival(
483:         vertex, selected_head=migrated.head, reviewed_text=reviewed_bytes.decode(),
484:         reviewed_sha256=reviewed_hash, declaration_text=published_text,
485:         observer="alice", credentials=provider,
486:     )
487:     assert adopted.commit is not None
488:     assert adopted.commit.before == migrated.head
489:     assert adopted.head == adopted.commit.after
490:     assert adopted.head.ordinal == migrated.head.ordinal + 1
491:     assert adopted.fact_id == migrated.lineage
492:     assert resolve_arrival_target(vertex).store == descriptor
493:     after_adoption = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
494:     assert after_adoption.startswith(prefix)
495:     assert len(after_adoption.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + 1
496:     assert migrated.report_path.read_bytes() == report_bytes
497:     assert hashlib.sha256(report_bytes).hexdigest() == evidence["report_sha256"]
498: 
499:     for result in (
500:         inspect_declaration(vertex), read_summary(vertex), read_facts(vertex),
501:         read_ticks(vertex), read_state(vertex),
502:     ):
503:         assert result.basis is not None
504:         assert result.basis.captured_head == adopted.head
505:         json.dumps(result.as_dict(), allow_nan=False)
506:     facts = read_facts(vertex, include_internal=True, order="oldest", limit=100)
507:     anchors = [row for row in facts.items if row["kind"] == "_decl.genesis"]
508:     assert len(anchors) == 1 and anchors[0]["id"] == migrated.lineage
509:     original_fact = read_fact_by_id(vertex, "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0")
510:     assert original_fact.fact is not None
511: 
512:     receipt = emit_fact(
513:         vertex, "concept", {"text": "after adoption"}, observer="alice",
514:         id_override="adoption-rehearsal-followup", credentials=provider,
515:     )
516:     assert receipt.stored and receipt.signed and receipt.witnessed
517:     assert receipt.commit is not None and receipt.commit.before == adopted.head
518:     final = verify_target(vertex)
519:     assert final.verified_through == receipt.commit.after
520:     exported = rehearsal / "captured.jsonl"
521:     export = export_target(vertex, exported)
522:     assert export.head == receipt.commit.after
523:     assert exported.read_bytes() == migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
524:     for result in (adopted, receipt, final, export):
525:         json.dumps(result.as_dict(), allow_nan=False)
526:     assert source.read_bytes() == original_bytes
527:     assert original_vertex.read_bytes() == reviewed_bytes
528:     assert copied_source.read_bytes() == copied_bytes
529: 
530: 
531: @pytest.mark.parametrize("stop_phase", ["after-intent", "after-append"])
532: @pytest.mark.parametrize("runtime_epoch", ["strict", "fresh"])
533: def test_adoption_process_exit_recovers_reserved_draft_without_credentials(
534:     tmp_path: Path, stop_phase: str, runtime_epoch: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
535: ) -> None:
536:     """Exit a real child process at a durable boundary, then reconcile once."""
537:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, recover_arrival_adoption
538: 
539:     data = tmp_path / "data"
540:     data.mkdir()
541:     source = data / "legacy.jsonl"
542:     _write_jsonl(source)
543:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
544:         tmp_path / "credentials", namespace="interrupted-adoption", receipt_observer="alice",
545:     )
546:     created = provider.create_binding("alice", token="create-alice")
547:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
548:     _write_vertex(vertex, created.public_key, source.name)
549:     reviewed = vertex.read_text()
550:     signed = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
551:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
552:     ))
553:     assert signed is not None
554:     migrated = run_migration(
555:         source, vertex, store_dir=data, signer=lambda _observer, digest: signed.sign_digest(digest),
556:     )
557:     prefix = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
558:     script = r'''
559: import hashlib, json, os, sys
560: from pathlib import Path
561: import engine.arrival_adoption as engine_adoption
562: from engine.arrival_contract import Head
563: from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival
564: target, root, namespace, reviewed, head_json, stop, runtime_epoch = sys.argv[1:]
565: original_apply = engine_adoption.apply_arrival_adoption
566: def stop_at(phase):
567:     if phase == stop:
568:         os._exit(86)
569: def interrupted(registry, plan):
570:     return original_apply(registry, plan, failure_hook=stop_at)
571: engine_adoption.apply_arrival_adoption = interrupted
572: provider = MappedCredentialProvider(Path(root), namespace=namespace, receipt_observer="alice")
573: adopt_arrival(
574:     target, selected_head=Head(**json.loads(head_json)), reviewed_text=reviewed,
575:     reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
576:     declaration_text=Path(target).read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=provider,
577:     runtime_epoch=runtime_epoch,
578: )
579: raise AssertionError("durable stop phase was not reached")
580: '''
581:     run = subprocess.run(
582:         [sys.executable, "-c", script, str(vertex), str(provider.root), provider.namespace,
583:          reviewed, json.dumps({"lineage": migrated.head.lineage,
584:                                "ordinal": migrated.head.ordinal,
585:                                "record_hash": migrated.head.record_hash}), stop_phase, runtime_epoch],
586:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, check=False,
587:     )
588:     assert run.returncode == 86, run.stderr
589:     intent = vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent")
590:     assert intent.exists()
591:     reserved = json.loads(intent.read_text())["drafts"][0]
592:     before_recovery = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
593:     expected_extra = 1 if stop_phase == "after-append" else 0
594:     assert len(before_recovery.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + expected_extra
595: 
596:     # Recovery cannot depend on the original private-key location or provider.
597:     provider.root.rename(tmp_path / "credentials-offline")
598:     def refuse_credentials(*_args, **_kwargs):
599:         raise AssertionError("recovery resolved signing credentials")
600:     monkeypatch.setattr(MappedCredentialProvider, "for_write", refuse_credentials)
601:     monkeypatch.setattr(MappedCredentialProvider, "resolve", refuse_credentials)
602:     authentic_intent = intent.read_bytes()
603:     mutations = [
604:         "binding", "inner-signature", "outer-signature", "epoch-self-consistent",
605:     ]
606:     if runtime_epoch == "fresh":
607:         mutations.append("epoch-downgrade")
608:     for mutation in mutations:
609:         changed = json.loads(authentic_intent)
610:         if mutation == "binding":
611:             changed["bindings"][0]["public_key"] = "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA="
612:         elif mutation == "inner-signature":
613:             changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["signature"] = "bogus"
614:         elif mutation == "epoch-downgrade":
615:             payload = json.loads(changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["payload"])
616:             payload["protocol"] = 1
617:             payload.pop("runtime_epoch")
618:             changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["payload"] = json.dumps(
619:                 payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
620:             )
621:         elif mutation == "epoch-self-consistent":
622:             # Both the unsigned intent field and signed draft payload claim the
623:             # opposite epoch. Only independent signature verification can
624:             # reject this self-consistent mutation.
625:             opposite = "strict" if runtime_epoch == "fresh" else "fresh"
626:             changed["runtime_epoch"] = opposite
627:             payload = json.loads(changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["payload"])
628:             if opposite == "fresh":
629:                 payload["protocol"] = 2
630:                 payload["runtime_epoch"] = "fresh-after-anchor-v1"
631:             else:
632:                 payload["protocol"] = 1
633:                 payload.pop("runtime_epoch", None)
634:             changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["payload"] = json.dumps(
635:                 payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
636:             )
637:         else:
638:             changed["drafts"][0]["signature"] = "bogus"
639:         # Keep the duplicate draft representations consistent: consistency
640:         # alone must not stand in for signature and captured-key verification.
641:         changed["exact_drafts"] = json.dumps(
642:             changed["drafts"], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
643:         )
644:         intent.write_text(json.dumps(changed))
645:         with pytest.raises(SdkError):
646:             recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
647:         assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == before_recovery
648:         assert intent.exists()
649:     intent.write_bytes(authentic_intent)
650:     recovered = recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
651:     assert recovered.runtime_epoch == runtime_epoch
652:     assert recovered.head is not None
653:     assert recovered.head.ordinal == migrated.head.ordinal + 1
654:     assert recovered.captured_head == migrated.head
655:     assert recovered.commit is not None
656:     assert recovered.commit.before == migrated.head
657:     assert recovered.commit.after == recovered.head
658:     final = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
659:     assert final.startswith(prefix)
660:     assert len(final.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + 1
661:     record = json.loads(final.splitlines()[-1])
662:     assert record["body"] == reserved["body"]
663:     assert record["sig"] == reserved["signature"]
664:     assert not intent.exists()
665:     assert read_summary(vertex).basis.captured_head == recovered.head
666:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
667:         recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
668:     assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == final
669:

671: def _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path: Path):
672:     """A real signed migration for adoption's SDK boundary fault tests."""
673:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider
674: 
675:     data = tmp_path / "data"
676:     data.mkdir()
677:     source = data / "legacy.jsonl"
678:     _write_jsonl(source)
679:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
680:         tmp_path / "credentials", namespace="adoption-faults", receipt_observer="alice",
681:     )
682:     binding = provider.create_binding("alice", token="create-alice")
683:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
684:     _write_vertex(vertex, binding.public_key, source.name)
685:     reviewed = vertex.read_text()
686:     signer = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(

688:     ))

696: @pytest.mark.parametrize("broken_domain", [SigningDomain.FACT, SigningDomain.ARRIVAL])
697: def test_adoption_independently_verifies_custom_mapped_signatures(
698:     tmp_path: Path, broken_domain: SigningDomain,
699: ) -> None:
700:     from engine.credentials import ResolvedCredential
701:     from sdk import adopt_arrival
702: 
703:     provider, _binding, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
704:     before = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
705: 
706:     def resolve(request):
707:         genuine = provider.resolve(request)
708:         assert genuine is not None
709:         if request.domain is broken_domain:
710:             return ResolvedCredential(genuine.evidence, lambda _digest: "junk-signature")
711:         return genuine
712: 
713:     untrustworthy = WriteCredentials(
714:         binding_namespace=provider.namespace,
715:         binding_resolver=resolve,
716:         signature_verifier=lambda *_args: True,
717:     )
718:     with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="sign|verif"):
719:         adopt_arrival(
720:             vertex, selected_head=migration.head, reviewed_text=reviewed,
721:             reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
722:             declaration_text=vertex.read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=untrustworthy,
723:         )
724:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == before
725:     assert not vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent").exists()
726:

728: def test_adoption_verifies_actual_binding_when_observer_has_two_valid_keys(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
729:     from engine.admission import fact_commitment_hash
730:     from engine.arrival import content_commitment
731:     from engine.arrival_contract import RecordDraft
732:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival
733: 
734:     provider, original, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
735:     selected = MappedCredentialProvider(
736:         tmp_path / "second-credentials", namespace="selected-key", receipt_observer="alice",
737:     )
738:     second = selected.create_binding("alice", token="create-second-key")
739:     signer = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
740:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, CredentialPurpose.KEY_INTRODUCTION,
741:     ))
742:     assert signer is not None
743:     body = {"observer": "alice", "key": second.public_key}
744:     digest = content_commitment("key", 1002.0, "alice", "", body)
745:     registry = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
746:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
747:     assert descriptor is not None
748:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
749:     try:
750:         introduction = ledger.append(migration.head, (
751:             RecordDraft("key", 1002.0, "alice", "", body, signer.sign_digest(digest)),
752:         ))
753:     finally:
754:         query.close()
755:         ledger.close()
756:     result = adopt_arrival(
757:         vertex, selected_head=introduction.after, reviewed_text=reviewed,
758:         reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
759:         declaration_text=vertex.read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=selected,
760:     )
761:     assert result.commit is not None and result.commit.before == introduction.after
762:     record = result.commit.records[0]
763:     fact = record["body"]
764:     inner = fact_commitment_hash(
765:         fact["kind"], fact["ts"], fact["observer"], fact["origin"], fact["payload"],
766:     )
767:     outer = content_commitment(
768:         record["k"], record["at"], record["observer"], record["origin"], fact,
769:     )
770:     for domain, signature, commitment in (
771:         (SigningDomain.FACT, fact["signature"], inner),
772:         (SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, record["sig"], outer),
773:     ):
774:         assert selected.verify(domain, second.public_key, signature, commitment)
775:         assert not provider.verify(domain, original.public_key, signature, commitment)
776:     assert read_summary(vertex).basis.captured_head == result.head

779: @pytest.mark.parametrize(
780:     "maintenance", ["missing", "no-catch-up", "rebuild-only", "catch-up-and-rebuild"],
781: )
782: def test_adoption_refuses_unusable_maintenance_before_resolving_credentials(
783:     tmp_path: Path, maintenance: str,
784: ) -> None:
785:     from engine.arrival_maintenance import MaintenanceCapabilities
786:     from engine.arrival_registry import _file_binding, _open_file_backend
787:     from sdk import adopt_arrival
788: 
789:     provider, _binding, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
790:     before = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
791:     calls = []
792: 
793:     class UnsupportedMaintenance:
794:         def capabilities(self):
795:             return MaintenanceCapabilities(
796:                 catch_up=maintenance == "catch-up-and-rebuild",
797:                 rebuild=maintenance in {"rebuild-only", "catch-up-and-rebuild"},
798:             )
799: 
800:         def catch_up(self, _head):
801:             calls.append("catch-up")
802:             raise AssertionError("preflight must not mutate projections")
803: 
804:         def close(self):
805:             calls.append("closed")
806: 
807:     registry = BackendRegistry()
808:     registry.register(
809:         "file", _open_file_backend, binding_provider=_file_binding,
810:         maintenance_opener=None if maintenance == "missing" else lambda _d: UnsupportedMaintenance(),
811:     )
812: 
813:     def resolve(request):
814:         calls.append("resolve")
815:         return provider.resolve(request)
816: 
817:     credentials = WriteCredentials(
818:         binding_namespace=provider.namespace,
819:         binding_resolver=resolve,
820:         signature_verifier=provider.verify,
821:     )
822:     with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="maintenance|catch-up|rebuild"):
823:         adopt_arrival(
824:             vertex, selected_head=migration.head, reviewed_text=reviewed,
825:             reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
826:             declaration_text=vertex.read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=credentials,
827:             registry=registry,
828:         )
829:     assert "resolve" not in calls and "catch-up" not in calls
830:     if maintenance != "missing":
831:         assert "closed" in calls
832:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == before
833:     assert not vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent").exists()
834: 
835: 
836: def test_sdk_retires_proven_superseded_intent_then_adopts_fresh_selected_head(
837:     tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
838: ) -> None:
839:     import engine.arrival_adoption as engine_adoption
840:     from engine.arrival_body import body_of_fact_row
841:     from engine.arrival_contract import RecordDraft
842:     from sdk import ArrivalRefusal, adopt_arrival, recover_arrival_adoption
843: 
844:     provider, _binding, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
845:     kwargs = {
846:         "selected_head": migration.head,
847:         "reviewed_text": reviewed,
848:         "reviewed_sha256": hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
849:         "declaration_text": vertex.read_text(),
850:         "observer": "alice",
851:         "credentials": provider,
852:     }
853:     actual_apply = engine_adoption.apply_arrival_adoption
854: 
855:     def stop_after_intent(phase):
856:         if phase == "after-intent":
857:             raise OSError("reserve the original adoption before any append")
858: 
859:     with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
860:         patch.setattr(
861:             engine_adoption, "apply_arrival_adoption",
862:             lambda registry, plan: actual_apply(registry, plan, failure_hook=stop_after_intent),
863:         )
864:         with pytest.raises(ArrivalRefusal):
865:             adopt_arrival(vertex, **kwargs)
866:     intent = vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent")
867:     assert intent.exists()
868:     registry = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
869:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
870:     assert descriptor is not None
871:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
872:     try:
873:         body = body_of_fact_row((
874:             "interleaved-before-adoption", "concept", 1002.0, "alice", "", "{}", None,
875:         ))
876:         intervening = ledger.append(migration.head, (RecordDraft("fact", 1002.0, "alice", "", body),))
877:     finally:
878:         query.close()
879:         ledger.close()
880:     prefix = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
881:     with pytest.raises(ArrivalRefusal, match="superseded.*retired"):
882:         recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
883:     assert not intent.exists()
884:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == prefix
885:     kwargs["selected_head"] = intervening.after
886:     adopted = adopt_arrival(vertex, **kwargs)
887:     assert adopted.commit is not None and adopted.commit.before == intervening.after
888:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes().startswith(prefix)
889:     anchors = [
890:         row for row in read_facts(vertex, include_internal=True, limit=100).items
891:         if row["kind"] == "_decl.genesis"
892:     ]
893:     assert len(anchors) == 1
894:     assert anchors[0]["id"] == migration.lineage

=== tests/test_arrival_rehearsal_runner.py ===
1: """One isolated end-to-end exercise of the offline rehearsal command."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import hashlib
6: import json
7: import subprocess
8: import sys
9: from pathlib import Path
10: 
11: from sign import ed25519
12: 
13: RUNNER = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts" / "arrival_rehearsal.py"
14: 
15: 
16: def _inputs(
17:     tmp_path: Path, *, owned_tick: bool = False, seal: bool = False
18: ) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path, Path]:
19:     sandbox = tmp_path / "isolated"
20:     source = sandbox / "input" / "legacy.jsonl"
21:     vertex = sandbox / "work" / ".loops" / "alice.vertex"
22:     source.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
23:     vertex.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
24:     rows = [{
25:             "t": "fact", "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0", "kind": "concept",
26:             "ts": 1000.0, "observer": "alice", "origin": "legacy",
27:             "payload": '{"text":"migrated"}',
28:     }]
29:     if owned_tick:
30:         rows.append({
31:             "t": "tick", "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
32:             "name": "alice", "ts": 1001.0, "since": 1000.0,
33:             "origin": "alice", "payload": "{}", "prev_hash": None,
34:             "window_start": None, "fact_cursor": None, "window_hash": None,
35:         })
36:     source.write_text(
37:         "".join(json.dumps(row, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n" for row in rows),
38:         encoding="utf-8",
39:     )
40:     keys = vertex.parent / "keys"
41:     pair = ed25519.load_or_generate(keys)
42:     vertex.write_text(
43:         f'''name "alice"
44: store "../../input/legacy.jsonl"
45: 
46: observers {{
47:   alice {{
48:     key "{pair.public_b64}"
49:   }}
50: }}
51: 
52: loops {{
53:   {('boundary when="seal"' if owned_tick or seal else '')}
54:   concept {{ fold {{ items "collect" 100 }} }}
55:   {('seal { fold { items "collect" 10 } }' if seal else '')}
56: }}
57: ''',
58:         encoding="utf-8",
59:     )
60:     return sandbox, vertex, source, keys
61: 
62: 
63: def _command(
64:     sandbox: Path,
65:     vertex: Path,
66:     source: Path,
67:     keys: Path,
68:     output: Path,
69:     *,
70:     runtime_epoch: str = "strict",
71:     emit_kind: str = "concept",
72: ) -> list[str]:
73:     return [
74:         sys.executable, str(RUNNER), "--sandbox", str(sandbox),
75:         "--vertex", str(vertex), "--source", str(source),
76:         "--legacy-key-dir", str(keys), "--output", str(output),
77:         "--observer", "alice", "--runtime-epoch", runtime_epoch,
78:         "--emit-kind", emit_kind,
79:         "--emit-payload-json", '{"text":"rehearsal"}',
80:     ]
81: 
82: 
83: def test_rehearsal_migrates_adopts_reads_emits_and_exactly_exports(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
84:     sandbox, vertex, source, keys = _inputs(tmp_path)
85:     source_hash = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
86:     output = sandbox / "output"
87:     result = subprocess.run(
88:         _command(sandbox, vertex, source, keys, output),
89:         capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
90:     )
91:     assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
92:     evidence = json.loads((output / "evidence.json").read_text())
93:     assert evidence["status"] == "complete"
94:     assert evidence["source_sha256_before"] == source_hash
95:     assert evidence["source_unchanged"] is True
96:     assert evidence["report_verified_at_S"] is True
97:     assert evidence["migration"]["prefix_unchanged_after_emit"] is True
98:     assert evidence["migration"]["report_unchanged_after_emit"] is True
99:     assert evidence["adoption"]["fact_id_equals_lineage"] is True
100:     assert evidence["rehearsal_emit"]["signed_fact_and_arrival"] is True
101:     assert evidence["rehearsal_emit"]["physical_fact_matches_commit"] is True
102:     assert evidence["export"]["exact_stream_match"] is True
103:     assert evidence["reads_at_A"]["history_matches_inventory"] is True
104:     assert evidence["reads_at_A"]["runtime_state"] == {
105:         "runtime_epoch": {"mode": "strict", "anchor_ordinal": None},
106:         "sections_sha256": evidence["reads_at_A"]["runtime_state"]["sections_sha256"],
107:         "declared_initial": None,
108:     }
109:     assert (output / "reviewed.vertex").is_file()
110:     assert (output / "final.arrival-jsonl").is_file()
111: 
112: 
113: def test_fresh_rehearsal_seal_emits_a_signed_fact_and_tick(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
114:     sandbox, vertex, source, keys = _inputs(tmp_path, seal=True)
115:     output = sandbox / "output"
116:     result = subprocess.run(
117:         _command(
118:             sandbox,
119:             vertex,
120:             source,
121:             keys,
122:             output,
123:             runtime_epoch="fresh",
124:             emit_kind="seal",
125:         ),
126:         capture_output=True,
127:         text=True,
128:         check=False,
129:     )
130: 
131:     assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
132:     evidence = json.loads((output / "evidence.json").read_text())
133:     assert evidence["adoption"]["runtime_epoch"] == "fresh"
134:     assert evidence["reads_at_A"]["runtime_state"]["runtime_epoch"] == {
135:         "mode": "fresh",
136:         "anchor_ordinal": evidence["adoption"]["head"]["ordinal"],
137:     }
138:     assert evidence["reads_at_A"]["runtime_state"]["declared_initial"] is True
139:     target = Path(evidence["migration"]["target_path"])
140:     assert [json.loads(line)["k"] for line in target.read_text().splitlines()][-2:] == [
141:         "fact",
142:         "tick",
143:     ]
144: 
145: 
146: def test_rehearsal_refuses_paths_outside_sandbox_before_writing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
147:     sandbox, vertex, source, keys = _inputs(tmp_path)
148:     output = tmp_path / "escaped-output"
149:     result = subprocess.run(
150:         _command(sandbox, vertex, source, keys, output),
151:         capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
152:     )
153:     assert result.returncode == 2
154:     assert not output.exists()
155:     assert "refused: RehearsalRefused" in result.stderr
156: 
157: 
158: def test_owned_legacy_period_tick_remains_a_write_refusal(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
159:     """Adoption preserves the tick; it cannot license its pre-genesis role."""
160:     sandbox, vertex, source, keys = _inputs(tmp_path, owned_tick=True)
161:     output = sandbox / "output"
162:     before = source.read_bytes()
163:     result = subprocess.run(
164:         _command(sandbox, vertex, source, keys, output),
165:         capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
166:     )
167:     assert result.returncode == 2
168:     evidence = json.loads((output / "evidence.json").read_text())
169:     assert evidence["stage"] == "rehearsal-only-emit"
170:     assert evidence["report_verified_at_S"] is True
171:     assert evidence["adoption"]["head"]["ordinal"] == 3
172:     assert evidence["refusal"] == {
173:         "type": "ArrivalRefusal",
174:         "source_type": "BoundaryContinuityRefused",
175:         "captured_head": evidence["adoption"]["head"],
176:         "phase": "prepare",
177:         "effects": {"custody": {"attempt": "not-entered", "state": "not-attempted"}},
178:         "boundary_reason": "ambiguous-tick-role",
179:         "tick_id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
180:         "tick_ordinal": 2,
181:     }
182:     assert evidence["source_unchanged"] is True
183:     assert evidence["target_unchanged_after_refusal"] is True
184:     assert source.read_bytes() == before
185:     target = Path(evidence["migration"]["target_path"])
186:     kinds = [json.loads(line)["k"] for line in target.read_text().splitlines()]
187:     assert kinds == ["genesis", "fact", "tick", "fact"]

74: class AdoptionPreparationRefused(ArrivalError):
75:     """The reviewed snapshot or selected prefix cannot be adopted."""
76: 
77: 
78: class AdoptionApplyError(ArrivalError):
79:     """An adoption intent cannot be applied or reconciled."""
80: 
81: 
82: class AdoptionStale(AdoptionApplyError):
83:     """The selected head or reviewed cache changed."""
84: 
85:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, intent_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
86:         super().__init__(message)
87:         self.intent_path = intent_path
88: 
89: 
90: class AdoptionRecoveryRequired(AdoptionApplyError):
91:     """A durable preappend intent needs explicit recovery."""
92: 
93:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, intent_path: Path, cause: BaseException) -> None:
94:         super().__init__(message)
95:         self.intent_path = intent_path
96:         self.cause = cause
97: 
98: 
99: class AdoptionOutcomeUnknown(AdoptionApplyError):
100:     """An append was entered, but its outcome is not established."""
101: 
102:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, head: Head, intent_path: Path,
103:                  cause: BaseException) -> None:
104:         super().__init__(message)
105:         self.head = head
106:         self.intent_path = intent_path
107:         self.cause = cause
108: 
109: 
110: class AdoptionUnwitnessed(AdoptionApplyError):
111:     """The append committed without a complete witness."""
112: 
113:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, head: Head, commit: Commit | None,
114:                  intent_path: Path, cause: BaseException) -> None:
115:         super().__init__(message)
116:         self.head = head
117:         self.commit = commit
118:         self.intent_path = intent_path
119:         self.cause = cause
120: 
121: 
122: class AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(AdoptionApplyError):
123:     """Known custody commit still needs projection or cache reconciliation."""
124: 
125:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, phase: str, head: Head,
126:                  commit: Commit | None, intent_path: Path,
127:                  cause: BaseException) -> None:
128:         super().__init__(message)
129:         self.phase = phase
130:         self.head = head
131:         self.commit = commit
132:         self.intent_path = intent_path
133:         self.cause = cause

136: @dataclass(frozen=True)
137: class ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
138:     target_path: Path
139:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
140:     lineage: str
141:     basis: ReadBasis
142:     captured_head: Head
143:     observer: str
144:     reviewed_text: str
145:     reviewed_sha256: str
146:     declaration_text: str
147:     declaration_sha256: str
148:     documents: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]
149:     draft: RecordDraft
150:     exact_drafts: str
151:     credential_bindings: tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]
152:     runtime_epoch: Literal["strict", "fresh"] = "strict"
153: 
154:     @property
155:     def fact_ids(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
156:         return (self.lineage,)
157: 
158: 
159: @dataclass(frozen=True)
160: class ArrivalAdoptionResult:
161:     status: str
162:     target_path: Path
163:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
164:     lineage: str
165:     basis: ReadBasis
166:     captured_head: Head
167:     head: Head
168:     commit: Commit | None
169:     fact_ids: tuple[str, ...]
170:     intent_path: Path | None
171:     phase: str
172:     file_written: bool
173:     projection: ProjectionSyncResult | None
174:     credential_bindings: tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]
175:     observed_head: Head | None = None
176:     runtime_epoch: Literal["strict", "fresh"] = "strict"
177: 
178: 
179: def _anchor_payload(documents: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...], runtime_epoch: str) -> str:
180:     if runtime_epoch not in {"strict", "fresh"}:
181:         raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption runtime epoch must be strict or fresh")
182:     body: dict[str, Any] = {
183:         "protocol": 1 if runtime_epoch == "strict" else 2,
184:         "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents],
185:     }
186:     if runtime_epoch == "fresh":
187:         body["runtime_epoch"] = "fresh-after-anchor-v1"
188:     return json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
189: 
190: 
191: def arrival_adoption_intent_path(target: Path | str) -> Path:
192:     path = Path(target).resolve()
193:     return path.with_name(path.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent")
194: 
195: 
196: def _close(handle: object | None) -> None:
197:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
198:     if callable(close):
199:         with suppress(Exception):
200:             close()
201: 
202: 
203: def _cache_bytes(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan) -> bytes:
204:     return plan.declaration_text.encode("utf-8")
205: 
206: 
207: def _require_cache(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan) -> None:
208:     try:
209:         actual = plan.target_path.read_bytes()
210:     except OSError as exc:
211:         raise AdoptionStale(f"reviewed declaration cache is unavailable: {exc}") from exc
212:     if (
213:         actual != _cache_bytes(plan)
214:         or hashlib.sha256(actual).hexdigest() != plan.declaration_sha256
215:     ):
216:         raise AdoptionStale("reviewed declaration bytes changed")
217: 
218: 
219: def _selected_documents(
220:     text: str, target: Path, descriptor: StoreDescriptor
221: ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
222:     try:
223:         ast = parse_vertex(text, path=target)
224:         validate_vertex(ast)
225:         validate_arrival_runtime_identity(ast.name, ast.loops, refusal=AdoptionPreparationRefused)
226:         backend = ast.store_backend
227:         if (
228:             backend is None or backend.name != descriptor.backend
229:             or backend.lineage != descriptor.lineage
230:             or backend.role != Profile.AUTHORITY.value
231:         ):
232:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
233:                 "reviewed declaration residence differs from authority descriptor"
234:             )
235:         location = (
236:             str(canonical_store_path(ast.store, target))
237:             if descriptor.backend == "file" and ast.store is not None
238:             else ast.store_location
239:         )
240:         if location != descriptor.location:
241:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
242:                 "reviewed declaration store location differs from authority descriptor"
243:             )
244:         documents = tuple(document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(ast))
245:         seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
246:         for document in documents:
247:             identity = (document["kind"], document["subject"])
248:             if identity in seen:
249:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"duplicate declaration document {identity!r}")
250:             seen.add(identity)
251:         return documents
252:     except AdoptionPreparationRefused:
253:         raise
254:     except Exception as exc:
255:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"reviewed declaration is invalid: {exc}") from exc
256: 
257: 
258: def _scan_identity(records: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...], lineage: str) -> None:
259:     for record in records:
260:         if record["k"] not in {"fact", "batch"}:
261:             continue
262:         for kind, row in rows_of_body(record["k"], record["body"]):
263:             if kind != "fact":
264:                 continue
265:             fact_id, fact_kind, _at, _observer, _origin, payload, _signature = row
266:             if fact_id == lineage:
267:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
268:                     "physical-lineage fact ID already occurs in captured prefix"
269:                 )
270:             if not fact_kind.startswith("_decl."):
271:                 continue
272:             try:
273:                 data = json.loads(payload)
274:             except ValueError:
275:                 continue
276:             if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("lineage") == lineage:
277:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
278:                     "historical declaration overlay claims physical lineage"
279:                 )
280: 
281: 
282: def prepare_arrival_adoption(
283:     registry: BackendRegistry,
284:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
285:     *, target: Path | str, selected_head: Head,
286:     reviewed_text: str, reviewed_sha256: str, declaration_text: str,
287:     observer: str, credentials: WriteCredentials,
288:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
289:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
290:     authored_at: float | None = None,
291:     runtime_epoch: Literal["strict", "fresh"] = "strict",
292: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
293:     """Capture Full(S), verify the migration registry and sign one exact anchor."""
294:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
295:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY or not descriptor.lineage:
296:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires a lineage-pinned Authority descriptor")
297:     if not isinstance(selected_head, Head) or selected_head.lineage != descriptor.lineage:
298:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("selected head must name the descriptor lineage")
299:     if not observer or not isinstance(observer, str):
300:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adopting observer must be explicit")
301:     if not credentials.mapped or credentials.signature_verifier is None:
302:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires pre-created mapped credentials")
303:     if not callable(fact_verify) or not callable(arrival_verify):
304:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires public FACT and ARRIVAL verifiers")
305:     if runtime_epoch not in {"strict", "fresh"}:
306:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption runtime epoch must be strict or fresh")
307:     if not all(
308:         isinstance(value, str)
309:         for value in (reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256, declaration_text)
310:     ):
311:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("reviewed and published declaration text are required")
312:     reviewed = reviewed_text.encode("utf-8")
313:     if hashlib.sha256(reviewed).hexdigest() != reviewed_sha256:
314:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("reviewed snapshot SHA-256 differs from selected text")
315:     published = declaration_text.encode("utf-8")
316:     try:
317:         if target_path.read_bytes() != published:
318:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
319:                 "published declaration bytes differ from supplied authority text"
320:             )
321:     except OSError as exc:
322:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"copied declaration is unavailable: {exc}") from exc
323:     documents = _selected_documents(declaration_text, target_path, descriptor)
324:     try:
325:         reviewed_ast = parse_vertex(reviewed_text, path=target_path)
326:         validate_vertex(reviewed_ast)
327:         reviewed_documents = tuple(
328:             document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(reviewed_ast)
329:         )
330:     except Exception as exc:
331:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"reviewed source snapshot is invalid: {exc}") from exc
332:     if reviewed_documents != documents:
333:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
334:             "published authority declaration differs from reviewed source documents"
335:         )
336:     ledger = query = None
337:     try:
338:         ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
339:         captured = ledger.head()
340:         if captured != selected_head or ledger.verify(Full(through=selected_head)) != selected_head:
341:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
342:                 "selected migration head is stale or not Full-verified"
343:             )
344:         records = tuple(ledger.scan(through=selected_head))
345:         _scan_identity(records, captured.lineage)
346:         keys, _evidence = key_registry_from_records(records, arrival_verify)
347:         valid = tuple(key for key, _ordinal in keys.keys_valid_at(observer, captured.ordinal + 1))
348:         if not valid:
349:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adopting observer has no key valid at selected head")
350:         for document in documents:
351:             if document["kind"] != DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED:
352:                 continue
353:             key = document["payload"].get("key")
354:             if key is not None:
355:                 named = document["subject"]
356:                 named_valid = {
357:                     item for item, _ordinal in keys.keys_valid_at(named, captured.ordinal + 1)
358:                 }
359:                 if key not in named_valid:
360:                     raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
361:                         f"declared observer {named!r} key is not valid at selected head"
362:                     )
363:         if ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records == 0:
364:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("backend cannot append an adoption anchor")
365:     except AdoptionPreparationRefused:
366:         raise
367:     except Exception as exc:
368:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"cannot establish adoption prefix: {exc}") from exc
369:     finally:
370:         _close(query)
371:         _close(ledger)
372: 
373:     try:
374:         preflight_projection_maintenance(registry, descriptor, through=captured)
375:     except Exception as exc:
376:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
377:             f"required projection maintenance is unavailable at S: {exc}"
378:         ) from exc
379: 
380:     session = CredentialResolutionSession()
381:     context = CapturedSigningContext(captured, ((observer, valid),), ())
382:     namespace = credentials.binding_namespace
383:     assert namespace is not None
384:     def _sign(domain: SigningDomain) -> Callable[[str, str], str | None]:
385:         def sign(named: str, digest: str) -> str | None:
386:             request = CredentialRequest(namespace, named, domain, CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP)
387:             return session.sign(
388:                 credentials, request, digest, context=context,
389:                 authorized_keys=context.keys_for_author(named), required=True,
390:             )
391:         return sign
392:     at = time.time() if authored_at is None else authored_at
393:     try:
394:         draft = build_declaration_anchor_draft(
395:             lineage=captured.lineage, authored_at=at, observer=observer,
396:             documents=documents, fact_signer=_sign(SigningDomain.FACT),
397:             arrival_signer=_sign(SigningDomain.ARRIVAL),
398:             runtime_epoch=runtime_epoch,
399:         )
400:     except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
401:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"mapped adoption binding refused: {exc}") from exc
402:     except Exception as exc:
403:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"cannot sign adoption anchor: {exc}") from exc
404:     bindings = session.evidence
405:     if (
406:         len(bindings) != 2
407:         or bindings[0].request.domain is not SigningDomain.FACT
408:         or bindings[1].request.domain is not SigningDomain.ARRIVAL
409:         or bindings[0].public_key != bindings[1].public_key
410:         or bindings[0].public_key not in valid
411:     ):
412:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("mapped adoption bindings disagree")
413:     bound_key = bindings[0].public_key
414:     if not isinstance(draft.signature, str) or not isinstance(
415:         draft.body.get("signature"), str
416:     ):
417:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("draft signatures are missing")
418:     inner_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
419:         "_decl.genesis", at, observer, "", draft.body["payload"]
420:     )
421:     outer_digest = content_commitment("fact", at, observer, "", dict(draft.body))
422:     try:
423:         fact_ok = fact_verify(bound_key, draft.body["signature"], inner_digest)
424:         arrival_ok = arrival_verify(bound_key, draft.signature, outer_digest)
425:     except Exception as exc:
426:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
427:             f"independent adoption signature verification failed: {exc}"
428:         ) from exc
429:     if not fact_ok or not arrival_ok:
430:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
431:             "draft signatures do not verify under the selected mapped public key"
432:         )
433:     return ArrivalAdoptionPlan(
434:         target_path, descriptor, captured.lineage,
435:         ReadBasis(captured.lineage, captured, None, None), captured, observer,
436:         reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256, declaration_text,
437:         hashlib.sha256(published).hexdigest(), documents, draft,
438:         _exact_drafts((draft,)), bindings, runtime_epoch,
439:     )
440: 
441: 
442: def _intent_data(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan, phase: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
443:     return {
444:         "schema": _SCHEMA, "phase": phase, "target": str(plan.target_path),
445:         "descriptor": _descriptor_dict(plan.descriptor),
446:         "captured_head": _head_dict(plan.captured_head),
447:         "observer": plan.observer, "reviewed_text": plan.reviewed_text,
448:         "reviewed_sha256": plan.reviewed_sha256,
449:         "declaration_text": plan.declaration_text,
450:         "declaration_sha256": plan.declaration_sha256,
451:         "runtime_epoch": plan.runtime_epoch,
452:         "drafts": json.loads(plan.exact_drafts), "exact_drafts": plan.exact_drafts,
453:         "bindings": [
454:             {"request": {"namespace": evidence.request.namespace,
455:                          "observer": evidence.request.observer,
456:                          "domain": evidence.request.domain.value,
457:                          "purpose": evidence.request.purpose.value},
458:              "key_ref": evidence.key_ref, "algorithm": evidence.algorithm,
459:              "public_key": evidence.public_key, "provenance": evidence.provenance}
460:             for evidence in plan.credential_bindings
461:         ],
462:     }
463: 
464: 
465: def _result(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan, status: str, head: Head,
466:             commit: Commit | None, projection: ProjectionSyncResult | None,
467:             phase: str, file_written: bool,
468:             observed_head: Head | None = None) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
469:     return ArrivalAdoptionResult(
470:         status, plan.target_path, plan.descriptor, plan.lineage, plan.basis,
471:         plan.captured_head, head, commit, plan.fact_ids, None, phase,
472:         file_written, projection, plan.credential_bindings, observed_head,
473:         plan.runtime_epoch,
474:     )
475: 
476: 
477: def _finish(registry: BackendRegistry, plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
478:             intent: Path, data: dict[str, Any], head: Head,
479:             commit: Commit | None, status: str,
480:             failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None,
481:             observed_head: Head | None = None) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
482:     projection = None
483:     try:
484:         projection = sync_projection(registry, plan.descriptor, through=head)
485:         if projection.projected_after.ordinal < head.ordinal:
486:             raise AdoptionApplyError("projection did not reach adoption head")
487:         data["phase"] = "synced"
488:         _replace_json(intent, data)
489:         if failure_hook:
490:             failure_hook("after-sync")
491:         _require_cache(plan)
492:         data["phase"] = "published"
493:         _replace_json(intent, data)
494:         if failure_hook:
495:             failure_hook("after-publication")
496:         _remove_intent(intent)
497:     except Exception as exc:
498:         raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
499:             "adoption committed but projection or cache reconciliation remains",
500:             phase=data["phase"], head=head, commit=commit,
501:             intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
502:         ) from exc
503:     return _result(
504:         plan, status, head, commit, projection, "published", False, observed_head
505:     )
506: 
507: 
508: def apply_arrival_adoption(
509:     registry: BackendRegistry, plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
510:     *, failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
511: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
512:     """Reserve the signed draft, CAS against S, then sync and reconcile cache."""
513:     with _declaration_lock(plan.target_path):
514:         if _exact_drafts((plan.draft,)) != plan.exact_drafts:
515:             raise AdoptionApplyError("prepared adoption draft changed")
516:         if plan.draft.body.get("payload") != _anchor_payload(
517:             plan.documents, plan.runtime_epoch
518:         ):
519:             raise AdoptionApplyError("prepared adoption epoch differs from signed draft")
520:         _require_cache(plan)
521:         intent = arrival_adoption_intent_path(plan.target_path)
522:         data = _intent_data(plan, "prepared")
523:         try:
524:             _write_exclusive_json(intent, data)
525:         except FileExistsError as exc:
526:             raise AdoptionApplyError(f"unfinished adoption intent exists: {intent}") from exc
527:         if failure_hook:
528:             try:
529:                 failure_hook("after-intent")
530:             except Exception as exc:
531:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
532:                     "adoption intent is durable before append; recover explicitly",
533:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
534:                 ) from exc
535:         ledger = query = None
536:         try:
537:             try:
538:                 ledger, query = registry.open(plan.descriptor)
539:                 current = ledger.head()
540:             except Exception as exc:
541:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
542:                     "adoption intent is durable but custody could not be opened",
543:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
544:                 ) from exc
545:             if current != plan.captured_head:
546:                 raise AdoptionStale(
547:                     "migration head changed before adoption append; recover intent "
548:                     "to prove whether it is superseded",
549:                     intent_path=intent,
550:                 )
551:             try:
552:                 preflight_projection_maintenance(
553:                     registry, plan.descriptor, through=plan.captured_head
554:                 )
555:             except Exception as exc:
556:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
557:                     "adoption intent is durable but required projection maintenance "
558:                     "is unavailable before append",
559:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
560:                 ) from exc
561:             try:
562:                 commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, (plan.draft,))
563:             except Exception as exc:
564:                 from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
565:                 from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
566:                 if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
567:                     raise AdoptionStale(
568:                         f"adoption append refused: {exc}; recover intent to "
569:                         "establish its disposition",
570:                         intent_path=intent,
571:                     ) from exc
572:                 if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
573:                     data["phase"] = "append-unwitnessed"
574:                     with suppress(Exception):
575:                         _replace_json(intent, data)
576:                     raise AdoptionUnwitnessed(
577:                         "adoption committed without a complete witness",
578:                         head=exc.head, commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
579:                     ) from exc
580:                 data["phase"] = "append-unknown"
581:                 with suppress(Exception):
582:                     _replace_json(intent, data)
583:                 raise AdoptionOutcomeUnknown(
584:                     "adoption append outcome is unknown", head=plan.captured_head,
585:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
586:                 ) from exc
587:             data["phase"] = "appended"
588:             try:
589:                 _replace_json(intent, data)
590:                 if failure_hook:
591:                     failure_hook("after-append")
592:             except Exception as exc:
593:                 raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
594:                     "adoption committed but append phase recording failed",
595:                     phase="appended", head=commit.after, commit=commit,
596:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
597:                 ) from exc
598:         finally:
599:             _close(query)
600:             _close(ledger)
601:         return _finish(registry, plan, intent, data, commit.after, commit,
602:                        "applied", failure_hook)
603: 
604: 
605: def _intent_object(value: Any, label: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
606:     if not isinstance(value, dict):
607:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"adoption intent {label} must be an object")
608:     return value
609: 
610: 
611: def _intent_string(value: Any, label: str) -> str:
612:     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
613:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"adoption intent {label} must be a nonempty string")
614:     return value
615: 
616: 
617: def _plan_from_intent(data: Any) -> ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
618:     root = _intent_object(data, "root")
619:     if root.get("schema") != _SCHEMA:
620:         raise AdoptionApplyError("unsupported adoption intent")
621:     if root.get("phase") not in {
622:         "prepared", "append-unknown", "append-unwitnessed", "appended",
623:         "synced", "published",
624:     }:
625:         raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent phase is invalid")
626:     try:
627:         captured_data = _intent_object(root["captured_head"], "captured head")
628:         ordinal = captured_data["ordinal"]
629:         if not isinstance(ordinal, int) or isinstance(ordinal, bool) or ordinal < 0:
630:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent captured ordinal is invalid")
631:         captured = Head(
632:             _intent_string(captured_data["lineage"], "captured lineage"),
633:             ordinal,
634:             _intent_string(captured_data["record_hash"], "captured hash"),
635:         )
636:         descriptor_data = _intent_object(root["descriptor"], "descriptor")
637:         for field in ("backend", "location", "lineage", "role"):
638:             _intent_string(descriptor_data[field], f"descriptor {field}")
639:         descriptor = _descriptor_from(descriptor_data)
640:         target = Path(_intent_string(root["target"], "target")).resolve()
641:         text = _intent_string(root["declaration_text"], "authority text")
642:         digest = _intent_string(root["declaration_sha256"], "authority hash")
643:         reviewed_text = _intent_string(root["reviewed_text"], "reviewed text")
644:         reviewed_sha256 = _intent_string(root["reviewed_sha256"], "reviewed hash")
645:         observer = _intent_string(root["observer"], "observer")
646:         runtime_epoch = root.get("runtime_epoch", "strict")
647:         if runtime_epoch not in {"strict", "fresh"}:
648:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent runtime epoch is invalid")
649:         if hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != digest:
650:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent declaration bytes do not verify")
651:         if hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != reviewed_sha256:
652:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent reviewed snapshot bytes do not verify")
653:         raw_drafts = root["drafts"]
654:         if not isinstance(raw_drafts, list):
655:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent drafts must be a list")
656:         exact_drafts = _intent_string(root["exact_drafts"], "exact drafts")
657:         drafts = _drafts_from_exact(raw_drafts, exact_drafts)
658:         if len(drafts) != 1 or drafts[0].body.get("id") != captured.lineage:
659:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent has no exact lineage anchor")
660:         raw_bindings = root["bindings"]
661:         if not isinstance(raw_bindings, list):
662:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent bindings must be a list")
663:         evidence = []
664:         for index, raw in enumerate(raw_bindings):
665:             item = _intent_object(raw, f"binding {index}")
666:             request = _intent_object(item["request"], f"binding {index} request")
