667:             evidence.append(CredentialBindingEvidence(
668:                 CredentialRequest(
669:                     _intent_string(request["namespace"], "binding namespace"),
670:                     _intent_string(request["observer"], "binding observer"),
671:                     SigningDomain(_intent_string(request["domain"], "binding domain")),
672:                     CredentialPurpose(_intent_string(request["purpose"], "binding purpose")),
673:                 ),
674:                 _intent_string(item["key_ref"], "binding key ref"),
675:                 _intent_string(item["algorithm"], "binding algorithm"),
676:                 _intent_string(item["public_key"], "binding public key"),
677:                 _intent_string(item["provenance"], "binding provenance"),
678:             ))
679:         if descriptor.lineage != captured.lineage:
680:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent descriptor and captured lineage disagree")
681:         return ArrivalAdoptionPlan(
682:             target, descriptor, captured.lineage,
683:             ReadBasis(captured.lineage, captured, None, None), captured,
684:             observer, reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256,
685:             text, digest, (), drafts[0], exact_drafts, tuple(evidence),
686:             runtime_epoch,
687:         )
688:     except AdoptionApplyError:
689:         raise
690:     except Exception as exc:
691:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"malformed adoption intent: {exc}") from exc
692: 
693: 
694: def _revalidate_reserved(
695:     ledger: Any,
696:     plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
697:     *,
698:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
699:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
700: ) -> None:
701:     """Prove a public recovery intent is the same authorized adoption proposal."""
702:     try:
703:         if plan.descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY or plan.descriptor.lineage != plan.lineage:
704:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved descriptor is not this lineage's Authority")
705:         if ledger.verify(Full(through=plan.captured_head)) != plan.captured_head:
706:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved predecessor is not Full-verified")
707:         records = tuple(ledger.scan(through=plan.captured_head))
708:         _scan_identity(records, plan.lineage)
709:         registry, _evidence = key_registry_from_records(records, arrival_verify)
710:         valid = {
711:             key for key, _ordinal in registry.keys_valid_at(
712:                 plan.observer, plan.captured_head.ordinal + 1
713:             )
714:         }
715:         if not valid:
716:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved author has no key valid at S")
717:         documents = _selected_documents(
718:             plan.declaration_text, plan.target_path, plan.descriptor
719:         )
720:         reviewed_ast = parse_vertex(plan.reviewed_text, path=plan.target_path)
721:         validate_vertex(reviewed_ast)
722:         reviewed_documents = tuple(
723:             document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(reviewed_ast)
724:         )
725:         if reviewed_documents != documents:
726:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved reviewed documents differ from authority form")
727:         for document in documents:
728:             if document["kind"] != DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED:
729:                 continue
730:             key = document["payload"].get("key")
731:             if key is not None and key not in {
732:                 item for item, _ordinal in registry.keys_valid_at(
733:                     document["subject"], plan.captured_head.ordinal + 1
734:                 )
735:             }:
736:                 raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved observer key was not valid at S")
737: 
738:         bindings = plan.credential_bindings
739:         if len(bindings) != 2:
740:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT and ARRIVAL binding evidence is incomplete")
741:         by_domain = {item.request.domain: item for item in bindings}
742:         if set(by_domain) != {SigningDomain.FACT, SigningDomain.ARRIVAL}:
743:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved binding domains are invalid or duplicated")
744:         fact_binding = by_domain[SigningDomain.FACT]
745:         arrival_binding = by_domain[SigningDomain.ARRIVAL]
746:         for item in bindings:
747:             if (
748:                 item.request.observer != plan.observer
749:                 or item.request.purpose is not CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP
750:                 or not all((item.key_ref, item.algorithm, item.public_key, item.provenance))
751:                 or item.public_key not in valid
752:             ):
753:                 raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved binding is not authorized at S")
754:         if (
755:             fact_binding.request.namespace != arrival_binding.request.namespace
756:             or fact_binding.key_ref != arrival_binding.key_ref
757:             or fact_binding.public_key != arrival_binding.public_key
758:         ):
759:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT and ARRIVAL bindings disagree")
760: 
761:         draft = plan.draft
762:         payload = _anchor_payload(documents, plan.runtime_epoch)
763:         body = draft.body
764:         if (
765:             draft.kind != "fact" or draft.observer != plan.observer
766:             or draft.origin != "" or body.get("id") != plan.lineage
767:             or body.get("kind") != "_decl.genesis"
768:             or body.get("ts") != draft.authored_at
769:             or body.get("observer") != plan.observer
770:             or body.get("origin") != "" or body.get("payload") != payload
771:             or not isinstance(body.get("signature"), str)
772:             or not isinstance(draft.signature, str)
773:         ):
774:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved declaration anchor differs from reviewed documents")
775:         expected_body = body_of_fact_row((
776:             plan.lineage, "_decl.genesis", draft.authored_at,
777:             plan.observer, "", payload, body["signature"],
778:         ))
779:         if dict(body) != expected_body:
780:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved declaration anchor has unexpected fields")
781:         fact_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
782:             "_decl.genesis", draft.authored_at, plan.observer, "", payload
783:         )
784:         arrival_digest = content_commitment(
785:             "fact", draft.authored_at, plan.observer, "", dict(body)
786:         )
787:         key = fact_binding.public_key
788:         if not fact_verify(key, body["signature"], fact_digest):
789:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT signature does not verify")
790:         if not arrival_verify(key, draft.signature, arrival_digest):
791:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved ARRIVAL signature does not verify")
792:     except AdoptionApplyError:
793:         raise
794:     except Exception as exc:
795:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"cannot revalidate reserved adoption: {exc}") from exc
796: 
797: 
798: def recover_arrival_adoption(
799:     registry: BackendRegistry, intent: Path | str,
800:     *, fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
801:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
802:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
803: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
804:     """Reconcile the exact reserved row; never re-sign or append a duplicate."""
805:     intent_path = Path(intent).resolve()
806:     suffix = ".arrival-adopt.intent"
807:     if (
808:         not intent_path.name.endswith(suffix)
809:         or len(intent_path.name) <= len(suffix)
810:     ):
811:         raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent path has an invalid suffix")
812:     target_path = intent_path.with_name(intent_path.name[:-len(suffix)])
813:     if not callable(fact_verify) or not callable(arrival_verify):
814:         raise AdoptionApplyError("recovery requires public FACT and ARRIVAL verifiers")
815:     with _declaration_lock(target_path):
816:         try:
817:             data = json.loads(intent_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
818:             plan = _plan_from_intent(data)
819:         except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
820:             raise AdoptionApplyError(f"cannot read adoption intent: {exc}") from exc
821:         if plan.target_path != target_path:
822:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent path does not match its target")
823:         ledger = query = None
824:         try:
825:             try:
826:                 ledger, query = registry.open(plan.descriptor)
827:             except Exception as exc:
828:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
829:                     "reserved adoption intent is durable but custody could not be opened",
830:                     intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
831:                 ) from exc
832:             predecessor = ledger.head_at(Watermark(plan.lineage, plan.captured_head.ordinal))
833:             if predecessor != plan.captured_head:
834:                 raise AdoptionStale("adoption predecessor hash changed")
835:             _revalidate_reserved(
836:                 ledger, plan, fact_verify=fact_verify,
837:                 arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
838:             )
839:             current = ledger.head()
840:             observed_head = current
841:             if current == predecessor:
842:                 _require_cache(plan)
843:                 try:
844:                     preflight_projection_maintenance(
845:                         registry, plan.descriptor, through=predecessor
846:                     )
847:                 except Exception as exc:
848:                     raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
849:                         "reserved adoption cannot append without required projection "
850:                         "maintenance",
851:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
852:                     ) from exc
853:                 try:
854:                     commit = ledger.append(predecessor, (plan.draft,))
855:                 except Exception as exc:
856:                     from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
857:                     from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
858:                     if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
859:                         raise AdoptionStale(
860:                             f"reserved adoption CAS refused: {exc}",
861:                             intent_path=intent_path,
862:                         ) from exc
863:                     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
864:                         raise AdoptionUnwitnessed(
865:                             "reserved adoption committed without a complete witness",
866:                             head=exc.head, commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent_path,
867:                             cause=exc,
868:                         ) from exc
869:                     raise AdoptionOutcomeUnknown(
870:                         "reserved adoption append outcome is unknown", head=predecessor,
871:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
872:                     ) from exc
873:                 current = commit.after
874:                 observed_head = current
875:                 data["phase"] = "appended"
876:                 try:
877:                     _replace_json(intent_path, data)
878:                     if failure_hook:
879:                         failure_hook("after-append")
880:                 except Exception as exc:
881:                     raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
882:                         "reserved adoption committed but phase recording failed",
883:                         phase="appended", head=current, commit=commit,
884:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
885:                     ) from exc
886:             else:
887:                 if current.ordinal < predecessor.ordinal + 1:
888:                     raise AdoptionStale("reserved adoption has no successor at S+1")
889:                 adoption_head = ledger.head_at(
890:                     Watermark(plan.lineage, predecessor.ordinal + 1)
891:                 )
892:                 if ledger.verify(Full(through=adoption_head)) != adoption_head:
893:                     raise AdoptionStale("reserved adoption suffix is not Full-verified")
894:                 successor = ledger.read(predecessor.ordinal + 1)
895:                 if not _record_matches_draft(successor, plan.draft):
896:                     _remove_intent(intent_path)
897:                     raise AdoptionStale(
898:                         "a different Full-verified record occupies S+1; "
899:                         "superseded adoption intent retired"
900:                     )
901:                 commit = Commit(
902:                     before=predecessor,
903:                     records=(successor,),
904:                     after=adoption_head,
905:                     durability=DurabilityReceipt(
906:                         ledger.capabilities().durability,
907:                         "reconciled from Full-verified custody; original witness unavailable",
908:                     ),
909:                 )
910:                 current = adoption_head
911:                 data["phase"] = "appended"
912:                 try:
913:                     _replace_json(intent_path, data)
914:                 except Exception as exc:
915:                     raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
916:                         "adoption row is committed but appended phase recording failed",
917:                         phase="appended", head=current, commit=commit,
918:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
919:                     ) from exc
920:         finally:
921:             _close(query)
922:             _close(ledger)
923:         return _finish(registry, plan, intent_path, data, current, commit,
924:                        "recovered", failure_hook, observed_head)

=== libs/engine/src/engine/declaration.py ===
1: """Store-backed declaration resolver — the file dissolves to locator + ingress.
2: 
3: SPEC §9.5: a store is the canonical residence of its vertex declaration. The
4: ``.vertex`` file survives as two things and no more — a **locator** (its
5: ``store`` field says where the store lives) and an **ingress form** (the human
6: edit surface, and the pre-genesis fallback). Once a store's lineage is opened
7: (a ``_decl.genesis`` event exists), "a file that does persist is a cache of a
8: fold head, never an authority."
9: 
10: This module is that authority's read half. It reconstructs the same
11: ``VertexFile`` AST the parser returns — so every downstream consumer (compiler,
12: vertex_reader, emit checks, signing) is untouched in type terms — from the
13: store's declaration events instead of from the file text.
14: 
15: The seam is :func:`load_declaration`. The fold is
16: :func:`resolve_declaration_documents`. Both are protocol surface the loops-go
17: conformance oracle mirrors, so the rules here are explicit over clever.
18: 
19: Resolution contract (SPEC §9.2 Lineage, §9.5):
20: 
21: - **Genesis is identity, not fold state.** A store's lineage is opened exactly
22:   once; the genesis event carries the whole initial document set as its
23:   payload. Genesis is NOT resolved by Latest — its documents are the base the
24:   overlay is applied on top of. **Which genesis is self is recorded outside
25:   the fact stream**: the ``store_meta`` table's ``own_lineage`` row, stamped
26:   by ``absorb_genesis`` in the same transaction that mints the genesis. Merge
27:   copies facts, never meta — so foreign genesis rows arrive as inert citizens
28:   and can never hijack identity. Resolution selects the genesis row whose id
29:   equals the marker; a marker whose row is missing is corruption
30:   (:class:`DeclarationResolutionError`). An UNMARKED store holding any genesis
31:   rows refuses (:class:`UnadoptedLineage`) — identity is claimed only by the
32:   explicit adopt ceremony (``loops store adopt``), never inferred from facts.
33: 
34: - **Self-lineage scoping from day one.** Merge already carries arbitrary fact
35:   rows across stores today, so a foreign store's declaration events can be
36:   physically present. Every overlay/tombstone row must carry
37:   ``lineage == <this store's genesis id>`` in its payload to participate; rows
38:   with a foreign or absent lineage are **inert** — read, counted, never folded.
39: 
40: - **Fail closed on protocol.** A genesis whose ``protocol`` exceeds
41:   :data:`~lang.document.DECLARATION_PROTOCOL_VERSION` is refused entirely
42:   (:class:`UnsupportedProtocol`) — never partially interpreted.
43: 
44: - **Overlay is Latest per (kind, subject), replay-ordered.** Non-genesis
45:   ``_decl.*-defined`` rows overlay the genesis document set; the store's replay
46:   order is receipt order, ``ORDER BY rowid`` (see ``SqliteStore.since``), so the
47:   row this store received *later* wins regardless of its ``ts``.
48:   ``_decl.*-retired``/``-removed`` rows are subject tombstones. Unknown
49:   ``_decl.*`` kinds (receipts, future protocol) are skipped safely.
50: 
51: - **Same-``ts`` tie-break: an edit is in force at its own ``ts``, inclusive.**
52:   The ``as_of`` cutoff is ``_ts <= as_of`` (a declaration edit at ``ts == as_of``
53:   participates), matching ``StoreReader.facts_between``'s inclusive upper bound
54:   (``ts <= until_ts``). With the equal-cursors default (``as_of = until_ts``,
55:   SPEC §9.3), the consequence is deterministic and explicit: **when a fact and a
56:   declaration edit share an exact float ``ts``, the fact folds under the NEW
57:   ontology** — the edit wins its own instant, regardless of physical append
58:   order. The tie-break is purely ``ts``-based (not witness/rowid order), so it is
59:   reproducible across runs and across a ``rebuild(dump(S))`` that reassigns
60:   rowids. This governs the ``as_of`` **event-time** axis only.
61: 
62: - **Witness-order selection (``at=``) is implemented (0.8.0).** The finer axis
63:   SPEC §9.4 grounds fact-residence on is now live: pass a
64:   :class:`~engine.witness.WitnessPosition` as ``at=`` (mutually exclusive with
65:   ``as_of``) and the cutoff is the receipt prefix ``arrival_ordinal <= at.ordinal`` rather
66:   than ``ts <= as_of``. Selection and replay now share one axis — the prefix is
67:   chosen by arrival coordinate and replayed by arrival coordinate — so ``at``
68:   no longer has to reconcile two orderings; the ``as_of`` ts tie-break above
69:   governs that selector only. The
70:   position is A10-verified against this store before its ordinal is applied
71:   (:func:`~engine.witness.verify_position_for_store`).
72: 
73: The overlay-row *payload shape* this reader consumes —
74: ``{"lineage": <genesis id>, "subject": <str>, "payload": <document payload>}``
75: for a ``*-defined`` row and ``{"lineage": <genesis id>, "subject": <str>}`` for
76: a tombstone — is the contract the S4 edit ceremony (``SqliteStore.absorb_edit``)
77: emits; the Go conformance oracle mirrors both halves.
78: """
79: 
80: from __future__ import annotations
81: 
82: import json
83: import sqlite3
84: from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
85: from dataclasses import dataclass
86: from pathlib import Path
87: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Literal
88: 
89: from lang.document import (
90:     DECL_GENESIS,
91:     DECL_LENS_DEFINED,
92:     DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED,
93:     DECLARATION_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
94:     DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE,
95:     documents_to_vertex,
96:     is_internal_kind,
97: )
98: 
99: from .arrival_contract import DeclarationAnchor, Fact, Head, NotAuthority
100: from .residence import resolve_store_path
101: 
102: 
103: @dataclass(frozen=True)
104: class RuntimeEpoch:
105:     """Signed own-genesis choice for runtime replay, separate from custody history."""
106: 
107:     mode: Literal["strict", "fresh"]
108:     start_ordinal: int | None
109: 
110: 
111: def _runtime_epoch_payload(payload: Mapping[str, Any], ordinal: int) -> RuntimeEpoch:
112:     protocol = payload.get("protocol", 1)
113:     if type(protocol) is not int:
114:         raise UnsupportedProtocol("declaration genesis protocol must be an integer")
115:     if protocol not in (1, 2):
116:         raise UnsupportedProtocol(
117:             f"genesis protocol {protocol} is unsupported; "
118:             f"supported protocols are 1 and {DECLARATION_PROTOCOL_VERSION}"
119:         )
120:     if protocol == 2:
121:         if (
122:             set(payload) != {"protocol", "documents", "runtime_epoch"}
123:             or payload.get("runtime_epoch") != "fresh-after-anchor-v1"
124:             or not isinstance(payload.get("documents"), list)
125:         ):
126:             raise UnsupportedProtocol("protocol 2 requires the exact fresh-after-anchor-v1 payload")
127:         return RuntimeEpoch("fresh", ordinal)
128:     if "runtime_epoch" in payload:
129:         raise UnsupportedProtocol("runtime epoch marker requires declaration protocol 2")
130:     return RuntimeEpoch("strict", None)
131: 
132: 
133: def runtime_epoch_from_anchor(
134:     anchor: DeclarationAnchor,
135:     *,
136:     wire_record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
137: ) -> RuntimeEpoch:
138:     """Interpret only the own genesis; a fresh marker needs its physical row.
139: 
140:     The wire comparison prevents a modified derived index from inventing an
141:     epoch. It does not replace custody's independent head attestation.
142:     """
143:     genesis = anchor.genesis
144:     if (
145:         anchor.own_lineage is None or genesis is None
146:         or genesis.id != anchor.own_lineage or genesis.kind != DECL_GENESIS
147:     ):
148:         raise DeclarationResolutionError("runtime epoch requires an own declaration genesis")
149:     epoch = _runtime_epoch_payload(genesis.payload, genesis.arrival_ordinal)
150:     if wire_record is None:
151:         if epoch.mode == "fresh":
152:             raise DeclarationResolutionError(
153:                 "fresh runtime epoch requires its physical genesis row"
154:             )
155:         return epoch
156:     from .arrival_body import rows_of_body
157: 
158:     kind = wire_record.get("k")
159:     body = wire_record.get("body")
160:     try:
161:         rows = tuple(rows_of_body(kind, body)) if kind in {"fact", "batch"} else ()
162:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
163:         raise DeclarationResolutionError("physical declaration genesis row is malformed") from exc
164:     selected = rows[genesis.arrival_seq] if genesis.arrival_seq < len(rows) else None
165:     if (
166:         wire_record.get("lin") != anchor.own_lineage
167:         or wire_record.get("ord") != genesis.arrival_ordinal
168:         or selected != (
169:             "fact", (
170:                 genesis.id, genesis.kind, genesis.ts, genesis.observer,
171:                 genesis.origin, genesis.payload_text, genesis.signature,
172:             ),
173:         )
174:     ):
175:         raise DeclarationResolutionError(
176:             "runtime epoch projection differs from physical declaration genesis"
177:         )
178:     try:
179:         physical_payload = json.loads(genesis.payload_text)
180:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
181:         raise DeclarationResolutionError(
182:             "physical declaration genesis payload is malformed"
183:         ) from exc
184:     if physical_payload != genesis.payload:
185:         raise DeclarationResolutionError("runtime epoch projection payload differs from custody")
186:     physical_epoch = _runtime_epoch_payload(physical_payload, genesis.arrival_ordinal)
187:     if physical_epoch != epoch:
188:         raise DeclarationResolutionError("runtime epoch projection disagrees with custody")
189:     if physical_epoch.mode == "fresh" and (
190:         kind != "fact"
191:         or genesis.arrival_seq != 0
192:         or not isinstance(wire_record.get("sig"), str)
193:         or not isinstance(genesis.signature, str)
194:     ):
195:         raise DeclarationResolutionError("fresh runtime epoch genesis is unsigned")
196:     return epoch
197: 
198: if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only
199:     from lang.ast import VertexFile
200: 
201:     from engine.witness import WitnessPosition
202: 
203: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
204: # Tombstone vocabulary: which "*-defined" subject a "*-retired/-removed" row
205: # removes. The forward mapping is the frozen vocabulary home in lang.document
206: # (DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE); this is its inverse. Lens and the vertex singleton
207: # have no tombstone — a lens is replaced by re-definition, and the
208: # vertex-defined singleton IS the identity.
209: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
210: 
211: _TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED: dict[str, str] = {v: k for k, v in DEFINED_TO_TOMBSTONE.items()}
212: 
213: #: The "*-defined" declaration kinds an overlay row may (re)define — the
214: #: tombstonable subjects plus the two singletons (lens, vertex) that are
215: #: replaced by re-definition and have no tombstone.
216: _DEFINED_KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(_TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED.values()) | {
217:     DECL_LENS_DEFINED,
218:     DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED,
219: }
220: 
221: 
222: class DeclarationResolutionError(Exception):
223:     """Base for store-declaration resolution failures (fail-closed conditions)."""
224: 
225: 
226: class AmbiguousLineage(DeclarationResolutionError):
227:     """Retained alias tier — see :class:`UnadoptedLineage` (its subclass).
228: 
229:     Historically raised for "several genesis rows, no marker". The refusal is
230:     now uniform for ANY unmarked store holding genesis rows (a singleton is
231:     just as unprovable), raised as :class:`UnadoptedLineage`; existing
232:     ``except AmbiguousLineage`` handlers keep working via subclassing.
233:     """
234: 
235: 
236: class UnadoptedLineage(AmbiguousLineage):
237:     """Genesis rows exist but no ``own_lineage`` marker claims one as self.
238: 
239:     Facts alone cannot prove which genesis is self — a pre-marker own genesis
240:     and a merged-foreign one are physically identical, so ANY inference (even
241:     a singleton heuristic) is the hijack vector it tries to close. Identity
242:     is claimed only by the explicit adopt ceremony (``loops store adopt``),
243:     which stamps the marker under human intent (SPEC §9.2: genesis is
244:     identity; explicit over implicit).
245:     """
246: 
247: 
248: class UnsupportedProtocol(DeclarationResolutionError):
249:     """The genesis declares a protocol version this reader does not implement.
250: 
251:     Fail closed: a newer protocol may change document semantics, so the whole
252:     lineage is inert rather than partially interpreted (SPEC §9.2, build-plan
253:     "Protocol version in genesis").
254:     """
255: 
256: 
257: class Unhistorized:
258:     """At the requested ``as_of``, the store had not opened its lineage.
259: 
260:     Distinct from ``None``. ``None`` means "no genesis at all" (pre-genesis
261:     store — the file is authoritative). ``Unhistorized`` means "a genesis
262:     exists, but it is *later* than the ``as_of`` cutoff" — at that instant the
263:     store's ontology was not yet historized. Per SPEC §9.2 the honest answer
264:     for that era is **the genesis document set as the earliest known state**
265:     ("rendered honestly as legacy, never retro-claimed") — NOT the current
266:     file, which may have drifted since and would retro-claim history that was
267:     never recorded. The instance therefore carries ``documents`` (the genesis
268:     floor) so the caller can project the earliest known ontology while
269:     rendering the unhistorized distinction.
270:     """
271: 
272:     __slots__ = ("documents",)
273: 
274:     def __init__(self, documents: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
275:         self.documents = documents
276: 
277:     def __repr__(self) -> str:  # pragma: no cover - trivial
278:         return f"Unhistorized({len(self.documents)} docs)"
279: 
280: 
281: def _read_own_lineage(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> str | None:
282:     """Read the store-local ``own_lineage`` marker, or None if unmarked.
283: 
284:     The marker lives in ``store_meta`` — a store-local table merge never
285:     copies (merge carries facts; identity is not a fact). Stores absorbed
286:     before the marker existed simply lack the table/row.
287:     """
288:     try:
289:         row = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM store_meta WHERE key = 'own_lineage'").fetchone()
290:     except sqlite3.Error:
291:         return None
292:     return row[0] if row else None
293: 
294: 
295: def validate_arrival_declaration_anchor(anchor: DeclarationAnchor, head: Head) -> None:
296:     """Check derived declaration identity against independently captured custody.
297: 
298:     An unadopted snapshot may have no anchor. Once a projection claims its own
299:     lineage, it must supply that lineage's genesis on the same snapshot. This
300:     validates identity, not the content or authorship of declaration rows.
301:     """
302:     if anchor.own_lineage is not None:
303:         if anchor.own_lineage != head.lineage:
304:             raise NotAuthority(
305:                 "projection declaration identity does not match captured Arrival lineage"
306:             )
307:         if anchor.genesis is None:
308:             raise NotAuthority("projection declaration identity has no matching genesis")
309:     if anchor.genesis is not None:
310:         if anchor.genesis.id != head.lineage:
311:             raise NotAuthority(
312:                 "projection declaration genesis does not match captured Arrival lineage"
313:             )
314:         if anchor.genesis.kind != DECL_GENESIS:
315:             raise NotAuthority("projection declaration anchor is not a genesis fact")
316:         _runtime_epoch_payload(anchor.genesis.payload, anchor.genesis.arrival_ordinal)
317: 
318: 
319: def resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot(
320:     anchor: DeclarationAnchor, facts: list[Fact] | tuple[Fact, ...]
321: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None | Unhistorized:
322:     """Fold declaration documents from one already-bounded query snapshot.
323: 
324:     This is the pure sibling of :func:`resolve_declaration_documents`. The
325:     caller supplies internal facts through its query prefix and the exact
326:     store-local identity anchor observed on that same transaction. It therefore
327:     never opens SQLite, does not consult a vertex file, and never guesses which
328:     ``_decl.genesis`` is self from rows that may have arrived by merge.
329:     """
330:     internal = sorted(
331:         (fact for fact in facts if is_internal_kind(fact.kind)),
332:         key=lambda fact: (fact.arrival_ordinal, fact.arrival_seq),
333:     )
334:     if anchor.own_lineage is None:
335:         if any(fact.kind == DECL_GENESIS for fact in internal):
336:             raise UnadoptedLineage(
337:                 "declaration genesis rows occur in this snapshot without an "
338:                 "own_lineage identity marker; facts cannot identify self"
339:             )
340:         return None
341:     genesis = anchor.genesis
342:     if genesis is None or genesis.id != anchor.own_lineage or genesis.kind != DECL_GENESIS:
343:         raise DeclarationResolutionError(
344:             f"own_lineage marker {anchor.own_lineage!r} has no matching "
345:             "_decl.genesis row in this snapshot"
346:         )
347:     payload = genesis.payload
348:     _runtime_epoch_payload(payload, genesis.arrival_ordinal)
349:     if genesis.arrival_ordinal > max((fact.arrival_ordinal for fact in internal), default=-1):
350:         return Unhistorized(list(payload.get("documents", ())))
351: 
352:     docs: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
353:     for document in payload.get("documents", ()):
354:         docs[(document["kind"], document["subject"])] = document
355:     for fact in internal:
356:         if fact.kind == DECL_GENESIS:
357:             continue
358:         row_payload = fact.payload
359:         if row_payload.get("lineage") != anchor.own_lineage:
360:             continue
361:         if fact.kind in _TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED:
362:             docs.pop((_TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED[fact.kind], row_payload.get("subject")), None)
363:         elif fact.kind in _DEFINED_KINDS:
364:             subject = row_payload.get("subject")
365:             docs[(fact.kind, subject)] = {
366:                 "kind": fact.kind,
367:                 "subject": subject,
368:                 "payload": row_payload.get("payload", {}),
369:             }
370:     return list(docs.values())
371: 
372: 
373: class StoreBusy(DeclarationResolutionError):
374:     """The store is lock-contended within the caller's timeout budget.
375: 
376:     Raised only under ``on_locked="raise"`` — latency-bounded callers (shell
377:     completion) that must distinguish "locked, under-list" from "pre-genesis,
378:     the file is authoritative". The default path keeps the historical
379:     swallow-to-file-fallback behavior for interactive commands.
380:     """
381: 
382: 
383: def _open_readonly(store_path: Path, *, timeout: float = 5.0) -> sqlite3.Connection | None:
384:     """Open ``store_path`` read-only, or None if it is not a usable store.
385: 
386:     Read-only (URI ``mode=ro``) so declaration resolution never mutates a
387:     store and is safe alongside a concurrent writer (WAL). A path that is not a
388:     valid SQLite database (empty file, non-db bytes) yields None — the caller
389:     falls back to the file, never crashes. ``timeout`` is sqlite's busy wait;
390:     latency-bounded callers pass a sub-second value.
391:     """
392:     try:
393:         conn = sqlite3.connect(f"file:{store_path}?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=timeout)
394:     except sqlite3.Error:
395:         return None
396:     return conn
397: 
398: 
399: def resolve_declaration_documents(
400:     store_path: Path,
401:     *,
402:     as_of: float | None = None,
403:     at: WitnessPosition | None = None,
404:     timeout: float = 5.0,
405:     on_locked: str = "swallow",
406: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None | Unhistorized:
407:     """Fold the store's declaration events into a document set.
408: 
409:     Two mutually-exclusive selectors (A8):
410: 
411:     - ``as_of`` — the shipped **event-time projection** (``ts <= as_of``): an
412:       explicitly-requested analytical mode. Its same-``ts`` inclusive tie-break
413:       is unchanged (module docstring).
414:     - ``at`` — a **witness prefix** (:class:`~engine.witness.WitnessPosition`):
415:       the ``_decl`` rows this store had *received* at that position
416:       (``arrival_ordinal <= at.ordinal``), replayed in arrival order
417:       (``arrival_ordinal, arrival_seq`` ascending) — the same axis the prefix
418:       is selected on. Late arrivals do not rewrite an
419:       earlier position; this is the §9.3 cursor default. A
420:       position strictly inside an atomic receipt group is refused
421:       (:class:`~engine.witness.MidReceiptGroupPosition`) — the guard runs at this
422:       engine selector, not only in CLI address parsing.
423: 
424:     Passing both raises ``ValueError``; passing neither is a head read.
425: 
426:     Returns:
427: 
428:     - ``None`` — the store has no own ``_decl.genesis`` (pre-genesis; the file
429:       is authoritative), or is not a usable SQLite store.
430:     - :class:`Unhistorized` — an own genesis exists but is later than the cutoff
431:       (``as_of``-earlier, or ``at`` before the genesis *ordinal*); carries the
432:       genesis document set as the earliest known state.
433:     - ``list[dict]`` — the folded documents (``{"kind", "subject", "payload"}``
434:       each), ready for :func:`~lang.document.documents_to_vertex`.
435: 
436:     Fold (all inside the store, replay-ordered ``ORDER BY arrival_ordinal, arrival_seq``):
437: 
438:     1. Locate the OWN genesis: the ``store_meta.own_lineage`` marker selects it
439:        by id (foreign genesis rows are inert regardless of count); a marker
440:        whose row is missing raises :class:`DeclarationResolutionError`.
441:        Compat without a marker: zero rows → ``None``; exactly one → self;
442:        more → :class:`AmbiguousLineage`.
443:     2. If the genesis is later than the cutoff → :class:`Unhistorized` carrying
444:        the genesis documents (the SPEC §9.2 earliest-known floor). For ``at`` the
445:        comparison is by genesis *ordinal* (witness order), not ``ts`` (A13).
446:     3. Check ``protocol`` ≤ supported → else :class:`UnsupportedProtocol`.
447:     4. Seed the document dict (keyed by ``(kind, subject)``) from the genesis
448:        payload's ``documents``.
449:     5. Overlay every LATER ``_decl.*`` row that is self-lineage-scoped
450:        (``payload["lineage"] == genesis id``) and within the cutoff
451:        (``ts <= as_of`` or ``arrival_ordinal <= at.ordinal``): ``*-defined`` replaces its
452:        ``(kind, subject)`` document (Latest wins by replay order); a tombstone
453:        removes the paired ``*-defined`` subject; ``_decl.genesis`` and unknown
454:        ``_decl.*`` kinds are skipped.
455:     6. Return the surviving documents in insertion order (definition order,
456:        modulo later replacements keeping position).
457:     """
458:     if as_of is not None and at is not None:
459:         raise ValueError(
460:             "resolve_declaration_documents: as_of and at are mutually exclusive "
461:             "(A8) — a read is either event-time-projected or witness-cursor'd, "
462:             "never both"
463:         )
464:     if at is not None:
465:         # A10 lineage guard at THIS public selector too (capstone M2): this API
466:         # is exported and applies at.ordinal directly, so a position from another
467:         # store must be refused / re-resolved here, not only in vertex_fold.
468:         # Same-store returns the position unchanged (no DB hit); a same-lineage
469:         # sibling re-resolves to the target ordinal; foreign/unadopted refuses.
470:         from engine.witness import verify_position_for_store
471: 
472:         at = verify_position_for_store(at, store_path, timeout=timeout)
473:     conn = _open_readonly(store_path, timeout=timeout)
474:     if conn is None:
475:         return None
476:     try:
477:         try:
478:             genesis_rows = conn.execute(
479:                 "SELECT id, arrival_ordinal, ts, payload FROM facts WHERE kind = ? "
480:                 "ORDER BY arrival_ordinal, arrival_seq",
481:                 (DECL_GENESIS,),
482:             ).fetchall()
483:         except sqlite3.Error as e:
484:             # Lock contention is DISTINGUISHABLE state for latency-bounded
485:             # callers: swallowing it here would flow to the file fallback —
486:             # a stale-file lie when a genesis exists (completion review
487:             # round 3 #2). Everything else (no facts table, unreadable
488:             # schema) means "not a usable store" as before.
489:             if on_locked == "raise" and "locked" in str(e).lower():
490:                 raise StoreBusy(f"{store_path} is lock-contended: {e}") from e
491:             return None
492: 
493:         # Receipt-group guard (A2) — UNCONDITIONAL, before EVERY honesty-ladder
494:         # early return below. Foreign/imported `_decl` rows can form a ceremony
495:         # even with no own genesis (merge carries arbitrary rows across stores),
496:         # and a pre-genesis position also short-circuits before the fold — so a
497:         # mid-group position must be refused here regardless of genesis/lineage
498:         # state, never only on the folded path. (`resolve_witness_position` also
499:         # guards the address→position step; this is the defense for a hand-built
500:         # position handed straight to the engine seam — review finding 2.)
501:         if at is not None:
502:             from engine.witness import MidReceiptGroupPosition, receipt_group_span
503: 
504:             span = receipt_group_span(conn, at.ordinal)
505:             if span is not None:
506:                 raise MidReceiptGroupPosition(
507:                     f"witness position ordinal {at.ordinal} lands inside the atomic "
508:                     f"receipt group at ordinals {span[0]}..{span[1]} — refusing to "
509:                     "resolve a partial declaration ceremony"
510:                 )
511: 
512:         if not genesis_rows:
513:             return None
514: 
515:         marker = _read_own_lineage(conn)
516:         if marker is not None:
517:             selected = [r for r in genesis_rows if r[0] == marker]
518:             if not selected:
519:                 raise DeclarationResolutionError(
520:                     f"own_lineage marker {marker!r} in {store_path} has no "
521:                     "matching _decl.genesis row — the store's identity record "
522:                     "is corrupt (marker without its genesis)"
523:                 )
524:             genesis_id, genesis_ordinal, genesis_ts, genesis_payload_text = selected[0]
525:         else:
526:             # No marker + genesis rows present. Facts alone CANNOT distinguish
527:             # a pre-marker own genesis from a merged-foreign one — a singleton
528:             # heuristic here is exactly the hijack it exists to prevent
529:             # (closing re-review #1). Identity is claimed only by the explicit
530:             # adopt ceremony (`loops store adopt`), never inferred.
531:             raise UnadoptedLineage(
532:                 f"{len(genesis_rows)} _decl.genesis row(s) in {store_path} and "
533:                 "no own_lineage marker — this store predates the identity "
534:                 "marker (or received a foreign genesis via merge). Run "
535:                 "`loops store adopt` to explicitly claim the store's own "
536:                 "lineage; facts alone cannot prove which genesis is self"
537:             )
538: 
539:         genesis_payload = json.loads(genesis_payload_text)
540:         if _runtime_epoch_payload(genesis_payload, genesis_ordinal).mode == "fresh":
541:             raise UnsupportedProtocol(
542:                 "fresh runtime epochs require Arrival custody and are unsupported in legacy stores"
543:             )
544: 
545:         if as_of is not None and genesis_ts > as_of:
546:             return Unhistorized(list(genesis_payload.get("documents", ())))
547:         if at is not None and genesis_ordinal > at.ordinal:
548:             # Witness-mode pre-genesis: the position predates the genesis ROW
549:             # (A13 — compared by ordinal, not ts). Same genesis floor, honestly
550:             # marked unhistorized upstream.
551:             return Unhistorized(list(genesis_payload.get("documents", ())))
552: 
553:         # (The receipt-group guard ran unconditionally above, before this
554:         # honesty-ladder — review finding 2.)
555: 
556:         # Seed from the genesis document set, keyed by (kind, subject).
557:         docs: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}
558:         for d in genesis_payload.get("documents", ()):
559:             docs[(d["kind"], d["subject"])] = d
560: 
561:         # Overlay later declaration rows. This GLOB scan only runs once a
562:         # genesis is confirmed — never on a pre-genesis store (the hot path),
563:         # where step 1 already returned None.
564:         overlay_rows = conn.execute(
565:             "SELECT id, arrival_ordinal, kind, ts, payload FROM facts "
566:             "WHERE kind GLOB '_decl.*' AND kind <> ? ORDER BY arrival_ordinal, arrival_seq",
567:             (DECL_GENESIS,),
568:         ).fetchall()
569: 
570:         for _row_id, _ord, kind, _ts, payload_text in overlay_rows:
571:             if not is_internal_kind(kind):  # defensive; GLOB already scopes
572:                 continue
573:             # Inclusive cutoff (`> as_of`, not `>= as_of`): an edit AT `as_of`
574:             # is in force. With equal-cursors (`as_of == until_ts`) a fact and an
575:             # edit sharing an exact `ts` fold the fact under the NEW ontology —
576:             # the deterministic ts-axis tie-break (module docstring).
577:             if as_of is not None and _ts > as_of:
578:                 continue
579:             # Witness cutoff: the row must be WITHIN the received prefix
580:             # (arrival_ordinal <= position). Selection and replay ride the same arrival axis
581:             # (the SELECT above), so this is a suffix trim, not a second sort: a
582:             # backdated edit that arrived after the position sorts last and is
583:             # excluded by its arrival ordinal.
584:             if at is not None and _ord > at.ordinal:
585:                 continue
586:             try:
587:                 payload = json.loads(payload_text)
588:             except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
589:                 continue
590:             # Self-lineage scoping: a foreign or absent lineage is inert.
591:             if payload.get("lineage") != genesis_id:
592:                 continue
593: 
594:             if kind in _TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED:
595:                 subject = payload.get("subject")
596:                 docs.pop((_TOMBSTONE_OF_DEFINED[kind], subject), None)
597:             elif kind in _DEFINED_KINDS:
598:                 subject = payload.get("subject")
599:                 docs[(kind, subject)] = {
600:                     "kind": kind,
601:                     "subject": subject,
602:                     "payload": payload.get("payload", {}),
603:                 }
604:             # else: receipts (_decl.transit/_decl.merged) and unknown _decl.*
605:             # kinds — skipped safely (forward compat).
606: 
607:         return list(docs.values())
608:     finally:
609:         conn.close()
610: 
611: 
612: #: Provenance statuses load_declaration_status reports alongside the AST.
613: #: "store"            — folded store declaration (head or honest as-of)
614: #: "file-pre-genesis" — no lineage opened; the file is authoritative
615: #: "unhistorized"     — as_of predates genesis; AST is the GENESIS FLOOR
616: #:                      (earliest known state), not a true as-of resolution
617: #: "aggregate-head"   — storeless combine/discover: membership is CURRENT
618: #:                      FILE state regardless of as_of (aggregation internal
619: #:                      tables not yet built — honesty caveat, SPEC §9.5)
620: DECLARATION_STATUSES = ("store", "file-pre-genesis", "unhistorized", "aggregate-head")
621: 
622: 
623: def load_declaration_status(
624:     vertex_path: Path,
625:     *,
626:     as_of: float | None = None,
627:     at: WitnessPosition | None = None,
628:     store_timeout: float = 5.0,
629:     on_locked: str = "swallow",
630: ) -> tuple[Any, str]:
631:     """:func:`load_declaration` plus the provenance of what was resolved.
632: 
633:     The bare seam erases the ``Unhistorized`` distinction (every caller gets
634:     an AST); surfaces that RENDER a historical read need to say honestly which
635:     era the ontology came from — this is that channel (closing re-review #2).
636: 
637:     ``at`` (a witness position) is the mutually-exclusive sibling of ``as_of``
638:     (A8). On a store that never opened a lineage — the dominant live case — a
639:     witness read still reports ``"file-pre-genesis"`` (N3): the ontology came
640:     from the CURRENT file, not from history, and the status says so rather than
641:     silently retro-claiming.
642:     """
643:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
644: 
645:     # Enforce A8 exclusivity HERE, not only in resolve_declaration_documents:
646:     # the early-return paths below (storeless aggregate, missing store,
647:     # file-pre-genesis) never reach the resolver, so without this check a caller
648:     # passing both selectors would be silently tolerated on those paths
649:     # (review finding 4).
650:     if as_of is not None and at is not None:
651:         raise ValueError(
652:             "load_declaration_status: as_of and at are mutually exclusive (A8) "
653:             "— a read is either event-time-projected or witness-cursor'd"
654:         )
655: 
656:     historical = as_of is not None or at is not None
657:     file_ast = parse_vertex_file(vertex_path)
658:     is_aggregate = file_ast.combine is not None or file_ast.discover is not None
659: 
660:     def _finish(ast: Any, status: str) -> tuple[Any, str]:
661:         # A9 named derogation (capstone M7): a combine/discover vertex's
662:         # membership/existence set is ALWAYS current — aggregation internal
663:         # tables are not built yet (SPEC §9.5) — so a historical read over an
664:         # aggregate is disclosed as ``aggregate-head`` even when the vertex ALSO
665:         # carries a self-knowledge store (whose ontology may resolve at the
666:         # cutoff). Without this override, a combine-with-store reported ``store``
667:         # and the membership derogation was silently lost.
668:         if historical and is_aggregate:
669:             return ast, "aggregate-head"
670:         return ast, status
671: 
672:     store_field = file_ast.store
673:     # The backend arm is residence, like the locator beside it: the documents
674:     # do not carry it, so the file is the only place it can come from. A
675:     # re-projection that supplied the locator but not the backend would hand
676:     # back a vertex whose adapter designation had silently reverted to suffix
677:     # inference — and nothing downstream would report that.
678:     backend_field = file_ast.store_backend
679:     location_field = file_ast.store_location
680:     if store_field is None:
681:         return _finish(file_ast, "file-pre-genesis")
682: 
683:     store_path = resolve_store_path(store_field, vertex_path)
684:     if not store_path.exists():
685:         return _finish(file_ast, "file-pre-genesis")
686: 
687:     docs = resolve_declaration_documents(
688:         store_path,
689:         as_of=as_of,
690:         at=at,
691:         timeout=store_timeout,
692:         on_locked=on_locked,
693:     )
694:     if isinstance(docs, list):
695:         resolved = documents_to_vertex(
696:             docs,
697:             path=vertex_path,
698:             store=store_field,
699:             store_backend=backend_field,
700:             store_location=location_field,
701:         )
702:         return _finish(_reattach_ingress(resolved, file_ast), "store")
703:     if isinstance(docs, Unhistorized):
704:         resolved = documents_to_vertex(
705:             docs.documents,
706:             path=vertex_path,
707:             store=store_field,
708:             store_backend=backend_field,
709:             store_location=location_field,
710:         )
711:         return _finish(_reattach_ingress(resolved, file_ast), "unhistorized")
712:     return _finish(file_ast, "file-pre-genesis")
713: 
714: 
715: def declaration_generation(
716:     vertex_path: Path,
717:     *,
718:     as_of: float | None = None,
719:     at: WitnessPosition | None = None,
720:     store_timeout: float = 5.0,
721: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
722:     """Disclose which GENERATION of declarations interpreted a read (0.9.0 S4).
723: 
724:     A REVIEW comparator — it answers ONE question: "did the ontology change
725:     between two reads?" It is explicitly **NOT** SPEC §10.2 JCS canonical bytes
726:     and carries **no interchange claim**; the fingerprint below is a
727:     review-only content hash, never a portable encoding.
728: 
729:     Honest across BOTH declaration residences — the adopted store and the
730:     pre-genesis file — because it fingerprints the RESOLVED declaration AST that
731:     :func:`load_declaration_status` returns (the same ``VertexFile`` every read
732:     actually folds under), projected through :func:`lang.vertex_to_documents`.
733:     That projection is the residence-excluded canonical decomposition: the
734:     ``store`` locator and the file ``path`` are dropped, so the fingerprint
735:     tracks the ONTOLOGY, not where it currently lives. Using one projection
736:     function for both residences (rather than hashing stored ``_decl`` documents
737:     on adopted stores but file bytes pre-genesis) means the two eras hash the
738:     same space and are directly comparable.
739: 
740:     Returns a dict with four keys:
741: 
742:     - ``status`` — the :func:`load_declaration_status` label
743:       (``store`` / ``file-pre-genesis`` / ``unhistorized`` / ``aggregate-head``).
744:     - ``lineage`` — the store's own genesis id, or ``None`` (pre-genesis /
745:       unadopted / aggregate / store-less).
746:     - ``review_fingerprint`` — ``"sha256:<hex>"`` over
747:       ``json.dumps([d.as_json() for d in vertex_to_documents(ast)],
748:       sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))``. Stable across repeated reads of
749:       an unedited declaration; changes when the declaration changes. Because it
750:       hashes the PARSED projection (not raw file bytes), a cosmetic ``.vertex``
751:       edit the parser discards — whitespace, comments, key reordering — does not
752:       churn it, while a semantic edit does. ``None`` only if the projection
753:       itself fails (a malformed declaration that still parsed to an AST).
754:     - ``decl_head`` — the ULID of the latest-applied self-lineage ``_decl.*``
755:       event within the cutoff on an ADOPTED store (rebuild-stable, unlike a
756:       rowid); ``None`` on pre-genesis (no ``_decl`` lineage), aggregate, and
757:       store-less vertices. On an adopted store with only a genesis it is the
758:       genesis id itself — the head of a one-event declaration lineage.
759:     """
760:     import hashlib
761: 
762:     from lang.document import vertex_to_documents
763: 
764:     ast, status = load_declaration_status(
765:         vertex_path,
766:         as_of=as_of,
767:         at=at,
768:         store_timeout=store_timeout,
769:     )
770:     try:
771:         docs = vertex_to_documents(ast)
772:         canonical = json.dumps(
773:             [d.as_json() for d in docs],
774:             sort_keys=True,
775:             separators=(",", ":"),
776:         )
777:         fingerprint: str | None = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
778:     except Exception:
779:         # A resolved-but-unprojectable declaration must not abort a review —
780:         # degrade the ONE field honestly rather than crash the read.
781:         fingerprint = None
782: 
783:     lineage, decl_head = _decl_lineage_and_head(
784:         vertex_path,
785:         as_of=as_of,
786:         at=at,
787:         timeout=store_timeout,
788:     )
789:     return {
790:         "status": status,
791:         "lineage": lineage,
792:         "review_fingerprint": fingerprint,
793:         "decl_head": decl_head,
794:     }
795: 
796: 
797: def _decl_lineage_and_head(
798:     vertex_path: Path,
799:     *,
800:     as_of: float | None,
801:     at: WitnessPosition | None,
802:     timeout: float,
803: ) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
804:     """``(lineage, decl_head)`` for the adopted-store residence, else ``(None,
805:     None)``.
806: 
807:     ``lineage`` is the store's ``own_lineage`` marker (the genesis id).
808:     ``decl_head`` is the ULID of the newest-by-receipt (``rowid``) self-lineage
809:     ``_decl.*`` row within the cutoff — the last declaration event that would
810:     win under replay. Pre-genesis / unadopted / aggregate / store-less all
811:     resolve to ``(None, None)`` — there is no declaration lineage to head.
812:     """
813:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
814: 
815:     file_ast = parse_vertex_file(vertex_path)
816:     if file_ast.combine is not None or file_ast.discover is not None:
817:         return None, None  # aggregate — no single store lineage
818:     store_field = file_ast.store
819:     if store_field is None:
820:         return None, None
821:     store_path = resolve_store_path(store_field, vertex_path)
822:     if not store_path.exists():
823:         return None, None
824:     conn = _open_readonly(store_path, timeout=timeout)
825:     if conn is None:
826:         return None, None
827:     try:
828:         return decl_lineage_and_head_on(conn, as_of=as_of, at=at)
829:     finally:
830:         conn.close()
831: 
832: 
833: def decl_lineage_and_head_on(
834:     conn: sqlite3.Connection,
835:     *,
836:     as_of: float | None = None,
837:     at: WitnessPosition | None = None,
838: ) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
839:     """``(lineage, decl_head)`` read on a CALLER-OWNED connection.
840: 
841:     Split out of :func:`_decl_lineage_and_head` so a caller holding its own
842:     read snapshot can observe the declaration head WITHIN that snapshot —
843:     the FTS coverage probe needs the head it certifies against to come from
844:     the same snapshot as the index state it reads (sol P2-a). The caller owns
845:     the connection's lifetime; this never closes it.
846:     """
847:     try:
848:         lineage = _read_own_lineage(conn)
849:         if lineage is None:
850:             return None, None  # pre-genesis / unadopted — no self lineage
851:         cutoff = ""
852:         params: list[Any] = []
853:         if at is not None:
854:             cutoff = " AND arrival_ordinal <= ?"
855:             params.append(at.ordinal)
856:         elif as_of is not None:
857:             cutoff = " AND ts <= ?"
858:             params.append(as_of)
859:         # Newest-first by replay order (arrival order, arrival_ordinal DESC, arrival_seq DESC — the
860:         # ``at`` ordinal cutoff and the ``as_of`` ts cutoff stay independent
861:         # selectors); the first row that is self-lineage
862:         # (the genesis itself, id == lineage, or an overlay whose payload
863:         # lineage matches) is the declaration head. Foreign _decl rows sort
864:         # in but are skipped — they never participate in this store's fold.
865:         try:
866:             rows = conn.execute(
867:                 "SELECT id, payload FROM facts WHERE kind GLOB '_decl.*'"
868:                 + cutoff
869:                 + " ORDER BY arrival_ordinal DESC, arrival_seq DESC",
870:                 params,
871:             ).fetchall()
872:         except sqlite3.Error:
873:             return lineage, None
874:         for fid, payload_text in rows:
875:             if fid == lineage:
876:                 return lineage, fid
877:             try:
878:                 if json.loads(payload_text).get("lineage") == lineage:
879:                     return lineage, fid
880:             except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, AttributeError):
881:                 continue
882:         return lineage, None
883:     except sqlite3.Error:
884:         return None, None
885: 
886: 
887: def load_declaration(
888:     vertex_path: Path,
889:     *,
890:     as_of: float | None = None,
891:     at: WitnessPosition | None = None,
892:     store_timeout: float = 5.0,
893:     on_locked: str = "swallow",
894: ):
895:     """Resolve a vertex's declaration — THE seam (SPEC §9.5).
896: 
897:     This is the single function every declaration-consulting site routes
898:     through instead of ``parse_vertex_file``. It returns the same
899:     ``VertexFile`` AST the parser returns, so callers are unchanged.
900: 
901:     The file is parsed for two irreducible things: the **locator** (its
902:     ``store`` field, which says where the store lives — this legitimately lives
903:     in the file, §9.5) and the **pre-genesis fallback** (the file AST itself,
904:     authoritative before a lineage is opened).
905: 
906:     - If the store exists and has opened its lineage → the store's folded
907:       declaration, projected back to a ``VertexFile`` with the file's locator
908:       re-attached.
909:     - If ``as_of`` predates the genesis → the **genesis document set** projected
910:       (SPEC §9.2: "an ontology-as-of read before the genesis reports
911:       unhistorized, earliest known state = the genesis document"). NOT the
912:       current file — the file may have drifted since absorption, and returning
913:       it would retro-claim history that was never recorded.
914:     - Otherwise (no store, store absent, no genesis) → the file AST unchanged,
915:       pre-genesis behavior: before the lineage opened, the file is the only
916:       surviving record of that era and IS authoritative.
917: 
918:     Rendering surfaces that must SAY which era the ontology came from use
919:     :func:`load_declaration_status` instead — this bare seam returns the same
920:     AST but erases the provenance.
921:     """
922:     ast, _status = load_declaration_status(
923:         vertex_path,
924:         as_of=as_of,
925:         at=at,
926:         store_timeout=store_timeout,
927:         on_locked=on_locked,
928:     )
929:     return ast
930: 
931: 
932: class SourceDrift(DeclarationResolutionError):
933:     """A pinned source file's content no longer matches its declaration pin.
934: 
935:     The ``source-defined`` event hash-pinned the referenced ``.loop`` (or
936:     template params file) at absorb time. Running with drifted content would
937:     ENACT undeclared behavior — the ontology would say one thing and the
938:     runtime do another (the §9.1 lie at the execution tier). Surfaced, never
939:     auto-enacted: re-absorb to accept the drift into the lineage.
940:     """
941: 
942: 
943: def validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
944:     vertex_name: str,
945:     loop_names: Iterable[str],
946:     *,
947:     refusal: Callable[[str], BaseException],
948: ) -> None:
949:     """Refuse loop identities ambiguous under the current Arrival tick profile.
950: 
951:     Arrival runtime interpretation reserves the vertex name from every local
952:     loop name. ``cite`` is included because materialization installs that
953:     implicit loop even when it is absent from the declaration. Callers supply
954:     their operation-specific pre-mutation refusal family; evidence-only
955:     declaration reconstruction deliberately does not invoke this validator.
956:     """
957:     if vertex_name == "cite" or vertex_name in loop_names:
958:         raise refusal(
959:             f"Arrival runtime reserves vertex name {vertex_name!r} "
960:             "from the loop-name namespace"
961:         )
962: 
963: 
964: def verify_source_pins_from_documents(
965:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]], base_dir: Path
966: ) -> None:
967:     """Verify source file pins using already-bounded declaration documents.
968: 
969:     This is the pure Arrival runtime boundary: callers supply the declaration
970:     documents captured from their attested snapshot and the locator's ingress
971:     directory. It never opens a store or resolves declaration state again.
972:     """
973:     import hashlib
974: 
975:     from lang.document import DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED
976: 
977:     def _check(ref: str, pinned: str, what: str) -> None:
978:         path = Path(ref)
979:         candidate = path if path.is_absolute() else (base_dir / path)
980:         try:
981:             current = hashlib.sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
982:         except OSError:
983:             raise SourceDrift(
984:                 f"{what} {ref} is pinned in the declaration but unreadable — "
985:                 "restore it or re-absorb"
986:             ) from None
987:         if current != pinned:
988:             raise SourceDrift(
989:                 f"{what} {ref} has drifted from its declaration pin — the "
990:                 "store's ontology no longer describes this file; run "
991:                 "`loops store absorb` to declare the change (never "
992:                 "auto-enacted)"
993:             )
994: 
995:     for document in documents:
996:         if document.get("kind") != DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED:
997:             continue
998:         payload = document.get("payload", {})
999:         pinned = payload.get("content_sha256")
1000:         if pinned:
1001:             ref = payload.get("template") or payload.get("path")
1002:             if ref:
1003:                 _check(ref, pinned, "source")
1004:         from_document = payload.get("from") or {}
1005:         params_pin = from_document.get("params_sha256")
1006:         if params_pin and from_document.get("path"):
1007:             _check(from_document["path"], params_pin, "params file")
1008: 
1009: 
1010: def effective_declaration_from_documents(
1011:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
1012:     locator: VertexFile,
1013:     *,
1014:     verify_pins: bool = True,
1015: ) -> VertexFile:
1016:     """Rebuild effective declaration state using only bounded documents.
1017: 
1018:     Residence and source environment ingress come from the already-parsed
1019:     locator. Source structure remains document-authoritative, and optional pin
1020:     verification reads source files only after the bounded declaration has
1021:     selected their expected hashes. No projection or declaration store opens.
1022:     """
1023:     import copy
1024: 
1025:     resolved = documents_to_vertex(
1026:         copy.deepcopy(list(documents)),
1027:         path=locator.path,
1028:         store=locator.store,
1029:         store_backend=locator.store_backend,
1030:         store_location=locator.store_location,
1031:     )
1032:     effective = _reattach_ingress(resolved, locator)
1033:     if verify_pins:
1034:         base_dir = locator.path.parent if locator.path is not None else Path.cwd()
1035:         verify_source_pins_from_documents(documents, base_dir)
1036:     return effective
1037: 
1038: 
1039: def verify_source_pins(vertex_path: Path) -> None:
1040:     """Refuse to run over drifted pinned sources (SPEC §9.2, no-auto-enact).
1041: 
1042:     For every ``source-defined`` document carrying a ``content_sha256`` (or a
1043:     ``from.params_sha256``), hash the referenced file's current bytes and
1044:     raise :class:`SourceDrift` on mismatch. Pre-genesis stores (no documents)
1045:     and unpinned rows (legacy genesis, missing files at absorb) are no-ops —
1046:     the pin gate only guards claims the declaration actually makes.
1047:     """
1048:     from lang import parse_vertex_file
1049: 
1050:     file_ast = parse_vertex_file(vertex_path)
1051:     store_field = file_ast.store
1052:     if store_field is None:
1053:         return
1054:     store_path = resolve_store_path(store_field, vertex_path)
1055:     if not store_path.exists():
1056:         return
1057:     docs = resolve_declaration_documents(store_path)
1058:     if not isinstance(docs, list):
1059:         return
1060: 
1061:     verify_source_pins_from_documents(docs, vertex_path.parent)
1062: 
1063: 
1064: def _reattach_ingress(resolved, file_ast):
1065:     """Re-attach ingress-class values from the file onto the resolved AST.
1066: 
1067:     Env VALUES are ingress (SPEC §9.5 secrets-indirection): declaration
1068:     payloads record only the key set, so projection yields values as "".
1069:     Like the ``store`` locator, the live values come from the file — the one
1070:     residence the declaration deliberately does not carry. The key SET stays
1071:     store-authoritative: a key present in the file but absent from the
1072:     resolved declaration is NOT added (that's an unabsorbed edit, surfaced at
1073:     ``absorb -n``); a resolved key missing from the file resolves to "" and
1074:     fails loudly at runtime, honestly reflecting the absent ingress.
1075:     """
1076:     if not resolved.sources_blocks or not file_ast.sources_blocks:
1077:         return resolved
1078: 
1079:     # File-side value lookup keyed by OCCURRENCE identity — (block index,
1080:     # command, occurrence-ordinal) — the same identity the document layer's
1081:     # collision-suffixed subjects encode. Keying by command alone cross-wired
1082:     # values between two sources sharing a command (re-review #4: a real
1083:     # secret cross-wire). Fallback to (command, ordinal) so a block reshuffle
1084:     # doesn't orphan values.
1085:     #
1086:     # Ordinal identity is only sound while the file's occurrence COUNT for a
1087:     # (block, command) group matches the resolved declaration's. A structural
1088:     # edit not yet absorbed (delete/insert one of two duplicate-command
1089:     # sources) re-numbers the survivors and would misroute values across
1090:     # sources (branch-review #2) — for such diverged groups re-attachment is
1091:     # SKIPPED entirely: values resolve empty and fail loudly at runtime, and
1092:     # `absorb -n` surfaces the divergence to reconcile. Within a count-stable
1093:     # group, position IS the identity ("order is meaning").
1094:     by_block: dict[tuple[int, str, int], dict[str, str]] = {}
1095:     file_seen: dict[tuple[int, str], int] = {}
1096:     for bi, block in enumerate(file_ast.sources_blocks):
1097:         for src in block.sources:
1098:             nth = file_seen.get((bi, src.command), 0)
1099:             file_seen[(bi, src.command)] = nth + 1
1100:             by_block[(bi, src.command, nth)] = dict(src.env)
1101: 
1102:     resolved_counts: dict[tuple[int, str], int] = {}
1103:     for bi, block in enumerate(resolved.sources_blocks):
1104:         for src in block.sources:
1105:             resolved_counts[(bi, src.command)] = resolved_counts.get((bi, src.command), 0) + 1
1106: 
1107:     from lang.ast import InlineSource, SourcesBlock
1108: 
1109:     changed = False
1110:     new_blocks = []
1111:     resolved_seen: dict[tuple[int, str], int] = {}
1112:     for bi, block in enumerate(resolved.sources_blocks):
1113:         new_sources = []
1114:         for src in block.sources:
1115:             nth = resolved_seen.get((bi, src.command), 0)
1116:             resolved_seen[(bi, src.command)] = nth + 1
1117:             if src.env and any(v == "" for _, v in src.env):
1118:                 # Re-attach ONLY within a count-stable (block, command)
1119:                 # group — the sole scope where ordinal identity is provable.
1120:                 # No cross-block fallback: a moved source resolves empty
1121:                 # until re-absorbed (a fallback with block-local ordinals
1122:                 # cross-wired secrets across blocks — branch-review round 2).
1123:                 block_stable = resolved_counts.get((bi, src.command)) == file_seen.get(
1124:                     (bi, src.command)
1125:                 )
1126:                 env_map = by_block.get((bi, src.command, nth), {}) if block_stable else {}
1127:                 env = tuple((k, v or env_map.get(k, "")) for k, v in src.env)
1128:                 if env != src.env:
1129:                     changed = True
1130:                     src = InlineSource(
1131:                         command=src.command,
1132:                         kind=src.kind,
1133:                         observer=src.observer,
1134:                         every=src.every,
1135:                         on=src.on,
1136:                         format=src.format,
1137:                         timeout=src.timeout,
1138:                         origin=src.origin,
1139:                         env=env,
1140:                         parse=src.parse,
1141:                         path=src.path,
1142:                     )
1143:             new_sources.append(src)
1144:         new_blocks.append(SourcesBlock(mode=block.mode, sources=tuple(new_sources)))
1145: 
1146:     if not changed:
1147:         return resolved
1148:     from lang.ast import VertexFile
1149: 
1150:     # ast.py's custom frozen decorator defeats dataclasses.replace — rebuild
1151:     # through the constructor with the full explicit field list.
1152:     return VertexFile(
1153:         name=resolved.name,
1154:         loops=resolved.loops,
1155:         store=resolved.store,
1156:         store_backend=resolved.store_backend,
1157:         store_location=resolved.store_location,
1158:         discover=resolved.discover,
1159:         sources=resolved.sources,
1160:         vertices=resolved.vertices,
1161:         routes=resolved.routes,
1162:         emit=resolved.emit,
1163:         combine=resolved.combine,
1164:         sources_blocks=tuple(new_blocks),
1165:         observers=resolved.observers,
1166:         lens=resolved.lens,
1167:         boundary=resolved.boundary,
1168:         observer_scoped=resolved.observer_scoped,
1169:         strict=resolved.strict,
1170:         path=resolved.path,
1171:     )

=== libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_store.py ===
1: """arrival_store — the write path for a store whose custody is its arrival log.
2: 
3: Cut A of the arrival substrate (decision:design/arrival-sliceA-authority):
4: a ``store`` locator ending in ``.arrival`` names an arrival log as the
5: canonical artifact, and the sqlite file at the sibling ``.db`` is a derived,
6: rebuildable index over it. Reads execute through the index in every mode
7: (arrival law 1); this class owns where a row lands FIRST.
8: 
9: Seam choice mirrors the log-canonical store one-for-one, and for the same
10: reason: ``SqliteStore.append``/``append_tick`` do the mint work and then
11: persist one fully-assembled row through the ``_write_fact_row`` /
12: ``_write_tick_row`` / ``_ceremony_persist`` seam. Overriding the seam sees
13: the committed row for free and inherits every read method unchanged.
14: 
15: How a row becomes a record
16: --------------------------
17: The arrival record's ``body`` is :mod:`engine.arrival_body`'s object for the
18: committed row — ``payload`` rides as the stored TEXT string inside it, so
19: every existing fact signature and commitment hash survives the round trip,
20: and the index rebuilds by handing ``body`` back to the same reader. No
21: second encoding exists to disagree with. The record's ``k`` is the row
22: class (``fact`` / ``tick`` / ``batch``), never the fact's own kind — a fact
23: kind is data and rides inside ``body``, where it cannot collide with the
24: grammar's structural kinds (genesis, key introductions). Since wire v1
25: dropped ``body.t``, ``k`` is also the ONLY place the row class is written.
26: 
27: ``origin``/``at`` mirror the row's own columns. ``observer`` splits by kind,
28: per decision:design/arrival-wire-v1-seam-triage: a FACT's names its author,
29: because that value selects the key that signs the record; a TICK's names
30: this log's custodian — the destination log's genesis observer — because a
31: tick has no author and its name is not an authorship claim. See
32: :meth:`ArrivalStore._custodian`.
33: 
34: Fact records are signed by the store's injected ``fact_signer`` over the
35: ARRIVAL content commitment — record-level attestation, resolvable by
36: :func:`engine.arrival.verify_authorship` from log coordinates alone. The
37: row's own signature (over the fact commitment) rides inside ``body`` as
38: data for the untouched legacy verify path; the two claims are different
39: subjects and both survive.
40: 
41: Write order per append: reconcile, stage the sqlite INSERT (uncommitted),
42: read the committed row back, append the record to the arrival log (durable,
43: fsync'd, under the log's own lock), stamp the resume mark, commit. A
44: failure after the log fsync rolls the index back and leaves the record
45: durable and unindexed — the recoverable state catch-up consumes.
46: 
47: Catch-up: consume, refuse — never rebuild
48: ------------------------------------------
49: The index's cursor is the ratified three-field resume mark
50: (``arrival_lineage`` / ``arrival_offset`` / ``arrival_ordinal`` in
51: ``store_meta``), validated at adoption by :meth:`engine.arrival.ArrivalLog.
52: walk_marked` — the anchor is re-checked at the site that trusts it, never
53: assumed. A fresh, empty index builds forward from ordinal 0 (building an
54: absent projection destroys nothing). Everything else that cannot be
55: consumed forward REFUSES with :class:`ArrivalCanonicalUnsupported`:
56: discarding rows and re-deriving the index is projection re-derivation, the
57: next cut's verb, and giving this class a repair verb now would pre-empt
58: that design (the same reasoning that keeps :meth:`adopt_lineage` a
59: refusal).
60: 
61: Detection scope, stated narrowly: the mark is offset-and-ordinal parity
62: plus the anchor checks. An out-of-band sqlite INSERT that leaves the mark
63: intact opens clean here — the arrival-side agreement audit is a later
64: cut's, alongside re-derivation. The log itself stays the authority either
65: way: everything the gate asks answers from the log alone.
66: """
67: 
68: from __future__ import annotations
69: 
70: import json
71: import sqlite3
72: from pathlib import Path
73: from typing import Any, Generic, Iterator, TypeVar
74: 
75: from .arrival import (
76:     ArrivalLog,
77:     GenesisRefused,
78:     ResumeMark,
79: )
80: from .arrival_body import (
81:     body_of_batch,
82:     body_of_fact_row,
83:     body_of_tick_row,
84: )
85: from .arrival_contract import Head
86: from .arrival_projection import has_rows, licensed_own_lineage, rows_of_record
87: from .file_projection_schema import (
88:     ARRIVAL_LINEAGE_KEY,
89:     ARRIVAL_OFFSET_KEY,
90:     ARRIVAL_ORDINAL_KEY,
91:     ArrivalCanonicalUnsupported,
92:     FACT_ALL_COLUMNS,
93:     FACT_COLUMN_INDEX,
94:     FACT_COLUMNS,
95:     FACT_INSERT_SQL,
96:     TICK_COLUMN_INDEX,
97:     TICK_INSERT_SQL,
98: )
99: from .jsonl_store import _as_int, _stamped_offset_current
100: from .residence import canonical_for, index_path_for
101: from .sqlite_store import SqliteStore
102: 
103: __all__ = [
104:     "ARRIVAL_LINEAGE_KEY",
105:     "ARRIVAL_OFFSET_KEY",
106:     "ARRIVAL_ORDINAL_KEY",
107:     "ArrivalCanonicalUnsupported",
108:     "ArrivalStore",
109:     "ensure_arrival_index",
110: ]
111: 
112: T = TypeVar("T")
113: 
114: 
115: class ArrivalStore(SqliteStore[T], Generic[T]):
116:     """A store whose canonical persistence is its arrival log.
117: 
118:     Same constructor as :class:`SqliteStore` plus an optional ``log_path``
119:     (defaults to the db path with the ``.arrival`` suffix). Opening runs
120:     catch-up; every append lands in the log first.
121: 
122:     The log must be minted before the store accepts writes — movement 1
123:     (genesis, with the founding key) is its custodian's act, performed with
124:     key material this class never sees. An unminted log opens as an empty
125:     store and refuses appends with the mint-first message.
126:     """
127: 
128:     def __init__(self, *, log_path: Path | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
129:         super().__init__(**kwargs)
130:         self._log = ArrivalLog(
131:             Path(log_path)
132:             if log_path is not None
133:             else canonical_for(self._path, "arrival")
134:         )
135:         self._coordinate_mode = "arrival"
136: 
137:         def _provider() -> Iterator[tuple[str, str, int, int]]:
138:             mark = self._read_mark()
139:             if mark is None:
140:                 return
141:             for record in self._log.walk():
142:                 if record["ord"] > mark.arrival_ordinal:
143:                     break
144:                 ord_val = record["ord"]
